// Task #770 — Download a CSV of alert events for an arbitrary range so
// recipients can re-pull a past quiet-hours digest export without
// hunting through their inbox. Columns mirror `buildDigestCsv` exactly.
router.get(
  "/admin/metrics/jobs/alerts/events.csv",
  requireAuth,
  requireRole("SUPER_ADMIN"),
  async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    try {
      const result = await jobMetricAlerts.buildAlertEventsCsvResponse({
        query: {
          from: typeof req.query.from === "string" ? req.query.from : undefined,
          to: typeof req.query.to === "string" ? req.query.to : undefined,
          range:
            typeof req.query.range === "string" ? req.query.range : undefined,
          job: typeof req.query.job === "string" ? req.query.job : undefined,
          counter:
            typeof req.query.counter === "string"
              ? req.query.counter
              : undefined,
        },
      });
      if (!result.ok) {
        const body: Record<string, unknown> = { error: result.error };
        if (result.details !== undefined) body.details = result.details;
        res.status(result.status).json(body);
        return;
      }
      res.setHeader("Content-Type", result.contentType);
      res.setHeader(
        "Content-Disposition",
        `attachment; filename="${result.filename}"`,
      );
      res.send(result.csv);
    } catch (err: any) {
      next(err);
    }
  },
);

router.post(
  "/admin/metrics/jobs/alerts/digests/test",
  requireAuth,
  requireRole("SUPER_ADMIN"),
  async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    try {
      const summary = await jobMetricAlerts.sendTestQuietHoursDigestToUser(
        req.user!.id,
      );
      await logAudit(
        {
          userId: req.user!.id,
          action: "EXECUTE",
          entityType: "job_metric_alert_quiet_hours_digest",
          entityId: req.user!.id,
          newValue: `Test digest: sent=${summary.digestsSent}, noEvents=${summary.digestsSkippedNoEvents}, disabled=${summary.digestsSkippedDisabled}`,
        },
        req,
      );
      res.json(summary);
    } catch (err: any) {
      next(err);
    }
  },
);

router.post(
  "/admin/metrics/jobs/alerts/run",
  requireAuth,
  requireRole("SUPER_ADMIN"),
  async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    try {
      const result = await jobMetricAlerts.evaluateJobMetricAlerts();
      await logAudit(
        {
          userId: req.user!.id,
          action: "EXECUTE",
          entityType: "background_job",
          entityId: "job_metric_alerts",
          newValue: `Manual evaluation: ${result.alertsFired.length} fired, ${result.alertsSuppressed} suppressed`,
        },
        req,
      );
      res.json(result);
    } catch (err: any) {
      next(err);
    }
  },
);

export default router;

----- FILE: server/jobMetricAlertRulesRoutes.ts (full) -----
/**
 * Task #1540 — Extract the wage refresh editor's POST/DELETE alert rule
 * routes (originally inlined in `server/routes.ts`) into a small typed
 * router so the route's Zod schema can be imported by tests instead of
 * being copy/pasted.
 */
import { Router, type Response, type NextFunction } from "express";
import { z } from "zod";
import { requireAuth, requireRole, type AuthRequest } from "./auth";
import { logAudit } from "./auditLogger";
import { parseOrRespond } from "./utils/validation";
import {
  upsertJobMetricAlertRule,
  deleteJobMetricAlertRule,
} from "./utils/jobMetricAlerts";

export const upsertJobMetricAlertRuleSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  job: z.string().min(1).max(120),
  counter: z.string().min(1).max(120).optional().nullable(),
  kind: z.enum(["spike", "stall"]),
  threshold: z.number().positive().max(100000),
  minAbsolute: z.number().int().min(0).max(1_000_000).optional(),
  dedupeMinutes: z.number().int().min(1).max(24 * 60).optional(),
  enabled: z.boolean().optional(),
  emailWhenAbove: z.number().positive().max(100000).nullable().optional(),
});

export type UpsertJobMetricAlertRuleInput = z.infer<
  typeof upsertJobMetricAlertRuleSchema
>;

const router = Router();

router.post(
  "/admin/metrics/jobs/alerts/rules",
  requireAuth,
  requireRole("SUPER_ADMIN"),
  async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    try {
      const parsed = parseOrRespond(upsertJobMetricAlertRuleSchema, req.body, res);
      if (!parsed) return;
      const rule = await upsertJobMetricAlertRule(parsed);
      await logAudit(
        {
          userId: req.user!.id,
          action: parsed.id ? "UPDATE" : "CREATE",
          entityType: "job_metric_alert_rule",
          entityId: rule.id,
          newValue: JSON.stringify(parsed),
        },
        req,
      );
      res.json(rule);
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err instanceof z.ZodError) {
        res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid rule", details: err.errors });
        return;
      }
      next(err);
    }
  },
);

router.delete(
  "/admin/metrics/jobs/alerts/rules/:id",
  requireAuth,
  requireRole("SUPER_ADMIN"),
  async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    try {
      const ok = await deleteJobMetricAlertRule(req.params.id);
      if (!ok) {
        res.status(404).json({ error: "Rule not found" });
        return;
      }
      await logAudit(
        {
          userId: req.user!.id,
          action: "DELETE",
          entityType: "job_metric_alert_rule",
          entityId: req.params.id,
        },
        req,
      );
      res.json({ deleted: req.params.id });
    } catch (err: any) {
      next(err);
    }
  },
);

export default router;

----- FILE: server/ai/profitabilityEngine.ts (full) -----
import { logError } from "../utils/logger";
import { db } from "../db";
import { 
  projects, 
  timeEntries, 
  procurementItems, 
  changeOrders, 
  receipts,
  users,
  subcontractorContracts,
  subcontractorPayments,
  tmTickets,
  projectEquipmentPlans,
  purchaseOrders,
  purchaseOrderItems,
} from "@shared/schema";
import { eq, sql, and, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";

export interface ProjectProfitability {
  project_code: string;
  project_name: string;
  budget: number;
  total_labor_cost: number;
  total_material_cost: number;
  total_subcontractor_cost: number;
  total_equipment_cost: number;
  total_other_expenses: number;
  total_tm_cost: number;
  approved_change_orders_value: number;
  approved_tm_value: number;
  adjusted_budget: number;
  projected_budget: number;
  total_actual_cost: number;
  variance: number;
  variance_percent: number;
  status: 'ON_BUDGET' | 'MINOR_CONCERN' | 'MAJOR_RISK';
  projected_completion_cost: number;
  estimated_margin: number;
  committed_cost: number;
  forecast_final_cost: number;
  cost_prediction: {
    weekly_burn_rate: number;
    burn_rate_trend: 'INCREASING' | 'STABLE' | 'DECREASING';
    predicted_overrun: number;
    confidence_score: number;
    weeks_until_overrun: number | null;
    forecast_at_completion: number;
    early_warning: boolean;
    warning_message: string | null;
  };
  texturaDrawDate: string | null;
  drawDayOfMonth: number | null;
  billingUrgency: 'CRITICAL' | 'HIGH' | null;
  daysUntilDraw: number | null;
  key_metrics: {
    labor_hours: number;
    labor_efficiency: string;
    material_spend_rate: string;
    pending_change_orders_value: number;
    pending_tm_value: number;
    subcontractor_count: number;
    tm_ticket_count: number;
    equipment_planned_cost: number;
    equipment_actual_cost: number;
    equipment_cost_variance: number;
    owned_equipment_count: number;
    rental_equipment_count: number;
  };
}

export async function calculateProjectProfitability(projectCode: string): Promise<ProjectProfitability> {
  // Fetch project
  const [project] = await db
    .select()
    .from(projects)
    .where(eq(projects.code, projectCode))
    .limit(1);

  if (!project) {
    throw new Error(`Project ${projectCode} not found`);
  }

  // Calculate labor costs - using actual user cost rates from users table
  const laborEntries = await db
    .select({
      hoursRegular: timeEntries.hoursRegular,
      hoursOvertime: timeEntries.hoursOvertime,
      costRateRegular: users.costRateRegular,
      costRateOvertime: users.costRateOvertime,
      date: timeEntries.date,
    })
    .from(timeEntries)
    .leftJoin(users, eq(timeEntries.userId, users.id))
    .where(eq(timeEntries.projectId, project.id))
    .limit(5000);

  const DEFAULT_COST_RATE = 50;
  const DEFAULT_OT_MULTIPLIER = 1.5;

  const totalLaborHours = laborEntries.reduce(
    (sum, entry) => 
      sum + Number(entry.hoursRegular || 0) + Number(entry.hoursOvertime || 0),
    0
  );
  
  const totalLaborCost = laborEntries.reduce((sum, entry) => {
    const regularRate = entry.costRateRegular ? Number(entry.costRateRegular) : DEFAULT_COST_RATE;
    const overtimeRate = entry.costRateOvertime ? Number(entry.costRateOvertime) : (regularRate * DEFAULT_OT_MULTIPLIER);
    
    const regularCost = Number(entry.hoursRegular || 0) * regularRate;
    const overtimeCost = Number(entry.hoursOvertime || 0) * overtimeRate;
    return sum + regularCost + overtimeCost;
  }, 0);

  // Calculate material costs - using unit cost × quantity for procurement items
  const materials = await db
    .select({
      unitCost: procurementItems.unitCost,
      quantity: procurementItems.quantity,
      status: procurementItems.status,
    })
    .from(procurementItems)
    .where(eq(procurementItems.projectId, project.id));

  const totalMaterialCost = materials.reduce((sum, item) => {
    const cost = Number(item.unitCost || 0) * Number(item.quantity || 0);
    return sum + cost;
  }, 0);

  // Calculate other expenses from receipts
  const expenseReceipts = await db
    .select({
      amount: receipts.amount,
      date: receipts.date,
    })
    .from(receipts)
    .where(eq(receipts.projectId, project.id));

  const totalOtherExpenses = expenseReceipts.reduce((sum, r) => sum + Number(r.amount || 0), 0);

  // Calculate subcontractor costs - use only paid amounts
  const subcontractorCost = await db
    .select({
      total: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${subcontractorPayments.amount}), 0)`.as("total")
    })
    .from(subcontractorPayments)
    .innerJoin(subcontractorContracts, eq(subcontractorPayments.contractId, subcontractorContracts.id))
    .where(eq(subcontractorContracts.projectId, project.id));

  const totalSubcontractorCost = Number(subcontractorCost[0]?.total || 0);

  const subcontractorContractsData = await db
    .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` })
    .from(subcontractorContracts)
    .where(eq(subcontractorContracts.projectId, project.id));

  const subcontractorCount = Number(subcontractorContractsData[0]?.count || 0);

  const committedSubContracts = await db
    .select({ total: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${subcontractorContracts.contractAmount}), 0)`.as("total") })
    .from(subcontractorContracts)
    .where(and(eq(subcontractorContracts.projectId, project.id), sql`${subcontractorContracts.status} IN ('ACTIVE', 'PENDING', 'APPROVED')`));
  const committedSubTotal = Number(committedSubContracts[0]?.total || 0);

  const committedPOData = await db
    .select({ total: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${purchaseOrderItems.quantity} * ${purchaseOrderItems.unitCost}), 0)`.as("total") })
    .from(purchaseOrderItems)
    .innerJoin(purchaseOrders, eq(purchaseOrderItems.poId, purchaseOrders.id))
    .where(and(eq(purchaseOrders.projectId, project.id), sql`${purchaseOrders.status} IN ('APPROVED', 'SENT', 'PARTIAL', 'FULFILLED')`));
  const committedPOTotal = Number(committedPOData[0]?.total || 0);

  const totalCommittedCost = committedPOTotal + committedSubTotal;

  // Calculate equipment costs from project equipment plans
  const equipmentPlans = await db
    .select({
      source: projectEquipmentPlans.source,
      plannedCost: projectEquipmentPlans.plannedCost,
      actualCost: projectEquipmentPlans.actualCost,
      status: projectEquipmentPlans.status,
    })
    .from(projectEquipmentPlans)
    .where(eq(projectEquipmentPlans.projectId, project.id))
    .limit(500);

  const ownedEquipmentPlans = equipmentPlans.filter(p => p.source === 'OWNED');
  const rentalEquipmentPlans = equipmentPlans.filter(p => p.source === 'RENTAL');

  const equipmentPlannedCost = equipmentPlans.reduce(
    (sum, p) => sum + Number(p.plannedCost || 0), 0
  );
  const equipmentActualCost = equipmentPlans.reduce(
    (sum, p) => sum + Number(p.actualCost || 0), 0
  );
  
  // For equipment, use actual cost if available, otherwise use planned
  const totalEquipmentCost = equipmentPlans.reduce((sum, p) => {
    const cost = Number(p.actualCost || 0) > 0 
      ? Number(p.actualCost) 
      : Number(p.plannedCost || 0);
    return sum + cost;
  }, 0);

  const equipmentCostVariance = equipmentPlannedCost - equipmentActualCost;

  // Calculate change orders
  const projectChangeOrders = await db
    .select({
      amount: changeOrders.amount,
      status: changeOrders.status,
    })
    .from(changeOrders)
    .where(eq(changeOrders.projectId, project.id))
    .limit(500);

  const approvedCOs = projectChangeOrders.filter(co => co.status === 'APPROVED');
  const pendingCOs = projectChangeOrders.filter(co => 
    ['SUBMITTED', 'PENDING_APPROVAL', 'PCO', 'PRICING_REVIEW'].includes(co.status)
  );

  const approvedCOValue = approvedCOs.reduce((sum, co) => sum + Number(co.amount || 0), 0);
  const pendingCOValue = pendingCOs.reduce((sum, co) => sum + Number(co.amount || 0), 0);

  // Calculate T&M ticket costs - approved T&M adds to adjusted budget (billable extra work)
  const projectTMTickets = await db
    .select({
      status: tmTickets.status,
      grandTotal: tmTickets.grandTotal,
    })
    .from(tmTickets)
    .where(eq(tmTickets.projectId, project.id))
    .limit(500);

  const approvedTMTickets = projectTMTickets.filter(t => t.status === 'APPROVED');
  const pendingTMTickets = projectTMTickets.filter(t => 
    ['DRAFT', 'SUBMITTED'].includes(t.status)
  );

  // Calculate total T&M value from grandTotal (already includes all costs)
  const calculateTMValue = (tickets: typeof projectTMTickets) => {
    return tickets.reduce((sum, t) => sum + Number(t.grandTotal || 0), 0);
  };

  const approvedTMValue = calculateTMValue(approvedTMTickets);
  const pendingTMValue = calculateTMValue(pendingTMTickets);
  const totalTMCost = calculateTMValue(projectTMTickets); // All T&M for tracking

  // Calculate totals - use contractAmount as primary, fallback to budget
  // Adjusted budget includes approved change orders AND approved T&M tickets (billable extra work)
  const baseBudget = Number(project.contractAmount || project.budget || 0);
  const adjustedBudget = baseBudget + approvedCOValue + approvedTMValue;
  const totalActualCost = totalLaborCost + totalMaterialCost + totalSubcontractorCost + totalEquipmentCost + totalOtherExpenses;
  const variance = adjustedBudget - totalActualCost;
  const variancePercent = adjustedBudget > 0 ? (variance / adjustedBudget) * 100 : 0;

  // Determine status based on variance percentage
  let status: 'ON_BUDGET' | 'MINOR_CONCERN' | 'MAJOR_RISK';
  if (variancePercent >= 10) {
    status = 'ON_BUDGET';
  } else if (variancePercent >= 0) {
    status = 'MINOR_CONCERN';
  } else {
    status = 'MAJOR_RISK';
  }

  // Project completion cost based on timeline percentage complete
  const startDate = project.startDate ? new Date(project.startDate) : new Date();
  const endDate = project.estimatedCompletionDate ? new Date(project.estimatedCompletionDate) : new Date();
  const now = new Date();
  const totalDuration = endDate.getTime() - startDate.getTime();
  const elapsed = now.getTime() - startDate.getTime();
  
  // Guard against invalid date ranges
  let percentComplete = 50; // Default to 50% if dates are invalid
  if (totalDuration > 0 && elapsed >= 0) {
    percentComplete = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (elapsed / totalDuration) * 100));
  }
  
  const projectedCompletionCost = percentComplete > 0 && percentComplete < 100
    ? (totalActualCost / (percentComplete / 100))
    : totalActualCost;

  const estimatedMargin = adjustedBudget - projectedCompletionCost;

  const committedActuals = totalMaterialCost + totalSubcontractorCost;
  const remainingCommitted = Math.max(0, totalCommittedCost - committedActuals);
  const estimatedOpenRemaining = Math.max(0, projectedCompletionCost - totalActualCost - remainingCommitted);
  const forecastFinalCost = totalActualCost + remainingCommitted + estimatedOpenRemaining;

  // Labor efficiency - compare actual hours to budgeted hours (assume 40% of budget is labor)
  const budgetedLaborHours = baseBudget * 0.4 / DEFAULT_COST_RATE;
  const laborEfficiency = budgetedLaborHours > 0
    ? `${Math.round((totalLaborHours / budgetedLaborHours) * 100)}% of budgeted hours`
    : 'Unknown';

  // Material spend rate - compare actual material costs to budgeted (assume 45% of budget)
  const budgetedMaterials = baseBudget * 0.45;
  const materialSpendRate = budgetedMaterials > 0
    ? `${Math.round((totalMaterialCost / budgetedMaterials) * 100)}% of budget`
    : 'Unknown';

  // Calculate billing urgency based on recurring draw schedule or override date
  let billingUrgency: 'CRITICAL' | 'HIGH' | null = null;
  let daysUntilDraw: number | null = null;
  
  // Helper function to calculate next draw date from recurring day
  const calculateNextDrawDate = (dayOfMonth: number): Date => {
    const today = new Date();
    today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0); // Normalize to start of day for comparison
    const year = today.getFullYear();
    const month = today.getMonth();
    
    // Try current month first
    let nextDraw = new Date(year, month, dayOfMonth);
    nextDraw.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    
    // If the date has passed this month (or is today but we want future dates), use next month
    if (nextDraw <= today) {
      nextDraw = new Date(year, month + 1, dayOfMonth);
      nextDraw.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    }
    
    // Handle months with fewer days (e.g., day 31 in February becomes last day of month)
    if (nextDraw.getDate() !== dayOfMonth) {
      // Get last day of the target month
      nextDraw = new Date(year, month + (new Date(year, month, dayOfMonth) <= today ? 2 : 1), 0);
      nextDraw.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    }
    
    return nextDraw;
  };
  
  // Determine which draw date to use
  let drawDate: Date | null = null;
  const today = new Date();
  today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0); // Normalize for comparison
  
  if (project.texturaDrawDate) {
    const overrideDate = new Date(project.texturaDrawDate);
    overrideDate.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    
    // Only use override if it's in the future
    if (overrideDate > today) {
      drawDate = overrideDate;
    } else if (project.drawDayOfMonth) {
      // Override has passed, fall back to recurring schedule
      drawDate = calculateNextDrawDate(project.drawDayOfMonth);
    }
  } else if (project.drawDayOfMonth) {
    // Calculate next draw based on recurring day
    drawDate = calculateNextDrawDate(project.drawDayOfMonth);
  }
  
  if (drawDate) {
    const diffDays = Math.ceil((drawDate.getTime() - today.getTime()) / 86400000);
    daysUntilDraw = diffDays;
    // Only set urgency for positive days (future dates)
    if (diffDays > 0 && diffDays <= 3) {
      billingUrgency = "CRITICAL";
    } else if (diffDays > 0 && diffDays <= 7) {
      billingUrgency = "HIGH";
    }
  }

  const elapsedWeeks = Math.max(1, elapsed / (7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
  const weeklyBurnRate = totalActualCost / elapsedWeeks;

  const halfwayPoint = new Date(startDate.getTime() + elapsed / 2);
  let firstHalfCost = 0;
  let secondHalfCost = 0;

  for (const entry of laborEntries) {
    const entryDate = entry.date ? new Date(entry.date) : null;
    if (!entryDate) continue;
    const regularRate = entry.costRateRegular ? Number(entry.costRateRegular) : DEFAULT_COST_RATE;
    const overtimeRate = entry.costRateOvertime ? Number(entry.costRateOvertime) : (regularRate * DEFAULT_OT_MULTIPLIER);
    const entryCost = Number(entry.hoursRegular || 0) * regularRate + Number(entry.hoursOvertime || 0) * overtimeRate;
    if (entryDate < halfwayPoint) {
      firstHalfCost += entryCost;
    } else {
      secondHalfCost += entryCost;
    }
  }

  for (const receipt of expenseReceipts) {
    const receiptDate = receipt.date ? new Date(receipt.date) : null;
    if (!receiptDate) continue;
    const amount = Number(receipt.amount) || 0;
    if (receiptDate < halfwayPoint) {
      firstHalfCost += amount;
    } else {
      secondHalfCost += amount;
    }
  }

  const halfElapsed = elapsedWeeks / 2;
  const firstHalfRate = halfElapsed > 0 ? firstHalfCost / halfElapsed : 0;
  const secondHalfRate = halfElapsed > 0 ? secondHalfCost / halfElapsed : 0;

  let burnRateTrend: 'INCREASING' | 'STABLE' | 'DECREASING' = 'STABLE';
  if (firstHalfRate > 0 && elapsedWeeks >= 2) {
    const trendRatio = secondHalfRate / firstHalfRate;
    if (trendRatio > 1.15) burnRateTrend = 'INCREASING';
    else if (trendRatio < 0.85) burnRateTrend = 'DECREASING';
  }

  const remainingWeeks = totalDuration > 0
    ? Math.max(0, (endDate.getTime() - now.getTime()) / (7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000))
    : 0;
  const forecastAtCompletion = totalActualCost + (weeklyBurnRate * remainingWeeks);
  const predictedOverrun = forecastAtCompletion - adjustedBudget;

  let weeksUntilOverrun: number | null = null;
  if (weeklyBurnRate > 0 && predictedOverrun > 0) {
    const remainingBudget = adjustedBudget - totalActualCost;
    weeksUntilOverrun = remainingBudget > 0 ? Math.round(remainingBudget / weeklyBurnRate) : 0;
  }

  let confidenceScore = 50;
  if (elapsedWeeks >= 8) confidenceScore += 20;
  else if (elapsedWeeks >= 4) confidenceScore += 10;
  if (percentComplete >= 30 && percentComplete <= 80) confidenceScore += 15;
  if (totalLaborHours > 100) confidenceScore += 10;
  if (burnRateTrend === 'STABLE') confidenceScore += 5;
  confidenceScore = Math.min(95, Math.max(20, confidenceScore));

  const earlyWarning = predictedOverrun > 0 && status !== 'MAJOR_RISK';
  let warningMessage: string | null = null;
  if (earlyWarning) {
    warningMessage = `Project projected to exceed budget by $${Math.round(predictedOverrun).toLocaleString()}${weeksUntilOverrun != null ? ` in ~${weeksUntilOverrun} weeks` : ''}. Current burn rate: $${Math.round(weeklyBurnRate).toLocaleString()}/week.`;
  } else if (burnRateTrend === 'INCREASING' && status !== 'MAJOR_RISK') {
    warningMessage = `Burn rate increasing — recent weekly spend trending ${Math.round((secondHalfRate / Math.max(1, firstHalfRate) - 1) * 100)}% higher than earlier periods.`;
  }

  const costPrediction = {
    weekly_burn_rate: Math.round(weeklyBurnRate * 100) / 100,
    burn_rate_trend: burnRateTrend,
    predicted_overrun: Math.round(predictedOverrun * 100) / 100,
    confidence_score: confidenceScore,
    weeks_until_overrun: weeksUntilOverrun,
    forecast_at_completion: Math.round(forecastAtCompletion * 100) / 100,
    early_warning: earlyWarning,
    warning_message: warningMessage,
  };

  return {
    project_code: projectCode,
    project_name: project.name,
    budget: baseBudget,
    total_labor_cost: Math.round(totalLaborCost * 100) / 100,
    total_material_cost: Math.round(totalMaterialCost * 100) / 100,
    total_subcontractor_cost: Math.round(totalSubcontractorCost * 100) / 100,
    total_equipment_cost: Math.round(totalEquipmentCost * 100) / 100,
    total_other_expenses: Math.round(totalOtherExpenses * 100) / 100,
    total_tm_cost: Math.round(totalTMCost * 100) / 100,
    approved_change_orders_value: Math.round(approvedCOValue * 100) / 100,
    approved_tm_value: Math.round(approvedTMValue * 100) / 100,
    adjusted_budget: Math.round(adjustedBudget * 100) / 100,
    projected_budget: Math.round((adjustedBudget + pendingCOValue) * 100) / 100,
    total_actual_cost: Math.round(totalActualCost * 100) / 100,
    variance: Math.round(variance * 100) / 100,
    variance_percent: Math.round(variancePercent * 100) / 100,
    status,
    projected_completion_cost: Math.round(projectedCompletionCost * 100) / 100,
    estimated_margin: Math.round(estimatedMargin * 100) / 100,
    committed_cost: Math.round(totalCommittedCost * 100) / 100,
    forecast_final_cost: Math.round(forecastFinalCost * 100) / 100,
    cost_prediction: costPrediction,
    texturaDrawDate: project.texturaDrawDate || null,
    drawDayOfMonth: project.drawDayOfMonth || null,
    billingUrgency,
    daysUntilDraw,
    key_metrics: {
      labor_hours: Math.round(totalLaborHours * 100) / 100,
      labor_efficiency: laborEfficiency,
      material_spend_rate: materialSpendRate,
      pending_change_orders_value: Math.round(pendingCOValue * 100) / 100,
      pending_tm_value: Math.round(pendingTMValue * 100) / 100,
      subcontractor_count: subcontractorCount,
      tm_ticket_count: projectTMTickets.length,
      equipment_planned_cost: Math.round(equipmentPlannedCost * 100) / 100,
      equipment_actual_cost: Math.round(equipmentActualCost * 100) / 100,
      equipment_cost_variance: Math.round(equipmentCostVariance * 100) / 100,
      owned_equipment_count: ownedEquipmentPlans.length,
      rental_equipment_count: rentalEquipmentPlans.length,
    },
  };
}

export async function calculateAllProjectsProfitability(): Promise<ProjectProfitability[]> {
  // Get all active projects
  const activeProjects = await db
    .select({ code: projects.code })
    .from(projects)
    .where(sql`${projects.status} IN ('ACTIVE', 'PLANNING')`)
    .limit(100);

  const results: ProjectProfitability[] = [];

  for (const project of activeProjects) {
    try {
      const profitability = await calculateProjectProfitability(project.code);
      results.push(profitability);
    } catch (error) {
      logError(`Error calculating profitability for ${project.code}:`, {}, error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error)));
    }
  }

  return results.sort((a, b) => {
    const statusOrder = { 'MAJOR_RISK': 0, 'MINOR_CONCERN': 1, 'ON_BUDGET': 2 };
    return statusOrder[a.status] - statusOrder[b.status];
  });
}

----- FILE: server/profitabilityAlertRoutes.ts (full) -----
import { logError } from "./utils/logger";
import { Router } from "express";
import { requireAuth } from "./auth";
import type { AuthRequest } from "./auth";
import { db } from "./db";
import { projects, organizations, users, projectProfitabilitySnapshots, notifications } from "@shared/schema";
import { eq, desc, and, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { calculateProjectProfitability } from "./ai/profitabilityEngine";
const router = Router();

function canAccessProfitability(role?: string): boolean {
  return role === "SUPER_ADMIN" || role === "ADMIN" || role === "HUSTLER" ||
    role === "MANAGER" || role === "SUPERINTENDENT" || role === "ESTIMATOR";
}

router.get("/api/projects/:code/profitability/live", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    const { code } = req.params;
    const orgId = req.user!.organizationId;

    if (!canAccessProfitability(req.user!.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Insufficient permissions" });
    }

    const [project] = await db.select().from(projects)
      .where(and(eq(projects.code, code), eq(projects.gcOrganizationId, orgId!)))
      .limit(1);

    if (!project) {
      return res.status(404).json({ error: "Project not found" });
    }

    const data = await calculateProjectProfitability(code);

    const [latestSnapshot] = await db.select()
      .from(projectProfitabilitySnapshots)
      .where(eq(projectProfitabilitySnapshots.projectId, project.id))
      .orderBy(desc(projectProfitabilitySnapshots.createdAt))
      .limit(1);

    return res.json({
      ...data,
      lastCalculated: new Date().toISOString(),
      previousSnapshot: latestSnapshot ? {
        totalActualCost: Number(latestSnapshot.totalActualCost),
        variancePercent: Number(latestSnapshot.variancePercent),
        status: latestSnapshot.status,
        createdAt: latestSnapshot.createdAt,
      } : null,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

router.get("/api/projects/:code/profitability/history", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    const { code } = req.params;
    const orgId = req.user!.organizationId;

    if (!canAccessProfitability(req.user!.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Insufficient permissions" });
    }

    const [project] = await db.select().from(projects)
      .where(and(eq(projects.code, code), eq(projects.gcOrganizationId, orgId!)))
      .limit(1);

    if (!project) {
      return res.status(404).json({ error: "Project not found" });
    }

    const snapshots = await db.select()
      .from(projectProfitabilitySnapshots)
      .where(eq(projectProfitabilitySnapshots.projectId, project.id))
      .orderBy(desc(projectProfitabilitySnapshots.createdAt))
      .limit(48);

    return res.json(snapshots);
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

export default router;

----- FILE: server/events/listeners/costEngineListener.ts (full) -----
import { eventBus } from "../eventBus";
import { logError } from "../../utils/logger";
import { postTransaction, bulkPost } from "../../costEngineService";
import type {
  PayrollRunCompletedEvent,
  PoReceiptRecordedEvent,
  TmTicketApprovedEvent,
  SubcontractorPaymentMadeEvent,
  ReceiptCreatedEvent,
} from "../eventTypes";

export function registerCostEngineListener(): void {
  eventBus.on("payroll.run.completed", async (event: PayrollRunCompletedEvent) => {
    try {
      const { db } = await import("../../db");
      const { costLedgerPostings } = await import("@shared/schema");
      const { eq } = await import("drizzle-orm");

      const postings = await db
        .select()
        .from(costLedgerPostings)
        .where(eq(costLedgerPostings.payRunId, event.payload.payRunId));

      if (postings.length > 0) {
        const inputs = postings.map(p => ({
          projectId: p.projectId,
          costCode: p.costCode || "UNASSIGNED",
          sourceCategory: "LABOR" as const,
          originModule: "PAYROLL" as const,
          amount: Number(p.burdenedTotalAmount) > 0 ? String(p.burdenedTotalAmount) : String(p.totalAmount),
          transactionDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
          referenceId: `payrun:${event.payload.payRunId}`,
          description: `Payroll run ${event.payload.periodStart} – ${event.payload.periodEnd}`,
          createdBy: event.actorId !== "system" ? event.actorId : undefined,
        }));
        await bulkPost(inputs);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      logError("costEngineListener: payroll.run.completed failed", { error: String(err) });
    }
  });

  eventBus.on("po.receipt_recorded", async (event: PoReceiptRecordedEvent) => {
    try {
      const inputs = event.payload.items
        .filter(item => item.amount > 0)
        .map(item => ({
          projectId: event.payload.projectId,
          costCode: item.costCode || "UNASSIGNED",
          sourceCategory: "MATERIAL" as const,
          originModule: "PO" as const,
          amount: String(item.amount),
          transactionDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
          referenceId: `po_receipt:${event.payload.receiptId}`,
          description: item.description || `PO receipt for PO ${event.payload.poId}`,
          createdBy: event.actorId !== "system" ? event.actorId : undefined,
        }));

      if (inputs.length > 0) {
        await bulkPost(inputs);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      logError("costEngineListener: po.receipt_recorded failed", { error: String(err) });
    }
  });

  eventBus.on("tm_ticket.approved", async (event: TmTicketApprovedEvent) => {
    try {
      const inputs = [];
      const baseRef = `tm_ticket:${event.payload.ticketId}`;
      const date = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
      const costCode = event.payload.costCode || "UNASSIGNED";

      if (event.payload.laborCost > 0) {
        inputs.push({
          projectId: event.payload.projectId,
          costCode,
          sourceCategory: "LABOR" as const,
          originModule: "TM" as const,
          amount: String(event.payload.laborCost),
          transactionDate: date,
          referenceId: baseRef,
          description: `T&M ticket labor`,
          createdBy: event.actorId !== "system" ? event.actorId : undefined,
        });
      }
      if (event.payload.equipmentCost > 0) {
        inputs.push({
          projectId: event.payload.projectId,
          costCode,
          sourceCategory: "EQUIPMENT" as const,
          originModule: "TM" as const,
          amount: String(event.payload.equipmentCost),
          transactionDate: date,
          referenceId: baseRef,
          description: `T&M ticket equipment`,
          createdBy: event.actorId !== "system" ? event.actorId : undefined,
        });
      }
      if (event.payload.materialCost > 0) {
        inputs.push({
          projectId: event.payload.projectId,
          costCode,
          sourceCategory: "MATERIAL" as const,
          originModule: "TM" as const,
          amount: String(event.payload.materialCost),
          transactionDate: date,
          referenceId: baseRef,
          description: `T&M ticket materials`,
          createdBy: event.actorId !== "system" ? event.actorId : undefined,
        });
      }

      if (inputs.length > 0) {
        await bulkPost(inputs);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      logError("costEngineListener: tm_ticket.approved failed", { error: String(err) });
    }
  });

  eventBus.on("subcontractor.payment_made", async (event: SubcontractorPaymentMadeEvent) => {
    try {
      if (event.payload.amount > 0) {
        await postTransaction({
          projectId: event.payload.projectId,
          costCode: event.payload.costCode || "UNASSIGNED",
          sourceCategory: "SUBCONTRACTOR",
          originModule: "SUBCONTRACTOR",
          amount: String(event.payload.amount),
          transactionDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
          referenceId: `sub_payment:${event.payload.paymentId}`,
          description: `Subcontractor payment for contract ${event.payload.contractId}`,
          createdBy: event.actorId !== "system" ? event.actorId : undefined,
        });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      logError("costEngineListener: subcontractor.payment_made failed", { error: String(err) });
    }
  });

  eventBus.on("receipt.created", async (event: ReceiptCreatedEvent) => {
    try {
      if (event.payload.amount > 0) {
        await postTransaction({
          projectId: event.payload.projectId,
          costCode: event.payload.costCode || "UNASSIGNED",
          sourceCategory: "OTHER",
          originModule: "RECEIPT",
          amount: String(event.payload.amount),
          transactionDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
          referenceId: `receipt:${event.payload.receiptId}`,
          description: `Receipt expense${event.payload.category ? ` (${event.payload.category})` : ""}`,
          createdBy: event.actorId !== "system" ? event.actorId : undefined,
        });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      logError("costEngineListener: receipt.created failed", { error: String(err) });
    }
  });
}

----- FILE: server/costLedgerService.ts (full) -----
import { db } from "./db";
import {
  timeEntries,
  users,
  purchaseOrders,
  purchaseOrderItems,
  poReceipts,
  poReceiptItems,
  subcontractorContracts,
  subcontractorPayments,
  tmTickets,
  projectEquipmentPlans,
  receipts,
  budgetLineItems,
  forecastAdjustments,
  costLedgerPostings,
} from "@shared/schema";
import { eq, sql, and, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import { hasTransactions, getActualsByProject } from "./costEngineService";

export interface CostCodeLedgerRow {
  costCode: string;
  description: string;
  division: string;
  budgetedLabor: number;
  budgetedMaterial: number;
  budgetedSub: number;
  budgetedEquipment: number;
  budgetedOther: number;
  budgetedTotal: number;
  committedPO: number;
  committedSub: number;
  committedTotal: number;
  actualLabor: number;
  actualMaterial: number;
  actualSub: number;
  actualEquipment: number;
  actualTM: number;
  actualOther: number;
  actualTotal: number;
  remainingBudget: number;
  percentUsed: number;
  forecast: number;
  etc: number;
  ffc: number;
  projectedVariance: number;
  forecastAdjustment: number | null;
  forecastAdjustmentNote: string | null;
  alertLevel: string | null;
}

export interface CostLedgerSummary {
  rows: CostCodeLedgerRow[];
  totals: CostCodeLedgerRow;
  totalRowCount: number;
  projectBudget: number;
  budgetLineTotal: number;
  percentComplete: number;
  budgetAlertThreshold: number;
  budgetCriticalThreshold: number;
}

const DEFAULT_COST_RATE = 50;
const DEFAULT_OT_MULTIPLIER = 1.5;

export async function calculateCostLedger(
  projectId: string,
  projectBudget: number,
  projectStartDate?: Date | null,
  projectEndDate?: Date | null,
  alertThreshold: number = 80,
  criticalThreshold: number = 95
): Promise<CostLedgerSummary> {
  const costMap = new Map<string, CostCodeLedgerRow>();

  const ensureRow = (costCode: string, description?: string, division?: string): CostCodeLedgerRow => {
    if (!costMap.has(costCode)) {
      costMap.set(costCode, {
        costCode,
        description: description || costCode,
        division: division || "Unassigned",
        budgetedLabor: 0, budgetedMaterial: 0, budgetedSub: 0, budgetedEquipment: 0, budgetedOther: 0, budgetedTotal: 0,
        committedPO: 0, committedSub: 0, committedTotal: 0,
        actualLabor: 0, actualMaterial: 0, actualSub: 0, actualEquipment: 0, actualTM: 0, actualOther: 0, actualTotal: 0,
        remainingBudget: 0, percentUsed: 0, forecast: 0,
        etc: 0, ffc: 0, projectedVariance: 0,
        forecastAdjustment: null, forecastAdjustmentNote: null,
        alertLevel: null,
      });
    }
    return costMap.get(costCode)!;
  };

  const lines = await db.select().from(budgetLineItems).where(eq(budgetLineItems.projectId, projectId));
  for (const line of lines) {
    const row = ensureRow(normalizeCostCode(line.costCode) || line.costCode, line.description, line.division);
    row.budgetedLabor = Number(line.laborBudget);
    row.budgetedMaterial = Number(line.materialBudget);
    row.budgetedSub = Number(line.subcontractorBudget);
    row.budgetedEquipment = Number(line.equipmentBudget);
    row.budgetedOther = Number(line.otherBudget);
    row.budgetedTotal = row.budgetedLabor + row.budgetedMaterial + row.budgetedSub + row.budgetedEquipment + row.budgetedOther;
  }

  const useUnifiedEngine = await hasTransactions(projectId);
  let unifiedActuals: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getActualsByProject>> | null = null;

  if (useUnifiedEngine) {
    unifiedActuals = await getActualsByProject(projectId);
    for (const [cc, entry] of unifiedActuals.byCostCode) {
      const row = ensureRow(cc);
      row.actualLabor += entry.labor;
      row.actualMaterial += entry.material;
      row.actualSub += entry.subcontractor;
      row.actualEquipment += entry.equipment;
      row.actualOther += entry.other;
    }
  }

  const poItems = await db
    .select({
      poItemId: purchaseOrderItems.id,
      costCode: purchaseOrderItems.costCode,
      quantity: purchaseOrderItems.quantity,
      unitCost: purchaseOrderItems.unitCost,
      poStatus: purchaseOrders.status,
      poId: purchaseOrders.id,
    })
    .from(purchaseOrderItems)
    .innerJoin(purchaseOrders, eq(purchaseOrderItems.poId, purchaseOrders.id))
    .where(eq(purchaseOrders.projectId, projectId))
    .limit(10000);

  for (const item of poItems) {
    const cc = normalizeCostCode(item.costCode);
    const row = ensureRow(cc);
    const lineCost = Number(item.quantity || 0) * Number(item.unitCost || 0);
    const status = item.poStatus as string;
    if (["APPROVED", "SENT", "PARTIAL", "FULFILLED"].includes(status)) {
      row.committedPO += lineCost;
    }
  }

  const subContracts = await db
    .select({
      id: subcontractorContracts.id,
      contractAmount: subcontractorContracts.contractAmount,
      status: subcontractorContracts.status,
      scopeOfWork: subcontractorContracts.scopeOfWork,
      costCode: subcontractorContracts.costCode,
    })
    .from(subcontractorContracts)
    .where(eq(subcontractorContracts.projectId, projectId));

  for (const contract of subContracts) {
    const cc = normalizeCostCode(contract.costCode || "UNASSIGNED");
    const row = ensureRow(cc, cc === "UNASSIGNED" ? "Subcontractors" : undefined, cc === "UNASSIGNED" ? "Unassigned" : undefined);
    const amount = Number(contract.contractAmount);
    if (["ACTIVE", "PENDING", "APPROVED"].includes(contract.status)) {
      row.committedSub += amount;
    }
  }

  if (!useUnifiedEngine) {
    const laborEntries = await db
      .select({
        costCode: timeEntries.costCode,
        hoursRegular: timeEntries.hoursRegular,
        hoursOvertime: timeEntries.hoursOvertime,
        costRateRegular: users.costRateRegular,
        costRateOvertime: users.costRateOvertime,
        approvalStatus: timeEntries.approvalStatus,
      })
      .from(timeEntries)
      .leftJoin(users, eq(timeEntries.userId, users.id))
      .where(eq(timeEntries.projectId, projectId))
      .limit(10000);

    const payrollPostings = await db
      .select({
        costCode: costLedgerPostings.costCode,
        totalAmount: costLedgerPostings.totalAmount,
        burdenedTotalAmount: costLedgerPostings.burdenedTotalAmount,
      })
      .from(costLedgerPostings)
      .where(eq(costLedgerPostings.projectId, projectId))
      .limit(10000);

    const payrollCostByCode = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const posting of payrollPostings) {
      const cc = normalizeCostCode(posting.costCode);
      const amount = Number(posting.burdenedTotalAmount) > 0 ? Number(posting.burdenedTotalAmount) : Number(posting.totalAmount);
      payrollCostByCode.set(cc, (payrollCostByCode.get(cc) || 0) + amount);
    }

    if (payrollPostings.length > 0) {
      for (const [cc, amount] of payrollCostByCode) {
        const row = ensureRow(cc);
        row.actualLabor += amount;
      }
    } else {
      for (const entry of laborEntries) {
        if (entry.approvalStatus !== "APPROVED") continue;
        const cc = normalizeCostCode(entry.costCode);
        const row = ensureRow(cc);
        const regRate = entry.costRateRegular ? Number(entry.costRateRegular) : DEFAULT_COST_RATE;
        const otRate = entry.costRateOvertime ? Number(entry.costRateOvertime) : regRate * DEFAULT_OT_MULTIPLIER;
        const cost = Number(entry.hoursRegular || 0) * regRate + Number(entry.hoursOvertime || 0) * otRate;
        row.actualLabor += cost;
      }
    }

    const receivablePoIds = poItems
      .filter(i => ["PARTIAL", "FULFILLED"].includes(i.poStatus as string))
      .map(i => i.poId);

    if (receivablePoIds.length > 0) {
      const uniquePoIds = [...new Set(receivablePoIds)];
      const receiptRows = await db
        .select({
          poItemId: poReceiptItems.poItemId,
          quantityReceived: poReceiptItems.quantityReceived,
        })
        .from(poReceiptItems)
        .innerJoin(poReceipts, eq(poReceiptItems.receiptId, poReceipts.id))
        .where(inArray(poReceipts.poId, uniquePoIds))
        .limit(10000);

      const receivedQtyMap = new Map<string, number>();
      for (const r of receiptRows) {
        receivedQtyMap.set(r.poItemId, (receivedQtyMap.get(r.poItemId) || 0) + Number(r.quantityReceived));
      }

      for (const item of poItems) {
        if (!["PARTIAL", "FULFILLED"].includes(item.poStatus as string)) continue;
        const cc = normalizeCostCode(item.costCode);
        const row = ensureRow(cc);
        const receivedQty = receivedQtyMap.get(item.poItemId) || 0;
        if (receivedQty > 0) {
          row.actualMaterial += receivedQty * Number(item.unitCost || 0);
        }
      }
    }

    const contractCostCodeMap = new Map<string, string | null>();
    for (const contract of subContracts) {
      contractCostCodeMap.set(contract.id, contract.costCode);
    }

    if (subContracts.length > 0) {
      const contractIds = subContracts.map(c => c.id);
      const payments = await db
        .select({
          contractId: subcontractorPayments.contractId,
          amount: subcontractorPayments.amount,
          status: subcontractorPayments.status,
        })
        .from(subcontractorPayments)
        .where(inArray(subcontractorPayments.contractId, contractIds))
        .limit(5000);

      for (const payment of payments) {
        if (payment.status !== "APPROVED" && payment.status !== "PAID") continue;
        const parentCostCode = contractCostCodeMap.get(payment.contractId) || "UNASSIGNED";
        const cc = normalizeCostCode(parentCostCode);
        const row = ensureRow(cc, cc === "UNASSIGNED" ? "Subcontractors" : undefined, cc === "UNASSIGNED" ? "Unassigned" : undefined);
        row.actualSub += Number(payment.amount);
      }
    }

    const equipPlans = await db
      .select({
        plannedCost: projectEquipmentPlans.plannedCost,
        actualCost: projectEquipmentPlans.actualCost,
        costCode: projectEquipmentPlans.costCode,
      })
      .from(projectEquipmentPlans)
      .where(eq(projectEquipmentPlans.projectId, projectId))
      .limit(500);

    for (const plan of equipPlans) {
      const cc = normalizeCostCode(plan.costCode || "UNASSIGNED");
      const row = ensureRow(cc, cc === "UNASSIGNED" ? "Equipment (unassigned)" : undefined, cc === "UNASSIGNED" ? "Unassigned" : undefined);
      const cost = Number(plan.actualCost || 0) > 0 ? Number(plan.actualCost) : Number(plan.plannedCost || 0);
      row.actualEquipment += cost;
    }

    const tmTicketRows = await db
      .select({
        grandTotal: tmTickets.grandTotal,
        status: tmTickets.status,
        costCode: tmTickets.costCode,
      })
      .from(tmTickets)
      .where(eq(tmTickets.projectId, projectId));

    for (const ticket of tmTicketRows) {
      if (ticket.status === "APPROVED") {
        const cc = normalizeCostCode(ticket.costCode || "UNASSIGNED");
        const row = ensureRow(cc, cc === "UNASSIGNED" ? "T&M Tickets" : undefined, cc === "UNASSIGNED" ? "Unassigned" : undefined);
        row.actualTM += Number(ticket.grandTotal || 0);
      }
    }

    const expenseReceipts = await db
      .select({
        amount: receipts.amount,
        category: receipts.category,
        costCode: receipts.costCode,
      })
      .from(receipts)
      .where(eq(receipts.projectId, projectId))
      .limit(5000);

    for (const receipt of expenseReceipts) {
      const cc = normalizeCostCode(receipt.costCode || "UNASSIGNED");
      const row = ensureRow(cc, cc === "UNASSIGNED" ? "Other Expenses" : undefined, cc === "UNASSIGNED" ? "Unassigned" : undefined);
      row.actualOther += Number(receipt.amount || 0);
    }
  }

  const adjustments = await db.select().from(forecastAdjustments).where(eq(forecastAdjustments.projectId, projectId));
  const adjustmentMap = new Map<string, { adjustedETC: number; note: string | null }>();
  for (const adj of adjustments) {
    adjustmentMap.set(normalizeCostCode(adj.costCode), { adjustedETC: Number(adj.adjustedETC), note: adj.note });
  }

  let percentComplete = 0;
  if (projectStartDate && projectEndDate) {
    const start = new Date(projectStartDate).getTime();
    const end = new Date(projectEndDate).getTime();
    const now = Date.now();
    const total = end - start;
    if (total > 0) {
      percentComplete = Math.min(100, Math.max(1, ((now - start) / total) * 100));
    }
  }

  const rows: CostCodeLedgerRow[] = [];
  for (const row of costMap.values()) {
    row.committedTotal = row.committedPO + row.committedSub;
    row.actualTotal = row.actualLabor + row.actualMaterial + row.actualSub + row.actualEquipment + row.actualTM + row.actualOther;
    row.remainingBudget = row.budgetedTotal - row.actualTotal;
    row.percentUsed = row.budgetedTotal > 0 ? (row.actualTotal / row.budgetedTotal) * 100 : (row.actualTotal > 0 ? 100 : 0);

    if (percentComplete > 0 && percentComplete < 100) {
      row.forecast = row.actualTotal / (percentComplete / 100);
    } else if (row.budgetedTotal > 0 && row.actualTotal > 0) {
      const budgetRatio = row.actualTotal / row.budgetedTotal;
      row.forecast = budgetRatio > 0 ? row.budgetedTotal * Math.max(1, budgetRatio) : row.actualTotal;
    } else {
      row.forecast = row.actualTotal;
    }

    const adj = adjustmentMap.get(row.costCode);
    if (adj) {
      row.forecastAdjustment = adj.adjustedETC;
      row.forecastAdjustmentNote = adj.note;
    }

    const committedActuals = row.actualMaterial + row.actualSub;
    const remainingCommitted = Math.max(0, row.committedTotal - committedActuals);

    let estimatedOpenRemaining = 0;
    if (row.budgetedTotal > 0) {
      const coveredByActualsAndCommitted = row.actualTotal + remainingCommitted;
      estimatedOpenRemaining = Math.max(0, row.budgetedTotal - coveredByActualsAndCommitted);
    }

    if (adj && adj.adjustedETC !== null) {
      row.etc = adj.adjustedETC;
    } else {
      row.etc = remainingCommitted + estimatedOpenRemaining;
    }

    row.ffc = row.actualTotal + row.etc;
    row.projectedVariance = row.budgetedTotal - row.ffc;

    if (row.budgetedTotal > 0) {
      if (row.percentUsed >= criticalThreshold) {
        row.alertLevel = "CRITICAL";
      } else if (row.percentUsed >= alertThreshold) {
        row.alertLevel = "WARNING";
      }
    }

    row.actualLabor = round2(row.actualLabor);
    row.actualMaterial = round2(row.actualMaterial);
    row.actualSub = round2(row.actualSub);
    row.actualEquipment = round2(row.actualEquipment);
    row.actualTM = round2(row.actualTM);
    row.actualOther = round2(row.actualOther);
    row.actualTotal = round2(row.actualTotal);
    row.committedPO = round2(row.committedPO);
    row.committedSub = round2(row.committedSub);
    row.committedTotal = round2(row.committedTotal);
    row.remainingBudget = round2(row.remainingBudget);
    row.percentUsed = round2(row.percentUsed);
    row.forecast = round2(row.forecast);
    row.etc = round2(row.etc);
    row.ffc = round2(row.ffc);
    row.projectedVariance = round2(row.projectedVariance);

    rows.push(row);
  }

  rows.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.costCode === "UNASSIGNED") return 1;
    if (b.costCode === "UNASSIGNED") return -1;
    return a.costCode.localeCompare(b.costCode);
  });

  const totals: CostCodeLedgerRow = {
    costCode: "TOTAL",
    description: "Project Total",
    division: "",
    budgetedLabor: 0, budgetedMaterial: 0, budgetedSub: 0, budgetedEquipment: 0, budgetedOther: 0, budgetedTotal: 0,
    committedPO: 0, committedSub: 0, committedTotal: 0,
    actualLabor: 0, actualMaterial: 0, actualSub: 0, actualEquipment: 0, actualTM: 0, actualOther: 0, actualTotal: 0,
    remainingBudget: 0, percentUsed: 0, forecast: 0,
    etc: 0, ffc: 0, projectedVariance: 0,
    forecastAdjustment: null, forecastAdjustmentNote: null,
    alertLevel: null,
  };

  for (const row of rows) {
    totals.budgetedLabor += row.budgetedLabor;
    totals.budgetedMaterial += row.budgetedMaterial;
    totals.budgetedSub += row.budgetedSub;
    totals.budgetedEquipment += row.budgetedEquipment;
    totals.budgetedOther += row.budgetedOther;
    totals.budgetedTotal += row.budgetedTotal;
    totals.committedPO += row.committedPO;
    totals.committedSub += row.committedSub;
    totals.committedTotal += row.committedTotal;
    totals.actualLabor += row.actualLabor;
    totals.actualMaterial += row.actualMaterial;
    totals.actualSub += row.actualSub;
    totals.actualEquipment += row.actualEquipment;
    totals.actualTM += row.actualTM;
    totals.actualOther += row.actualOther;
    totals.actualTotal += row.actualTotal;
    totals.etc += row.etc;
    totals.ffc += row.ffc;
  }

  totals.budgetedLabor = round2(totals.budgetedLabor);
  totals.budgetedMaterial = round2(totals.budgetedMaterial);
  totals.budgetedSub = round2(totals.budgetedSub);
  totals.budgetedEquipment = round2(totals.budgetedEquipment);
  totals.budgetedOther = round2(totals.budgetedOther);
  totals.budgetedTotal = round2(totals.budgetedTotal);
  totals.committedPO = round2(totals.committedPO);
  totals.committedSub = round2(totals.committedSub);
  totals.committedTotal = round2(totals.committedTotal);
  totals.actualLabor = round2(totals.actualLabor);
  totals.actualMaterial = round2(totals.actualMaterial);
  totals.actualSub = round2(totals.actualSub);
  totals.actualEquipment = round2(totals.actualEquipment);
  totals.actualTM = round2(totals.actualTM);
  totals.actualOther = round2(totals.actualOther);
  totals.actualTotal = round2(totals.actualTotal);
  totals.remainingBudget = round2(totals.budgetedTotal - totals.actualTotal);
  totals.percentUsed = totals.budgetedTotal > 0 ? round2((totals.actualTotal / totals.budgetedTotal) * 100) : 0;
  totals.etc = round2(totals.etc);
  totals.ffc = round2(totals.ffc);
  totals.projectedVariance = round2(totals.budgetedTotal - totals.ffc);

  if (percentComplete > 0 && percentComplete < 100) {
    totals.forecast = round2(totals.actualTotal / (percentComplete / 100));
  } else if (totals.budgetedTotal > 0 && totals.actualTotal > 0) {
    const budgetRatio = totals.actualTotal / totals.budgetedTotal;
    totals.forecast = round2(totals.budgetedTotal * Math.max(1, budgetRatio));
  } else {
    totals.forecast = round2(totals.actualTotal);
  }

  if (totals.budgetedTotal > 0) {
    if (totals.percentUsed >= criticalThreshold) {
      totals.alertLevel = "CRITICAL";
    } else if (totals.percentUsed >= alertThreshold) {
      totals.alertLevel = "WARNING";
    }
  }

  const budgetLineTotal = round2(totals.budgetedTotal);

  return {
    rows,
    totals,
    totalRowCount: rows.length,
    projectBudget: projectBudget,
    budgetLineTotal,
    percentComplete: round2(percentComplete),
    budgetAlertThreshold: alertThreshold,
    budgetCriticalThreshold: criticalThreshold,
  };
}

function normalizeCostCode(raw: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!raw || raw.trim() === "") return "UNASSIGNED";
  const parts = raw.split(" - ");
  return parts[0].trim();
}

function round2(n: number): number {
  return Math.round(n * 100) / 100;
}

----- FILE: server/costEngineService.ts (full) -----
import { db } from "./db";
import { costTransactions } from "@shared/schema";
import { eq, and, sql, desc, gte, lte, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import type { InsertCostTransaction, CostTransaction } from "@shared/schema";

export type CostSourceCategory = "LABOR" | "MATERIAL" | "EQUIPMENT" | "SUBCONTRACTOR" | "OTHER";
export type CostOriginModule = "ESTIMATE" | "PO" | "PAYROLL" | "TM" | "RECEIPT" | "CHANGE_ORDER" | "EQUIPMENT" | "SUBCONTRACTOR";

export interface PostTransactionInput {
  projectId: string;
  costCode: string;
  sourceCategory: CostSourceCategory;
  originModule: CostOriginModule;
  amount: string | number;
  transactionDate: string;
  referenceId?: string;
  description?: string;
  createdBy?: string;
}

export interface CostSummaryRow {
  groupKey: string;
  totalAmount: number;
  transactionCount: number;
}

export interface CostSummaryFilters {
  projectId: string;
  startDate?: string;
  endDate?: string;
  sourceCategory?: CostSourceCategory;
  originModule?: CostOriginModule;
  costCode?: string;
  groupBy?: "cost_code" | "source_category" | "origin_module" | "month";
}

function normalizeCostCode(raw: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!raw || raw.trim() === "") return "UNASSIGNED";
  const parts = raw.split(" - ");
  return parts[0].trim();
}

export async function postTransaction(input: PostTransactionInput): Promise<CostTransaction> {
  if (input.referenceId) {
    const existing = await db
      .select()
      .from(costTransactions)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(costTransactions.projectId, input.projectId),
          eq(costTransactions.referenceId, input.referenceId),
          eq(costTransactions.sourceCategory, input.sourceCategory),
          eq(costTransactions.costCode, normalizeCostCode(input.costCode)),
          eq(costTransactions.status, "ACTIVE")
        )
      )
      .limit(1);
    if (existing.length > 0) {
      return existing[0];
    }
  }

  const [row] = await db.insert(costTransactions).values({
    projectId: input.projectId,
    costCode: normalizeCostCode(input.costCode),
    sourceCategory: input.sourceCategory,
    originModule: input.originModule,
    amount: String(input.amount),
    transactionDate: input.transactionDate,
    referenceId: input.referenceId || null,
    description: input.description || null,
    createdBy: input.createdBy || null,
    status: "ACTIVE",
  }).returning();
  return row;
}

export async function voidTransaction(
  transactionId: string,
  projectId: string,
  voidedBy: string,
  reason?: string
): Promise<CostTransaction | null> {
  const [row] = await db
    .update(costTransactions)
    .set({
      status: "VOIDED",
      voidedAt: new Date(),
      voidedBy,
      voidReason: reason || null,
    })
    .where(and(
      eq(costTransactions.id, transactionId),
      eq(costTransactions.projectId, projectId),
      eq(costTransactions.status, "ACTIVE")
    ))
    .returning();
  return row || null;
}

export async function bulkPost(inputs: PostTransactionInput[]): Promise<CostTransaction[]> {
  if (inputs.length === 0) return [];

  const results: CostTransaction[] = [];
  const toInsert: typeof inputs = [];

  for (const input of inputs) {
    if (input.referenceId) {
      const existing = await db
        .select()
        .from(costTransactions)
        .where(
          and(
            eq(costTransactions.projectId, input.projectId),
            eq(costTransactions.referenceId, input.referenceId),
            eq(costTransactions.sourceCategory, input.sourceCategory),
            eq(costTransactions.costCode, normalizeCostCode(input.costCode)),
            eq(costTransactions.status, "ACTIVE")
          )
        )
        .limit(1);
      if (existing.length > 0) {
        results.push(existing[0]);
        continue;
      }
    }
    toInsert.push(input);
  }

  if (toInsert.length > 0) {
    const inserted = [];
    for (const input of toInsert) {
      const [row] = await db.insert(costTransactions).values({
        projectId: input.projectId,
        costCode: normalizeCostCode(input.costCode),
        sourceCategory: input.sourceCategory,
        originModule: input.originModule,
        amount: String(input.amount),
        transactionDate: input.transactionDate,
        referenceId: input.referenceId || null,
        description: input.description || null,
        createdBy: input.createdBy || null,
        status: "ACTIVE",
      }).returning();
      inserted.push(row);
    }
    results.push(...inserted);
  }

  return results;
}

export async function getByProject(
  projectId: string,
  options?: {
    startDate?: string;
    endDate?: string;
    sourceCategory?: CostSourceCategory;
    originModule?: CostOriginModule;
    costCode?: string;
    limit?: number;
    offset?: number;
  }
): Promise<{ transactions: CostTransaction[]; total: number }> {
  const conditions = [
    eq(costTransactions.projectId, projectId),
    eq(costTransactions.status, "ACTIVE"),
  ];

  if (options?.startDate) {
    conditions.push(gte(costTransactions.transactionDate, options.startDate));
  }
  if (options?.endDate) {
    conditions.push(lte(costTransactions.transactionDate, options.endDate));
  }
  if (options?.sourceCategory) {
    conditions.push(eq(costTransactions.sourceCategory, options.sourceCategory));
  }
  if (options?.originModule) {
    conditions.push(eq(costTransactions.originModule, options.originModule));
  }
  if (options?.costCode) {
    conditions.push(eq(costTransactions.costCode, options.costCode));
  }

  const whereClause = and(...conditions);

  const [countResult] = await db
    .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
    .from(costTransactions)
    .where(whereClause!);

  const transactions = await db
    .select()
    .from(costTransactions)
    .where(whereClause!)
    .orderBy(desc(costTransactions.transactionDate), desc(costTransactions.createdAt))
    .limit(options?.limit || 100)
    .offset(options?.offset || 0);

  return { transactions, total: countResult?.count || 0 };
}

export async function getByCostCode(
  projectId: string,
  costCode: string
): Promise<CostTransaction[]> {
  return db
    .select()
    .from(costTransactions)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(costTransactions.projectId, projectId),
        eq(costTransactions.costCode, costCode),
        eq(costTransactions.status, "ACTIVE")
      )
    )
    .orderBy(desc(costTransactions.transactionDate));
}

export async function getSummary(filters: CostSummaryFilters): Promise<CostSummaryRow[]> {
  const conditions = [
    eq(costTransactions.projectId, filters.projectId),
    eq(costTransactions.status, "ACTIVE"),
  ];

  if (filters.startDate) {
    conditions.push(gte(costTransactions.transactionDate, filters.startDate));
  }
  if (filters.endDate) {
    conditions.push(lte(costTransactions.transactionDate, filters.endDate));
  }
  if (filters.sourceCategory) {
    conditions.push(eq(costTransactions.sourceCategory, filters.sourceCategory));
  }
  if (filters.originModule) {
    conditions.push(eq(costTransactions.originModule, filters.originModule));
  }
  if (filters.costCode) {
    conditions.push(eq(costTransactions.costCode, filters.costCode));
  }

  const whereClause = and(...conditions);
  const groupBy = filters.groupBy || "cost_code";

  let groupExpr;
  switch (groupBy) {
    case "source_category":
      groupExpr = sql<string>`${costTransactions.sourceCategory}`;
      break;
    case "origin_module":
      groupExpr = sql<string>`${costTransactions.originModule}`;
      break;
    case "month":
      groupExpr = sql<string>`to_char(${costTransactions.transactionDate}::date, 'YYYY-MM')`;
      break;
    default:
      groupExpr = sql<string>`${costTransactions.costCode}`;
  }

  const rows = await db
    .select({
      groupKey: groupExpr,
      totalAmount: sql<number>`sum(${costTransactions.amount}::numeric)::float`,
      transactionCount: sql<number>`count(*)::int`,
    })
    .from(costTransactions)
    .where(whereClause!)
    .groupBy(groupExpr)
    .orderBy(groupExpr);

  return rows.map(r => ({
    groupKey: String(r.groupKey),
    totalAmount: Math.round((r.totalAmount || 0) * 100) / 100,
    transactionCount: r.transactionCount || 0,
  }));
}

export async function getActualsByProject(projectId: string): Promise<{
  labor: number;
  material: number;
  equipment: number;
  subcontractor: number;
  other: number;
  total: number;
  byCostCode: Map<string, { labor: number; material: number; equipment: number; subcontractor: number; other: number; tm: number; total: number }>;
}> {
  const rows = await db
    .select({
      costCode: costTransactions.costCode,
      sourceCategory: costTransactions.sourceCategory,
      originModule: costTransactions.originModule,
      totalAmount: sql<number>`sum(${costTransactions.amount}::numeric)::float`,
    })
    .from(costTransactions)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(costTransactions.projectId, projectId),
        eq(costTransactions.status, "ACTIVE")
      )
    )
    .groupBy(costTransactions.costCode, costTransactions.sourceCategory, costTransactions.originModule);

  let labor = 0, material = 0, equipment = 0, subcontractor = 0, other = 0;
  const byCostCode = new Map<string, { labor: number; material: number; equipment: number; subcontractor: number; other: number; tm: number; total: number }>();

  for (const row of rows) {
    const amount = row.totalAmount || 0;
    const cc = row.costCode || "UNASSIGNED";

    if (!byCostCode.has(cc)) {
      byCostCode.set(cc, { labor: 0, material: 0, equipment: 0, subcontractor: 0, other: 0, tm: 0, total: 0 });
    }
    const entry = byCostCode.get(cc)!;

    if (row.originModule === "TM") {
      entry.tm += amount;
    }

    switch (row.sourceCategory) {
      case "LABOR":
        labor += amount;
        entry.labor += amount;
        break;
      case "MATERIAL":
        material += amount;
        entry.material += amount;
        break;
      case "EQUIPMENT":
        equipment += amount;
        entry.equipment += amount;
        break;
      case "SUBCONTRACTOR":
        subcontractor += amount;
        entry.subcontractor += amount;
        break;
      case "OTHER":
        other += amount;
        entry.other += amount;
        break;
    }
    entry.total += amount;
  }

  const total = labor + material + equipment + subcontractor + other;
  return { labor, material, equipment, subcontractor, other, total, byCostCode };
}

export async function hasTransactions(projectId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const [result] = await db
    .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
    .from(costTransactions)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(costTransactions.projectId, projectId),
        eq(costTransactions.status, "ACTIVE")
      )
    )
    .limit(1);
  return (result?.count || 0) > 0;
}

export async function voidByReference(referenceId: string, voidedBy: string, reason?: string): Promise<number> {
  const voided = await db
    .update(costTransactions)
    .set({
      status: "VOIDED",
      voidedAt: new Date(),
      voidedBy,
      voidReason: reason || null,
    })
    .where(and(eq(costTransactions.referenceId, referenceId), eq(costTransactions.status, "ACTIVE")))
    .returning();
  return voided.length;
}

----- FILE: server/costLedgerRoutes.ts (full) -----
import { logError } from "./utils/logger";
import { Router } from "express";
import { db } from "./db";
import { projects, budgetLineItems, insertBudgetLineItemSchema, orgCostCodes, organizations, forecastAdjustments, budgetAlerts } from "@shared/schema";
import { eq, and, inArray, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { z } from "zod";
import { requireAuth, requireRole, type AuthRequest } from "./auth";
import { verifyOrgAccess } from "./orgFilter";
import { calculateCostLedger } from "./costLedgerService";
import { getCostCodesForTrade } from "@shared/costCodes";
import { successResponse } from "./responseHelper";

async function getValidCostCodes(orgId: string): Promise<Set<string>> {
  const [org] = await db.select({ tradeType: organizations.tradeType })
    .from(organizations).where(eq(organizations.id, orgId));
  const staticCodes = org?.tradeType ? getCostCodesForTrade(org.tradeType) : [];
  const customCodes = await db.select({ code: orgCostCodes.code })
    .from(orgCostCodes)
    .where(eq(orgCostCodes.organizationId, orgId));
  const validSet = new Set<string>();
  for (const c of staticCodes) validSet.add(c.code);
  for (const c of customCodes) validSet.add(c.code);
  return validSet;
}

async function validateCostCodes(orgId: string, codes: string[]): Promise<string[]> {
  if (codes.length === 0) return [];
  const validSet = await getValidCostCodes(orgId);
  return codes.filter(c => !validSet.has(c));
}

const router = Router();

function canAccessCostLedger(role?: string): boolean {
  return role === "ADMIN" || role === "MANAGER" || role === "SUPERINTENDENT" ||
    role === "ESTIMATOR" || role === "MANAGER" || role === "HUSTLER";
}

function canEditBudgetLines(role?: string): boolean {
  return role === "ADMIN" || role === "MANAGER";
}

async function getProjectByCode(code: string) {
  const [project] = await db.select().from(projects).where(eq(projects.code, code)).limit(1);
  return project;
}

router.get("/api/projects/:code/budget-lines", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    if (!canAccessCostLedger(req.user!.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Insufficient permissions" });
    }
    const project = await getProjectByCode(req.params.code);
    if (!project) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Project not found" });
    if (!verifyOrgAccess(req, res, project.gcOrganizationId)) return;

    const [lines, [summary]] = await Promise.all([
      db.select().from(budgetLineItems).where(eq(budgetLineItems.projectId, project.id)),
      db.select({
        totalLabor: sql<string>`COALESCE(SUM(${budgetLineItems.laborBudget}::numeric), 0)`,
        totalMaterial: sql<string>`COALESCE(SUM(${budgetLineItems.materialBudget}::numeric), 0)`,
        totalSubcontractor: sql<string>`COALESCE(SUM(${budgetLineItems.subcontractorBudget}::numeric), 0)`,
        totalEquipment: sql<string>`COALESCE(SUM(${budgetLineItems.equipmentBudget}::numeric), 0)`,
        totalOther: sql<string>`COALESCE(SUM(${budgetLineItems.otherBudget}::numeric), 0)`,
        grandTotal: sql<string>`COALESCE(SUM(${budgetLineItems.totalBudget}::numeric), 0)`,
      }).from(budgetLineItems).where(eq(budgetLineItems.projectId, project.id)),
    ]);

    res.json({
      lines,
      summary: summary ?? {
        totalLabor: "0", totalMaterial: "0", totalSubcontractor: "0",
        totalEquipment: "0", totalOther: "0", grandTotal: "0",
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

router.post("/api/projects/:code/budget-lines", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    if (!canEditBudgetLines(req.user!.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Insufficient permissions" });
    }
    const project = await getProjectByCode(req.params.code);
    if (!project) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Project not found" });
    if (!verifyOrgAccess(req, res, project.gcOrganizationId)) return;

    const parseResult = insertBudgetLineItemSchema.safeParse({
      ...req.body,
      projectId: project.id,
    });
    if (!parseResult.success) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Validation error", details: parseResult.error.flatten() });
    }

    const data = parseResult.data;
    const invalid = await validateCostCodes(project.gcOrganizationId!, [data.costCode]);
    if (invalid.length > 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: `Invalid cost code '${data.costCode}' for this organization` });
    }
    const total = Number(data.laborBudget || 0) + Number(data.materialBudget || 0) +
      Number(data.subcontractorBudget || 0) + Number(data.equipmentBudget || 0) + Number(data.otherBudget || 0);
    const [row] = await db.insert(budgetLineItems).values({ ...data, totalBudget: String(total) }).returning();
    evaluateBudgetAlerts(project.id).catch(e => logError("Budget alert eval error:", {}, e instanceof Error ? e : new Error(String(e))));
    res.status(201).json(row);
  } catch (error: unknown) {
    if (error instanceof Error && "code" in error && (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "23505") {
      return res.status(409).json({ error: "A budget line for that cost code already exists on this project" });
    }
    next(error);
  }
});

const bulkUpsertSchema = z.array(z.object({
  costCode: z.string().min(1),
  description: z.string().min(1),
  division: z.string().min(1),
  laborBudget: z.union([z.string(), z.number()]).transform(v => String(v)),
  materialBudget: z.union([z.string(), z.number()]).transform(v => String(v)),
  subcontractorBudget: z.union([z.string(), z.number()]).transform(v => String(v)),
  equipmentBudget: z.union([z.string(), z.number()]).transform(v => String(v)),
  otherBudget: z.union([z.string(), z.number()]).transform(v => String(v)),
}));

router.put("/api/projects/:code/budget-lines", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    if (!canEditBudgetLines(req.user!.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Insufficient permissions" });
    }
    const project = await getProjectByCode(req.params.code);
    if (!project) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Project not found" });
    if (!verifyOrgAccess(req, res, project.gcOrganizationId)) return;

    const parseResult = bulkUpsertSchema.safeParse(req.body);
    if (!parseResult.success) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Validation error", details: parseResult.error.flatten() });
    }
    const parsed = parseResult.data;

    if (parsed.length > 0) {
      const costCodes = parsed.map(l => l.costCode);
      const invalid = await validateCostCodes(project.gcOrganizationId!, costCodes);
      if (invalid.length > 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: `Invalid cost codes for this organization: ${invalid.join(", ")}` });
      }
    }

    const rows = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.delete(budgetLineItems).where(eq(budgetLineItems.projectId, project.id));
      if (parsed.length === 0) return [];
      const toInsert = parsed.map(line => {
        const total = Number(line.laborBudget) + Number(line.materialBudget) +
          Number(line.subcontractorBudget) + Number(line.equipmentBudget) + Number(line.otherBudget);
        return {
          projectId: project.id,
          costCode: line.costCode,
          description: line.description,
          division: line.division,
          laborBudget: line.laborBudget,
          materialBudget: line.materialBudget,
          subcontractorBudget: line.subcontractorBudget,
          equipmentBudget: line.equipmentBudget,
          otherBudget: line.otherBudget,
          totalBudget: String(total),
        };
      });
      return tx.insert(budgetLineItems).values(toInsert).returning();
    });
    evaluateBudgetAlerts(project.id).catch(e => logError("Budget alert eval error:", {}, e instanceof Error ? e : new Error(String(e))));
    res.json(rows);
  } catch (error: unknown) {
    next(error);
  }
});

router.patch("/api/projects/:code/budget-lines/:lineId", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    if (!canEditBudgetLines(req.user!.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Insufficient permissions" });
    }
    const project = await getProjectByCode(req.params.code);
    if (!project) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Project not found" });
    if (!verifyOrgAccess(req, res, project.gcOrganizationId)) return;

    const updates: Partial<{
      laborBudget: string;
      materialBudget: string;
      subcontractorBudget: string;
      equipmentBudget: string;
      otherBudget: string;
      description: string;
      division: string;
      updatedAt: Date;
    }> = { updatedAt: new Date() };
    const { laborBudget, materialBudget, subcontractorBudget, equipmentBudget, otherBudget, description, division } = req.body;
    if (laborBudget !== undefined) updates.laborBudget = String(laborBudget);
    if (materialBudget !== undefined) updates.materialBudget = String(materialBudget);
    if (subcontractorBudget !== undefined) updates.subcontractorBudget = String(subcontractorBudget);
    if (equipmentBudget !== undefined) updates.equipmentBudget = String(equipmentBudget);
    if (otherBudget !== undefined) updates.otherBudget = String(otherBudget);
    if (description !== undefined) updates.description = String(description);
    if (division !== undefined) updates.division = String(division);

    const [existing] = await db.select().from(budgetLineItems)
      .where(and(eq(budgetLineItems.id, req.params.lineId), eq(budgetLineItems.projectId, project.id)));
    if (!existing) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Budget line not found" });

    const merged = {
      laborBudget: updates.laborBudget ?? existing.laborBudget,
      materialBudget: updates.materialBudget ?? existing.materialBudget,
      subcontractorBudget: updates.subcontractorBudget ?? existing.subcontractorBudget,
      equipmentBudget: updates.equipmentBudget ?? existing.equipmentBudget,
      otherBudget: updates.otherBudget ?? existing.otherBudget,
    };
    const totalBudget = String(
      Number(merged.laborBudget) + Number(merged.materialBudget) +
      Number(merged.subcontractorBudget) + Number(merged.equipmentBudget) + Number(merged.otherBudget)
    );

    const [row] = await db.update(budgetLineItems).set({ ...updates, totalBudget })
      .where(and(eq(budgetLineItems.id, req.params.lineId), eq(budgetLineItems.projectId, project.id)))
      .returning();

    evaluateBudgetAlerts(project.id).catch(e => logError("Budget alert eval error:", {}, e instanceof Error ? e : new Error(String(e))));
    res.json(row);
  } catch (error: unknown) {
    next(error);
  }
});

router.delete("/api/projects/:code/budget-lines/:lineId", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    if (!canEditBudgetLines(req.user!.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Insufficient permissions" });
    }
    const project = await getProjectByCode(req.params.code);
    if (!project) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Project not found" });
    if (!verifyOrgAccess(req, res, project.gcOrganizationId)) return;

    const [deleted] = await db.delete(budgetLineItems)
      .where(and(eq(budgetLineItems.id, req.params.lineId), eq(budgetLineItems.projectId, project.id)))
      .returning();

    if (!deleted) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Budget line not found" });
    successResponse(res);
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
