router.get("/api/projects/:code/cost-ledger", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    if (!canAccessCostLedger(req.user!.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Insufficient permissions" });
    }
    const project = await getProjectByCode(req.params.code);
    if (!project) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Project not found" });
    if (!verifyOrgAccess(req, res, project.gcOrganizationId)) return;

    const projectBudget = Number(project.contractAmount || project.budget || 0);
    const result = await calculateCostLedger(
      project.id,
      projectBudget,
      project.startDate,
      project.estimatedCompletionDate,
      Number(project.budgetAlertThreshold || 80),
      Number(project.budgetCriticalThreshold || 95),
    );

    const page = Math.max(1, parseInt(req.query.page as string) || 1);
    const rawPageSize = req.query.pageSize ? parseInt(req.query.pageSize as string) : 0;
    const pageSize = rawPageSize > 0 ? Math.min(200, rawPageSize) : 0;

    if (pageSize > 0) {
      const totalRows = result.rows.length;
      const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRows / pageSize);
      const start = (page - 1) * pageSize;
      const paginatedRows = result.rows.slice(start, start + pageSize);
      res.json({
        ...result,
        rows: paginatedRows,
        totalRowCount: totalRows,
        pagination: { page, pageSize, totalPages, totalRows },
      });
    } else {
      res.json(result);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

const forecastAdjustmentSchema = z.object({
  costCode: z.string().min(1),
  adjustedETC: z.coerce.number().finite().min(0, "ETC must be non-negative").transform(v => String(v)),
  note: z.string().optional().nullable(),
});

router.put("/api/projects/:code/forecast-adjustments", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    if (!canEditBudgetLines(req.user!.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Insufficient permissions" });
    }
    const project = await getProjectByCode(req.params.code);
    if (!project) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Project not found" });
    if (!verifyOrgAccess(req, res, project.gcOrganizationId)) return;

    const parseResult = forecastAdjustmentSchema.safeParse(req.body);
    if (!parseResult.success) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Validation error", details: parseResult.error.flatten() });
    }

    const { costCode, adjustedETC, note } = parseResult.data;

    const [existing] = await db.select().from(forecastAdjustments)
      .where(and(eq(forecastAdjustments.projectId, project.id), eq(forecastAdjustments.costCode, costCode)));

    let row;
    if (existing) {
      [row] = await db.update(forecastAdjustments).set({
        adjustedETC,
        note: note || null,
        updatedBy: req.user!.id,
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      }).where(eq(forecastAdjustments.id, existing.id)).returning();
    } else {
      [row] = await db.insert(forecastAdjustments).values({
        projectId: project.id,
        costCode,
        adjustedETC,
        note: note || null,
        updatedBy: req.user!.id,
      }).returning();
    }

    evaluateBudgetAlerts(project.id).catch(e => logError("Budget alert eval error:", {}, e instanceof Error ? e : new Error(String(e))));
    res.json(row);
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

router.patch("/api/projects/:code/budget-thresholds", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    if (req.user!.role !== "ADMIN" && req.user!.role !== "MANAGER") {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Insufficient permissions" });
    }
    const project = await getProjectByCode(req.params.code);
    if (!project) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Project not found" });
    if (!verifyOrgAccess(req, res, project.gcOrganizationId)) return;

    const schema = z.object({
      budgetAlertThreshold: z.number().min(1).max(100).optional(),
      budgetCriticalThreshold: z.number().min(1).max(100).optional(),
    });
    const parseResult = schema.safeParse(req.body);
    if (!parseResult.success) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Validation error", details: parseResult.error.flatten() });
    }

    const updates: Record<string, any> = { updatedAt: new Date() };
    if (parseResult.data.budgetAlertThreshold !== undefined) {
      updates.budgetAlertThreshold = String(parseResult.data.budgetAlertThreshold);
    }
    if (parseResult.data.budgetCriticalThreshold !== undefined) {
      updates.budgetCriticalThreshold = String(parseResult.data.budgetCriticalThreshold);
    }

    const [updated] = await db.update(projects).set(updates).where(eq(projects.id, project.id)).returning();
    res.json({
      budgetAlertThreshold: Number(updated.budgetAlertThreshold || 80),
      budgetCriticalThreshold: Number(updated.budgetCriticalThreshold || 95),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

export async function evaluateBudgetAlerts(projectId: string): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const [project] = await db.select().from(projects).where(eq(projects.id, projectId));
    if (!project) return;

    const projectBudget = Number(project.contractAmount || project.budget || 0);
    const alertThreshold = Number(project.budgetAlertThreshold || 80);
    const criticalThreshold = Number(project.budgetCriticalThreshold || 95);

    const ledger = await calculateCostLedger(
      project.id,
      projectBudget,
      project.startDate,
      project.estimatedCompletionDate,
      alertThreshold,
      criticalThreshold,
    );

    const existingAlerts = await db.select().from(budgetAlerts)
      .where(and(eq(budgetAlerts.projectId, project.id), eq(budgetAlerts.isDismissed, false)));

    const existingAlertKeys = new Set(existingAlerts.map(a => `${a.costCode || 'TOTAL'}-${a.alertLevel}`));

    const alertsToCreate: Array<{
      projectId: string;
      costCode: string | null;
      alertLevel: string;
      thresholdPercent: string;
      currentPercent: string;
      budgetAmount: string;
      actualAmount: string;
    }> = [];

    for (const row of [...ledger.rows, ledger.totals]) {
      if (!row.alertLevel) continue;
      const costCodeKey = row.costCode === "TOTAL" ? null : row.costCode;
      const key = `${costCodeKey || 'TOTAL'}-${row.alertLevel}`;
      if (existingAlertKeys.has(key)) continue;

      alertsToCreate.push({
        projectId: project.id,
        costCode: costCodeKey,
        alertLevel: row.alertLevel,
        thresholdPercent: String(row.alertLevel === "CRITICAL" ? criticalThreshold : alertThreshold),
        currentPercent: String(row.percentUsed),
        budgetAmount: String(row.budgetedTotal),
        actualAmount: String(row.actualTotal),
      });
    }

    if (alertsToCreate.length > 0) {
      await db.insert(budgetAlerts).values(alertsToCreate);

      const { eventBus } = await import("./events");

      for (const alert of alertsToCreate) {
        await eventBus.emit({
          type: "budget.alert_triggered",
          version: 1,
          orgId: project.gcOrganizationId || "",
          actorId: "system",
          timestamp: new Date(),
          payload: {
            projectId: project.id,
            projectCode: project.code,
            costCode: alert.costCode || null,
            alertLevel: alert.alertLevel,
            thresholdPercent: Number(alert.thresholdPercent),
            currentPercent: Number(alert.currentPercent),
          },
        });
      }
    }
  } catch (error) {
    logError("evaluateBudgetAlerts error:", {}, error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error)));
  }
}

export default router;

function canAccessCostEngine(role?: string): boolean {

    role === "MANAGER" || role === "SUPERINTENDENT" || role === "ESTIMATOR";

  return project;

const postTransactionSchema = z.object({
  costCode: z.string().min(1),
  sourceCategory: z.enum(["LABOR", "MATERIAL", "EQUIPMENT", "SUBCONTRACTOR", "OTHER"]),
  originModule: z.enum(["ESTIMATE", "PO", "PAYROLL", "TM", "RECEIPT", "CHANGE_ORDER", "EQUIPMENT", "SUBCONTRACTOR"]),
  amount: z.coerce.number().finite(),
  transactionDate: z.string().min(1),
  referenceId: z.string().optional(),
  description: z.string().optional(),
});

router.post("/api/projects/:code/cost-transactions", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    const writeRoles = ["SUPER_ADMIN", "ADMIN", "HUSTLER", "MANAGER"];
    if (!writeRoles.includes(req.user!.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Insufficient permissions" });
    }
    const project = await getProjectByCode(req.params.code);
    if (!project) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Project not found" });
    if (!verifyOrgAccess(req, res, project.gcOrganizationId)) return;

    const parseResult = postTransactionSchema.safeParse(req.body);
    if (!parseResult.success) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Validation error", details: parseResult.error.flatten() });
    }

    const row = await postTransaction({
      projectId: project.id,
      ...parseResult.data,
      amount: String(parseResult.data.amount),
      createdBy: req.user!.id,
    });

    res.status(201).json(row);
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

    }
    const project = await getProjectByCode(req.params.code);

    const row = await voidTransaction(
      req.params.transactionId,
      project.id,
      req.user!.id,
      req.body.reason
    );

  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }

export async function saveAnalysisResults(
  results: ModuleAnalysisResult,
  organizationId?: string,
  projectId?: string,
  triggeredById?: string
): Promise<string[]> {
  const savedInsightIds: string[] = [];

  for (const insight of results.insights) {
    try {
      const [saved] = await db.insert(aiInsights).values({
        organizationId: organizationId!,
        projectId,
        module: insight.module as any,
        severity: insight.severity as any,
        title: insight.title,
        summary: insight.summary,
        details: insight.details,
        recommendations: insight.recommendations,
        metrics: insight.metrics ? JSON.stringify(insight.metrics) : null,
        affectedEntities: insight.affectedEntities ? JSON.stringify(insight.affectedEntities) : null,
        estimatedImpact: insight.estimatedImpact,
        actionItems: insight.actionItems ? JSON.stringify(insight.actionItems) : null,
        triggeredById,
        expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000),
      }).returning();

      savedInsightIds.push(saved.id);

      if (insight.module === 'SCHEDULING' && results.ganttRecommendations) {
        for (const gantt of results.ganttRecommendations) {
          await db.insert(aiGanttRecommendations).values({
            projectId: projectId!,
            insightId: saved.id,
            recommendationType: gantt.recommendationType,
            title: gantt.title,
            description: gantt.description,
            suggestedTasks: gantt.suggestedTasks ? JSON.stringify(gantt.suggestedTasks) : null,
            suggestedDependencies: gantt.suggestedDependencies ? JSON.stringify(gantt.suggestedDependencies) : null,
            estimatedDaysImprovement: gantt.estimatedDaysImprovement,
            estimatedCostSavings: gantt.estimatedCostSavings?.toString(),
          });
        }
      }

      if (insight.module === 'VENDOR_PRICING' && results.vendorComparisons && organizationId) {
        for (const comparison of results.vendorComparisons) {
          await db.insert(aiVendorComparisons).values({
            organizationId,
            insightId: saved.id,
            materialCategory: comparison.materialCategory,
            analysisData: JSON.stringify(comparison.analysisData),
            recommendedVendorId: comparison.recommendedVendorId,
            recommendedVendorName: comparison.recommendedVendorName,
            potentialSavings: comparison.potentialSavings?.toString(),
            priceConfidence: comparison.priceConfidence,
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      logError('[AI Save] Error saving insight:', {}, error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error)));
    }
  }

  return savedInsightIds;
}

import { and, desc, eq, gte, inArray, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

type RfiStatus = (typeof rfiStatusEnum.enumValues)[number];
type ChangeOrderStatus = (typeof changeOrderStatusEnum.enumValues)[number];
type PunchListStatus = (typeof punchListStatusEnum.enumValues)[number];

const OPEN_RFI_STATUSES: RfiStatus[] = ["DRAFT", "SUBMITTED", "UNDER_REVIEW", "FORWARDED"];
const OPEN_CO_STATUSES: ChangeOrderStatus[] = ["DRAFT", "PENDING_APPROVAL", "SUBMITTED", "PCO", "PRICING_REVIEW"];
const OPEN_PUNCH_STATUSES: PunchListStatus[] = ["OPEN", "IN_PROGRESS"];

export function startOfWeekUtc(d: Date): Date {
  const dt = new Date(d);
  dt.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  const day = dt.getUTCDay();
  dt.setUTCDate(dt.getUTCDate() - day);
  return dt;
}

export function weekStartIso(d: Date): string {
  return startOfWeekUtc(d).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

export function computeHealthScore(opts: {
  budgetVariancePct: number;
  openRfis: number;
  openCos: number;
  openPunch: number;
  schedulePctComplete: number;
  expectedPctComplete: number;
}): number {
  const { budgetVariancePct, openRfis, openCos, openPunch, schedulePctComplete, expectedPctComplete } = opts;
  let score = 100;
  if (budgetVariancePct < -15) score -= 35;
  else if (budgetVariancePct < -5) score -= 18;
  else if (budgetVariancePct < 0) score -= 8;
  const sched = schedulePctComplete - expectedPctComplete;
  if (sched < -20) score -= 25;
  else if (sched < -10) score -= 15;
  else if (sched < -5) score -= 7;
  if (openRfis > 20) score -= 10;
  else if (openRfis > 10) score -= 5;
  if (openCos > 8) score -= 8;
  else if (openCos > 4) score -= 4;
  if (openPunch > 30) score -= 8;
  else if (openPunch > 15) score -= 4;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(score)));
}

/**
 * Capture a per-project KPI snapshot for the current week. Idempotent:
 * re-running on the same day overwrites the row for the current week
 * (one row per project per week, keyed by `(projectId, weekStart)`).
 */
export async function captureWeeklyKpiSnapshots(now: Date = new Date()): Promise<{ snapshots: number }> {
  const weekStart = startOfWeekUtc(now);
  const weekStartStr = weekStart.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  // Burn for the *current* week is summed from time entries since the start of the week.
  const burnSince = weekStart;

  const projectRows = await db
    .select({
      id: projects.id,
      code: projects.code,
      gcOrganizationId: projects.gcOrganizationId,
      startDate: projects.startDate,
      endDate: projects.endDate,
      estimatedCompletionDate: projects.estimatedCompletionDate,
    })
    .from(projects);

  if (projectRows.length === 0) return { snapshots: 0 };

  const projectIds = projectRows.map((p) => p.id);

  const profitability = await calculateAllProjectsProfitability().catch((err) => {
    logError("kpi-snapshot.profitability", err);
    return [] as Awaited<ReturnType<typeof calculateAllProjectsProfitability>>;
  });
  const profByCode = new Map(profitability.map((p) => [p.project_code, p]));

  const [rfiRows, coRows, punchRows, schedRows, burnRows] = await Promise.all([
    db
      .select({ projectId: rfis.projectId, count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(rfis)
      .where(and(inArray(rfis.projectId, projectIds), inArray(rfis.status, OPEN_RFI_STATUSES)))
      .groupBy(rfis.projectId),
    db
      .select({ projectId: changeOrders.projectId, count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(changeOrders)
      .where(and(inArray(changeOrders.projectId, projectIds), inArray(changeOrders.status, OPEN_CO_STATUSES)))
      .groupBy(changeOrders.projectId),
    db
      .select({ projectId: punchListItems.projectId, count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(punchListItems)
      .where(and(inArray(punchListItems.projectId, projectIds), inArray(punchListItems.status, OPEN_PUNCH_STATUSES)))
      .groupBy(punchListItems.projectId),
    db
      .select({
        projectId: projectSchedules.projectId,
        avgPct: sql<number>`coalesce(avg(${projectScheduleTasks.percentComplete}), 0)::float`,
      })
      .from(projectSchedules)
      .leftJoin(projectScheduleTasks, eq(projectScheduleTasks.scheduleId, projectSchedules.id))
      .where(inArray(projectSchedules.projectId, projectIds))
      .groupBy(projectSchedules.projectId),
    db
      .select({
        projectId: timeEntries.projectId,
        hoursRegular: timeEntries.hoursRegular,
        hoursOvertime: timeEntries.hoursOvertime,
      })
      .from(timeEntries)
      .where(and(inArray(timeEntries.projectId, projectIds), gte(timeEntries.date, burnSince))),
  ]);

  const rfiMap = new Map(rfiRows.map((r) => [r.projectId, Number(r.count)]));
  const coMap = new Map(coRows.map((r) => [r.projectId, Number(r.count)]));
  const punchMap = new Map(punchRows.map((r) => [r.projectId, Number(r.count)]));
  const schedMap = new Map(schedRows.map((r) => [r.projectId, Number(r.avgPct) || 0]));

  const burnMap = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const r of burnRows) {
    const reg = parseFloat(r.hoursRegular || "0") || 0;
    const ot = parseFloat(r.hoursOvertime || "0") || 0;
    const dollars = (reg + ot * 1.5) * 50;
    burnMap.set(r.projectId, (burnMap.get(r.projectId) || 0) + dollars);
  }

  let inserted = 0;
  for (const p of projectRows) {
    const prof = profByCode.get(p.code);
    const variancePct = prof ? prof.variance_percent : 0;
    const schedulePct = schedMap.get(p.id) || 0;
    const end = p.endDate || p.estimatedCompletionDate;
    const expectedPct = (() => {
      if (!p.startDate || !end) return schedulePct;
      const endMs = end.getTime();
      const startMs = p.startDate.getTime();
      if (endMs <= startMs) return schedulePct;
      return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, ((now.getTime() - startMs) / (endMs - startMs)) * 100));
    })();
    const openRfis = rfiMap.get(p.id) || 0;
    const openCos = coMap.get(p.id) || 0;
    const openPunch = punchMap.get(p.id) || 0;
    const health = computeHealthScore({
      budgetVariancePct: variancePct,
      openRfis,
      openCos,
      openPunch,
      schedulePctComplete: schedulePct,
      expectedPctComplete: expectedPct,
    });
    const spi = expectedPct > 0 ? Math.round((schedulePct / expectedPct) * 1000) / 1000 : 1;
    const cpi = prof && prof.total_actual_cost > 0
      ? Math.round((prof.adjusted_budget / prof.total_actual_cost) * 1000) / 1000
      : 1;
    const burn = burnMap.get(p.id) || 0;

    await db
      .insert(projectKpiSnapshots)
      .values({
        organizationId: p.gcOrganizationId ?? null,
        projectId: p.id,
        weekStart: weekStartStr,
        health,
        spi: String(spi),
        cpi: String(cpi),
        burn: String(Math.round(burn * 100) / 100),
        variancePct: String(Math.round(variancePct * 100) / 100),
        schedulePct: Math.round(schedulePct),
        expectedPct: Math.round(expectedPct),
        openRfis,
        openCos,
        openPunch,
      })
      .onConflictDoUpdate({
        target: [projectKpiSnapshots.projectId, projectKpiSnapshots.weekStart],
        set: {
          organizationId: p.gcOrganizationId ?? null,
          health,
          spi: String(spi),
          cpi: String(cpi),
          burn: String(Math.round(burn * 100) / 100),
          variancePct: String(Math.round(variancePct * 100) / 100),
          schedulePct: Math.round(schedulePct),
          expectedPct: Math.round(expectedPct),
          openRfis,
          openCos,
          openPunch,
          capturedAt: new Date(),
        },
      });
    inserted += 1;
  }

  logInfo("Portfolio KPI snapshots captured", { weekStart: weekStartStr, projects: inserted });
  return { snapshots: inserted };
}

/**
 * Backfill up to `weeks` (default 12) prior weekly KPI snapshots for every
 * project from existing data sources:
 *   - variance / CPI: latest `project_profitability_snapshots` row whose
 *     `created_at` falls on or before each historical week-end
 *   - burn: sum of `time_entries` for that ISO week (Sun→Sat UTC)
 *   - openRfis / openCos / openPunch: approximate "open at end of week"
 *     counts derived from `created_at` and (resolved/closed/approved)At
 *     timestamps on each table
 *   - schedulePct / expectedPct: linear interpolation between project
 *     start and end (we have no historical schedule snapshots)
 *
 * Idempotent: relies on `project_kpi_snap_project_week_uniq` and uses
 * `onConflictDoNothing` so live capture rows already present for a week
 * are preserved.
 */
export async function backfillKpiSnapshots(
  options: { weeks?: number; now?: Date } = {},
): Promise<{ projects: number; weeks: number; inserted: number; skipped: number }> {
  const weeks = Math.max(1, Math.min(52, options.weeks ?? 12));
  const now = options.now ?? new Date();
  const currentWeekStart = startOfWeekUtc(now);

  // Build the list of week-start dates we want to backfill, oldest first.
  // We seed prior weeks only — the current week is handled by captureWeeklyKpiSnapshots.
  const weekStarts: Date[] = [];
  for (let i = weeks; i >= 1; i--) {
    const ws = new Date(currentWeekStart);
    ws.setUTCDate(ws.getUTCDate() - i * 7);
    weekStarts.push(ws);
  }
  const oldestWeekStart = weekStarts[0];

  const projectRows = await db
    .select({
      id: projects.id,
      code: projects.code,
      gcOrganizationId: projects.gcOrganizationId,
      startDate: projects.startDate,
      endDate: projects.endDate,
      estimatedCompletionDate: projects.estimatedCompletionDate,
    })
    .from(projects);

  if (projectRows.length === 0) {
    return { projects: 0, weeks: weekStarts.length, inserted: 0, skipped: 0 };
  }

  const projectIds = projectRows.map((p) => p.id);

  // Pull all relevant historical rows once and bucket in memory.
  const [profRows, rfiRows, coRows, punchRows, burnRows] = await Promise.all([
    db
      .select({
        projectId: projectProfitabilitySnapshots.projectId,
        createdAt: projectProfitabilitySnapshots.createdAt,
        adjustedBudget: projectProfitabilitySnapshots.adjustedBudget,
        totalActualCost: projectProfitabilitySnapshots.totalActualCost,
        variancePercent: projectProfitabilitySnapshots.variancePercent,
      })
      .from(projectProfitabilitySnapshots)
      .where(inArray(projectProfitabilitySnapshots.projectId, projectIds))
      .orderBy(desc(projectProfitabilitySnapshots.createdAt)),
    db
      .select({
        projectId: rfis.projectId,
        createdAt: rfis.createdAt,
        closedAt: rfis.closedAt,
      })
      .from(rfis)
      .where(inArray(rfis.projectId, projectIds)),
    db
      .select({
        projectId: changeOrders.projectId,
        createdAt: changeOrders.createdAt,
        approvedAt: changeOrders.approvedAt,
      })
      .from(changeOrders)
      .where(inArray(changeOrders.projectId, projectIds)),
    db
      .select({
        projectId: punchListItems.projectId,
        createdAt: punchListItems.createdAt,
        resolvedAt: punchListItems.resolvedAt,
      })
      .from(punchListItems)
      .where(inArray(punchListItems.projectId, projectIds)),
    db
      .select({
        projectId: timeEntries.projectId,
        date: timeEntries.date,
        hoursRegular: timeEntries.hoursRegular,
        hoursOvertime: timeEntries.hoursOvertime,
      })
      .from(timeEntries)
      .where(and(inArray(timeEntries.projectId, projectIds), gte(timeEntries.date, oldestWeekStart))),
  ]);

  // Bucket by project for fast per-week lookups.
  const profByProject = new Map<string, typeof profRows>();
  for (const r of profRows) {
    const arr = profByProject.get(r.projectId) ?? [];
    arr.push(r);
    profByProject.set(r.projectId, arr);
  }
  const rfiByProject = new Map<string, typeof rfiRows>();
  for (const r of rfiRows) {
    const arr = rfiByProject.get(r.projectId) ?? [];
    arr.push(r);
    rfiByProject.set(r.projectId, arr);
  }
  const coByProject = new Map<string, typeof coRows>();
  for (const r of coRows) {
    const arr = coByProject.get(r.projectId) ?? [];
    arr.push(r);
    coByProject.set(r.projectId, arr);
  }
  const punchByProject = new Map<string, typeof punchRows>();
  for (const r of punchRows) {
    const arr = punchByProject.get(r.projectId) ?? [];
    arr.push(r);
    punchByProject.set(r.projectId, arr);
  }

  // Bucket time entries into (projectId, weekStartIso) -> burn dollars.
  const burnByProjectWeek = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const r of burnRows) {
    if (!r.date) continue;
    const ws = startOfWeekUtc(r.date).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const key = `${r.projectId}::${ws}`;
    const reg = parseFloat(r.hoursRegular || "0") || 0;
    const ot = parseFloat(r.hoursOvertime || "0") || 0;
    const dollars = (reg + ot * 1.5) * 50;
    burnByProjectWeek.set(key, (burnByProjectWeek.get(key) || 0) + dollars);
  }

  let inserted = 0;
  let skipped = 0;

  for (const p of projectRows) {
    const projProf = profByProject.get(p.id) ?? [];
    const projRfis = rfiByProject.get(p.id) ?? [];
    const projCos = coByProject.get(p.id) ?? [];
    const projPunch = punchByProject.get(p.id) ?? [];

    for (const ws of weekStarts) {
      const weekEnd = new Date(ws);
      weekEnd.setUTCDate(weekEnd.getUTCDate() + 7);
      const weekStartStr = ws.toISOString().slice(0, 10);

      // Latest profitability snapshot strictly before the next week starts
      // (i.e. inside this week). profRows is ordered desc by createdAt
      // globally; per-project list inherits that order.
      const profAtWeek = projProf.find((r) => r.createdAt && r.createdAt.getTime() < weekEnd.getTime());
      const variancePct = profAtWeek ? Number(profAtWeek.variancePercent) || 0 : 0;
      const adjustedBudget = profAtWeek ? Number(profAtWeek.adjustedBudget) || 0 : 0;
      const actualCost = profAtWeek ? Number(profAtWeek.totalActualCost) || 0 : 0;
      const cpi = actualCost > 0 ? Math.round((adjustedBudget / actualCost) * 1000) / 1000 : 1;

      // Open-at-end-of-week approximations. Use strict `<` against the next
      // week's start so an event at exactly next-week-midnight bucketed
      // into the next week, not this one.
      const openRfis = projRfis.filter(
        (r) => r.createdAt && r.createdAt.getTime() < weekEnd.getTime() &&
          (!r.closedAt || r.closedAt.getTime() >= weekEnd.getTime()),
      ).length;
      const openCos = projCos.filter(
        (r) => r.createdAt && r.createdAt.getTime() < weekEnd.getTime() &&
          (!r.approvedAt || r.approvedAt.getTime() >= weekEnd.getTime()),
      ).length;
      const openPunch = projPunch.filter(
        (r) => r.createdAt && r.createdAt.getTime() < weekEnd.getTime() &&
          (!r.resolvedAt || r.resolvedAt.getTime() >= weekEnd.getTime()),
      ).length;

      // Schedule + expected % via linear interpolation between project start/end.
      const end = p.endDate || p.estimatedCompletionDate;
      let expectedPct = 0;
      if (p.startDate && end) {
        const startMs = p.startDate.getTime();
        const endMs = end.getTime();
        if (endMs > startMs) {
          expectedPct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, ((weekEnd.getTime() - startMs) / (endMs - startMs)) * 100));
        }
      }
      const schedulePct = expectedPct; // no historical schedule data

      const burn = burnByProjectWeek.get(`${p.id}::${weekStartStr}`) || 0;
      const spi = expectedPct > 0 ? Math.round((schedulePct / expectedPct) * 1000) / 1000 : 1;
      const health = computeHealthScore({
        budgetVariancePct: variancePct,
        openRfis,
        openCos,
        openPunch,
        schedulePctComplete: schedulePct,
        expectedPctComplete: expectedPct,
      });

      const result = await db
        .insert(projectKpiSnapshots)
        .values({
          organizationId: p.gcOrganizationId ?? null,
          projectId: p.id,
          weekStart: weekStartStr,
          health,
          spi: String(spi),
          cpi: String(cpi),
          burn: String(Math.round(burn * 100) / 100),
          variancePct: String(Math.round(variancePct * 100) / 100),
          schedulePct: Math.round(schedulePct),
          expectedPct: Math.round(expectedPct),
          openRfis,
          openCos,
          openPunch,
        })
        .onConflictDoNothing({
          target: [projectKpiSnapshots.projectId, projectKpiSnapshots.weekStart],
        })
        .returning({ id: projectKpiSnapshots.id });

      if (result.length > 0) inserted += 1;
      else skipped += 1;
    }
  }

  logInfo("Portfolio KPI snapshots backfilled", {
    projects: projectRows.length,
    weeks: weekStarts.length,
    inserted,
    skipped,
  });

  return { projects: projectRows.length, weeks: weekStarts.length, inserted, skipped };
}

----- KPI snapshot table creator — server/utils/ensureProjectKpiSnapshotsTable.ts -----
import { logInfo } from "./logger";
import { db } from "../db";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logError } from "./logger";

export async function ensureProjectKpiSnapshotsTable(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const check = await db.execute(sql`
      SELECT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_name = 'project_kpi_snapshots'
      )::boolean AS exists
    `);
    if (check.rows[0]?.exists === true) {
      logInfo("[migration] project_kpi_snapshots — already present, skipped");
      return;
    }

    await db.execute(sql`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "project_kpi_snapshots" (
        "id" varchar PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
        "organization_id" varchar REFERENCES "organizations"("id") ON DELETE CASCADE,
        "project_id" varchar NOT NULL REFERENCES "projects"("id") ON DELETE CASCADE,
        "week_start" date NOT NULL,
        "health" integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        "spi" numeric(8,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        "cpi" numeric(8,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        "burn" numeric(14,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        "variance_pct" numeric(8,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        "schedule_pct" integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        "expected_pct" integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        "open_rfis" integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        "open_cos" integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        "open_punch" integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        "captured_at" timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
      )
    `);
    await db.execute(sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "project_kpi_snap_project_idx" ON "project_kpi_snapshots" ("project_id")`);
    await db.execute(sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "project_kpi_snap_week_idx" ON "project_kpi_snapshots" ("week_start")`);
    await db.execute(sql`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "project_kpi_snap_project_week_uniq" ON "project_kpi_snapshots" ("project_id", "week_start")`);

    logInfo("[migration] project_kpi_snapshots — applied");
  } catch (err) {
    logError("[migration] Failed to ensure project_kpi_snapshots table:", {}, err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)));
  }
}

                  relatedEntityId: req.params.projectId,
                });

            logError("Failed to create material alert notification:", {}, notifError instanceof Error ? notifError : new Error(String(notifError)));
          }
        }
      }
    }

    res.status(201).json(newUsage);
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

----- FILE: server/executiveDigestRoutes.ts (full) -----
// prompt-injection-guard: system-prompt-anchored summarization over tenant-internal aggregated KPIs; no third-party text
import { Router, type Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { db } from "./db";
import { projects, changeOrders, bids, organizations, executiveDigests } from "@shared/schema";
import { eq, and, desc, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { z } from "zod";
import { type AuthRequest, requireAuth, requireRole } from "./auth";
import { logError } from "./utils/logger";
import { openai, AI_MODEL_GPT5 } from "./ai/openaiClient";
import { routedGeneralChatCompletion } from "./ai/providers";
import { governedAiCall } from "./services/aiGovernance";
import { isFeatureEnabled } from "./services/featureFlagService";
import { createExecutiveDigest, getLatestExecutiveDigest } from "./executiveDigest/storage";

const digestOutputSchema = z.object({
  executiveSummary: z.string().min(1),
  keyHighlights: z.array(z.string()).min(1),
  sections: z.array(z.object({
    title: z.string(),
    icon: z.enum(["financial", "operational", "risk", "opportunity"]),
    summary: z.string(),
    bullets: z.array(z.string()),
    sentiment: z.enum(["positive", "neutral", "negative"]),
  })).min(1),
  actionItems: z.array(z.object({
    priority: z.enum(["high", "medium", "low"]),
    description: z.string(),
    dueBy: z.string().optional(),
  })),
  kpiSnapshot: z.array(z.object({
    name: z.string(),
    current: z.number(),
    previous: z.number(),
    unit: z.string(),
    trend: z.enum(["up", "down", "stable"]),
  })),
});

const router = Router();

async function checkDigestFeatureFlag(req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<boolean> {
  const orgId = req.user!.organizationId;
  if (!orgId) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Organization required" });
    return false;
  }
  const enabled = await isFeatureEnabled(orgId, "EXECUTIVE_DIGEST");
  if (!enabled) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Executive Digest feature is not enabled for this organization" });
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

type ExecutiveDigestRow = typeof executiveDigests.$inferSelect;

function formatDigestResponse(digest: ExecutiveDigestRow) {
  return {
    id: digest.id,
    generatedAt: digest.generatedAt instanceof Date ? digest.generatedAt.toISOString() : String(digest.generatedAt),
    period: digest.period,
    executiveSummary: digest.executiveSummary,
    keyHighlights: digest.keyHighlights,
    sections: digest.sections,
    actionItems: digest.actionItems,
    kpiSnapshot: digest.kpiSnapshot,
  };
}

router.get("/api/executive/digest/:period", requireAuth, requireRole("ADMIN", "MANAGER", "HUSTLER"), async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  try {
    if (!(await checkDigestFeatureFlag(req, res))) return;

    const orgId = req.user!.organizationId!;
    const period = req.params.period;
    if (!["daily", "weekly", "monthly"].includes(period)) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid period. Must be daily, weekly, or monthly." });
    }

    const digest = await getLatestExecutiveDigest(orgId, period);

    if (!digest) {
      return res.json(null);
    }

    res.json(formatDigestResponse(digest));
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

router.post("/api/executive/digest/generate", requireAuth, requireRole("ADMIN", "MANAGER", "HUSTLER"), async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  try {
    if (!(await checkDigestFeatureFlag(req, res))) return;

    const orgId = req.user!.organizationId!;
    const period = req.body.period || "daily";
    if (!["daily", "weekly", "monthly"].includes(period)) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid period" });
    }

    const [org] = await db
      .select({ name: organizations.name })
      .from(organizations)
      .where(eq(organizations.id, orgId))
      .limit(1);

    const orgName = org?.name || "Organization";

    const activeProjectsResult = await db
      .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(projects)
      .where(and(eq(projects.status, "ACTIVE"), eq(projects.gcOrganizationId, orgId)));
    const activeProjects = activeProjectsResult[0]?.count || 0;

    const totalProjectsResult = await db
      .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(projects)
      .where(eq(projects.gcOrganizationId, orgId));
    const totalProjects = totalProjectsResult[0]?.count || 0;

    const completedProjectsResult = await db
      .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(projects)
      .where(and(eq(projects.status, "COMPLETE"), eq(projects.gcOrganizationId, orgId)));
    const completedProjects = completedProjectsResult[0]?.count || 0;

    const pendingCOResult = await db
      .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(changeOrders)
      .innerJoin(projects, eq(changeOrders.projectId, projects.id))
      .where(and(eq(changeOrders.status, "SUBMITTED"), eq(projects.gcOrganizationId, orgId)));
    const pendingChangeOrders = pendingCOResult[0]?.count || 0;

    const approvedCOResult = await db
      .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(changeOrders)
      .innerJoin(projects, eq(changeOrders.projectId, projects.id))
      .where(and(eq(changeOrders.status, "APPROVED"), eq(projects.gcOrganizationId, orgId)));
    const approvedChangeOrders = approvedCOResult[0]?.count || 0;

    const openBidsResult = await db
      .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(bids)
      .where(and(eq(bids.status, "DRAFT"), eq(bids.gcOrganizationId, orgId)));
    const openBids = openBidsResult[0]?.count || 0;

    const projectsList = await db
      .select({
        name: projects.name,
        status: projects.status,
        contractAmount: projects.contractAmount,
      })
      .from(projects)
      .where(eq(projects.gcOrganizationId, orgId))
      .orderBy(desc(projects.updatedAt))
      .limit(10);

    const dataContext = `
Organization: ${orgName}
Period: ${period}

Project Statistics:
- Total projects: ${totalProjects}
- Active projects: ${activeProjects}
- Completed projects: ${completedProjects}

Change Orders:
- Pending approval: ${pendingChangeOrders}
- Approved: ${approvedChangeOrders}

Bids:
- Open/Draft bids: ${openBids}

Recent Projects:
${projectsList.map((p) => `- ${p.name} (${p.status}) - Contract: $${p.contractAmount || "N/A"}`).join("\n")}
`;

    const systemPrompt = `You are an executive business intelligence analyst for a construction company. Generate a comprehensive ${period} executive digest based on the organization's data.

You MUST respond with valid JSON matching this exact structure:
{
  "executiveSummary": "A 2-3 sentence overview of the organization's current state",
  "keyHighlights": ["highlight1", "highlight2", "highlight3"],
  "sections": [
    {
      "title": "Section Title",
      "icon": "financial|operational|risk|opportunity",
      "summary": "Brief section summary",
      "bullets": ["bullet1", "bullet2"],
      "sentiment": "positive|neutral|negative"
    }
  ],
  "actionItems": [
    {
      "priority": "high|medium|low",
      "description": "Action item description",
      "dueBy": "Optional due date string"
    }
  ],
  "kpiSnapshot": [
    {
      "name": "KPI Name",
      "current": 100,
      "previous": 90,
      "unit": "$|%|#",
      "trend": "up|down|stable"
    }
  ]
}

Requirements:
- Generate 3-5 key highlights
- Include exactly 4 sections covering financial, operational, risk, and opportunity areas
- Include 3-6 action items prioritized by urgency
- Include 4-6 KPIs with realistic current/previous values based on the data
- Make the content specific to the data provided, not generic
- Use dollar amounts that make sense for a construction company
- Ensure all JSON is valid and parseable`;

    const aiResult = await governedAiCall({
      orgId,
      userId: req.user!.id,
      actionType: "executive_digest_generation",
      inputRefs: { period, orgId },
      fn: async () => {
        const completion = await routedGeneralChatCompletion({
          orgId,
          featureKey: "general.executive_digest",
          request: {
            messages: [
              { role: "system", content: systemPrompt },
              { role: "user", content: dataContext },
            ],
            response_format: { type: "json_object" },
            max_completion_tokens: 4096,
          },
        });

        const content = completion.choices[0]?.message?.content || "{}";
        const parsed = JSON.parse(content);
        return { result: parsed, completion };
      },
    });

    if (aiResult.blocked) {
      return res.status(429).json({ error: aiResult.reason || "AI usage limit exceeded" });
    }

    const parseResult = digestOutputSchema.safeParse(aiResult.result);
    if (!parseResult.success) {
      return next(new Error(`Failed to parse executive digest AI output: ${JSON.stringify(parseResult.error.format())}`));
    }

    const digestData = parseResult.data;

    const saved = await createExecutiveDigest({
      organizationId: orgId,
      period,
      executiveSummary: digestData.executiveSummary,
      keyHighlights: digestData.keyHighlights,
      sections: digestData.sections,
      actionItems: digestData.actionItems,
      kpiSnapshot: digestData.kpiSnapshot,
      generatedBy: req.user!.id,
    });

    res.json(formatDigestResponse(saved));
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

export default router;

----- FILE: server/aiCfoAnalyticsRoutes.ts (first 350 lines — full surface) -----
import { Router , NextFunction } from "express";
import { safeJsonParse } from "./responseHelper";
import { db } from "./db";
import { vendorPricebooks, vendorPricebookItems, aiCoDrafts, organizations, projects, changeOrders, equipment as equipmentTable } from "@shared/schema";
import { eq, desc, and, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { z } from "zod";
import { type AuthRequest, requireAuth, requireRole, requireAnyFeaturePermission } from "./auth";
import { logError } from "./utils/logger";
import { getOrgId, parsePagination } from "./utils/routeHelpers";
import { getLatestAIRun } from "./ai/orchestrator";
import { getAiAuditLog, getOrgLimitsWithUsage, getCostBreakdownByActionType, getCostBreakdownByKeySource } from "./services/aiGovernance";

const router = Router();

router.use("/cfo", requireAuth, requireAnyFeaturePermission('canUseAiFeatures', 'canViewFinancials'));

router.get("/cfo/profitability", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    const latestRun = await getLatestAIRun();

    if (latestRun?.profitabilityData) {
      const profitabilityData = safeJsonParse(latestRun.profitabilityData, null);
      return res.json(profitabilityData);
    }

    const { calculateAllProjectsProfitability } = await import("./ai/profitabilityEngine");
    const liveData = await calculateAllProjectsProfitability();
    res.json(liveData);
  } catch (error) {
  next(error);
  }
});

router.get("/cfo/equipment-metrics", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    const latestRun = await getLatestAIRun();

    if (!latestRun || !latestRun.snapshotData) {
      const allEquipment = await db.select().from(equipmentTable).where(eq(equipmentTable.organizationId, req.user!.organizationId!)).limit(500);
      const totalCount = allEquipment.length;
      const assignedCount = allEquipment.filter((e: any) => e.status === 'ASSIGNED').length;

      return res.json({
        summary: {
          total_owned_equipment: totalCount,
          active_rentals: assignedCount,
          total_rental_cost_mtd: 0,
          underutilized_count: 0,
        },
        rental_vs_owned: [],
        frequently_rented: [],
        underutilized: [],
        project_equipment_costs: [],
        last_updated: null,
      });
    }

    const snapshotData = safeJsonParse(latestRun.snapshotData, null);
    const equipment = snapshotData.equipment || {};
    const roiAnalysis = equipment.roi_analysis || {};

    const fleetSize = equipment.fleet?.length || 0;
    const activeRentals = equipment.active_rentals?.length || 0;
    const rentalHistory = equipment.rental_usage_history || [];
    
    const now = new Date();
    const monthStart = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1);
    const mtdRentals = rentalHistory.filter((r: any) => {
      const startDate = r.actual_start_date ? new Date(r.actual_start_date) : null;
      return startDate && startDate >= monthStart;
    });
    const totalRentalCostMtd = mtdRentals.reduce((sum: number, r: any) => 
      sum + (r.actual_total_cost || r.quoted_total_cost || 0), 0
    );

    const underutilizedCount = roiAnalysis.underutilized_owned?.length || 0;

    const projectsWithEquipment = equipment.projects_with_equipment || [];
    const projectCosts = projectsWithEquipment.map((p: any) => {
      const planned = Number(p.planned_cost) || 0;
      const actual = Number(p.actual_cost) || 0;
      const total = actual > 0 ? actual : planned;
      return {
        project_code: p.project_code,
        project_name: p.project_name,
        planned_cost: planned,
        actual_cost: actual,
        total_cost: total,
      };
    });

    projectCosts.sort((a: any, b: any) => b.total_cost - a.total_cost);

    res.json({
      summary: {
        total_owned_equipment: fleetSize,
        active_rentals: activeRentals,
        total_rental_cost_mtd: Math.round(totalRentalCostMtd * 100) / 100,
        underutilized_count: underutilizedCount,
      },
      rental_vs_owned: roiAnalysis.rental_vs_owned_comparison || [],
      frequently_rented: roiAnalysis.frequently_rented_equipment || [],
      underutilized: roiAnalysis.underutilized_owned || [],
      project_equipment_costs: projectCosts.slice(0, 10),
      last_updated: latestRun.createdAt,
    });
  } catch (error) {
  next(error);
  }
});

router.get("/cfo/forecasting", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    const orgId = req.user?.organizationId;
    if (!orgId) return res.status(403).json({ error: "Organization required" });

    const { calculateAllProjectsProfitability } = await import("./ai/profitabilityEngine");
    const { projects } = await import("@shared/schema");
    const allProjects = await db.select({ id: projects.id, code: projects.code, gcOrganizationId: projects.gcOrganizationId }).from(projects).where(eq(projects.gcOrganizationId, orgId));
    const allProfitability = await calculateAllProjectsProfitability();
    const orgProjectCodes = new Set(allProjects.map(p => p.code));
    const profitabilityData = allProfitability.filter(p => orgProjectCodes.has(p.project_code));

    const portfolioFFC = profitabilityData.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.forecast_final_cost, 0);
    const portfolioBudget = profitabilityData.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.adjusted_budget, 0);
    const portfolioCommitted = profitabilityData.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.committed_cost, 0);
    const portfolioActual = profitabilityData.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.total_actual_cost, 0);
    const portfolioVariance = portfolioBudget - portfolioFFC;

    const projectsByOverrun = [...profitabilityData]
      .map(p => ({
        project_code: p.project_code,
        project_name: p.project_name,
        budget: p.adjusted_budget,
        committed: p.committed_cost,
        actual: p.total_actual_cost,
        ffc: p.forecast_final_cost,
        variance: p.adjusted_budget - p.forecast_final_cost,
        variance_percent: p.adjusted_budget > 0 ? ((p.adjusted_budget - p.forecast_final_cost) / p.adjusted_budget) * 100 : 0,
        status: p.status,
        cost_prediction: p.cost_prediction,
      }))
      .sort((a, b) => a.variance - b.variance);

    const { budgetAlerts } = await import("@shared/schema");
    const activeAlerts = await db.select().from(budgetAlerts).where(eq(budgetAlerts.isDismissed, false)).limit(200);

    const projectIdMap = new Map<string, typeof profitabilityData[0]>();
    for (const p of profitabilityData) {
      const projRow = allProjects.find(pr => pr.code === p.project_code);
      if (projRow) projectIdMap.set(projRow.id, p);
    }

    const alertsWithProject = activeAlerts
      .filter(a => {
        const proj = projectIdMap.get(a.projectId);
        return proj !== undefined;
      })
      .map(a => {
        const proj = projectIdMap.get(a.projectId);
        return {
          id: a.id,
          projectId: a.projectId,
          projectCode: proj?.project_code || "",
          projectName: proj?.project_name || "",
          costCode: a.costCode,
          alertLevel: a.alertLevel,
          thresholdPercent: Number(a.thresholdPercent),
          currentPercent: Number(a.currentPercent),
          budgetAmount: Number(a.budgetAmount),
          actualAmount: Number(a.actualAmount),
          createdAt: a.createdAt,
        };
      });

    const earlyWarnings = profitabilityData
      .filter(p => p.cost_prediction.early_warning || p.cost_prediction.burn_rate_trend === 'INCREASING')
      .map(p => ({
        project_code: p.project_code,
        project_name: p.project_name,
        warning_message: p.cost_prediction.warning_message,
        predicted_overrun: p.cost_prediction.predicted_overrun,
        confidence_score: p.cost_prediction.confidence_score,
        weeks_until_overrun: p.cost_prediction.weeks_until_overrun,
        burn_rate_trend: p.cost_prediction.burn_rate_trend,
        weekly_burn_rate: p.cost_prediction.weekly_burn_rate,
      }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.predicted_overrun - a.predicted_overrun);

    res.json({
      summary: {
        portfolio_budget: Math.round(portfolioBudget * 100) / 100,
        portfolio_ffc: Math.round(portfolioFFC * 100) / 100,
        portfolio_committed: Math.round(portfolioCommitted * 100) / 100,
        portfolio_actual: Math.round(portfolioActual * 100) / 100,
        portfolio_variance: Math.round(portfolioVariance * 100) / 100,
        project_count: profitabilityData.length,
        at_risk_count: profitabilityData.filter(p => p.status === 'MAJOR_RISK').length,
        early_warning_count: earlyWarnings.length,
      },
      projects: projectsByOverrun,
      alerts: alertsWithProject,
      early_warnings: earlyWarnings,
    });
  } catch (error) {
  next(error);
  }
});

router.get("/cfo/scurve-data", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    const orgId = req.user?.organizationId;
    if (!orgId) return res.status(403).json({ error: "Organization required" });

    const { projects: projectsTable, timeEntries, receipts, subcontractorPayments, subcontractorContracts, budgetLineItems } = await import("@shared/schema");
    const { inArray } = await import("drizzle-orm");

    const orgProjects = await db.select({
      id: projectsTable.id,
      budget: projectsTable.budget,
      contractAmount: projectsTable.contractAmount,
      startDate: projectsTable.startDate,
      endDate: projectsTable.endDate,
    }).from(projectsTable).where(and(
      eq(projectsTable.gcOrganizationId, orgId),
      sql`${projectsTable.status} IN ('ACTIVE', 'PLANNING')`
    )).limit(100);

    if (orgProjects.length === 0) return res.json({ months: [] });

    const projectIds = orgProjects.map(p => p.id);

    const totalBudget = orgProjects.reduce((sum, p) => sum + Number(p.contractAmount || p.budget || 0), 0);

    let earliestStart: Date | null = null;
    let latestEnd: Date | null = null;
    for (const p of orgProjects) {
      if (p.startDate) {
        const s = new Date(p.startDate);
        if (!earliestStart || s < earliestStart) earliestStart = s;
      }
      if (p.endDate) {
        const e = new Date(p.endDate);
        if (!latestEnd || e > latestEnd) latestEnd = e;
      }
    }

    if (!earliestStart) earliestStart = new Date(Date.now() - 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    if (!latestEnd) latestEnd = new Date(Date.now() + 180 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

    const orgDefaultRateResult = await db.execute(sql`
      SELECT COALESCE(AVG(NULLIF(jc.default_regular_rate, 0)), 0) as avg_regular,
             COALESCE(AVG(NULLIF(jc.default_overtime_rate, 0)), 0) as avg_overtime
      FROM job_classifications jc
      WHERE jc.organization_id = ${orgId} AND jc.is_active = true
    `);
    const orgDefaultRows = (orgDefaultRateResult.rows || orgDefaultRateResult) as { avg_regular: number; avg_overtime: number }[];
    const orgDefaultRegularRate = Number(orgDefaultRows[0]?.avg_regular) || 0;
    const orgDefaultOvertimeRate = Number(orgDefaultRows[0]?.avg_overtime) || 0;
    const fallbackRegularRate = orgDefaultRegularRate > 0 ? orgDefaultRegularRate : 45;
    const fallbackOvertimeRate = orgDefaultOvertimeRate > 0 ? orgDefaultOvertimeRate : (fallbackRegularRate * 1.5);

    const laborResult = await db.execute(sql`
      SELECT TO_CHAR(te.date, 'YYYY-MM') as month,
        COALESCE(SUM(
          COALESCE(te.hours_regular, 0) * COALESCE(ec.regular_rate, jc.default_regular_rate, ${fallbackRegularRate})
          + COALESCE(te.hours_overtime, 0) * COALESCE(ec.overtime_rate, jc.default_overtime_rate, ${fallbackOvertimeRate})
        ), 0) as total
      FROM time_entries te
      LEFT JOIN employee_classifications ec
        ON ec.user_id = te.user_id AND ec.organization_id = ${orgId} AND ec.is_primary = true AND (ec.end_date IS NULL OR ec.end_date >= NOW())
      LEFT JOIN job_classifications jc
        ON jc.id = ec.classification_id AND jc.organization_id = ${orgId}
      WHERE te.project_id IN (${sql.join(projectIds.map(id => sql`${id}`), sql`, `)})
        AND te.approval_status = 'APPROVED'
      GROUP BY TO_CHAR(te.date, 'YYYY-MM')
      ORDER BY TO_CHAR(te.date, 'YYYY-MM')
    `);
    const laborByMonth = (laborResult.rows || laborResult) as { month: string; total: number }[];

    const receiptsByMonth = await db.select({
      month: sql<string>`TO_CHAR(${receipts.date}, 'YYYY-MM')`.as("month"),
      total: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${receipts.amount}), 0)`.as("total"),
    }).from(receipts)
      .where(inArray(receipts.projectId, projectIds))
      .groupBy(sql`TO_CHAR(${receipts.date}, 'YYYY-MM')`)
      .orderBy(sql`TO_CHAR(${receipts.date}, 'YYYY-MM')`);

    const contractIds = await db.select({ id: subcontractorContracts.id })
      .from(subcontractorContracts)
      .where(inArray(subcontractorContracts.projectId, projectIds));
    
    let subPaymentsByMonth: { month: string; total: number }[] = [];
    if (contractIds.length > 0) {
      subPaymentsByMonth = await db.select({
        month: sql<string>`TO_CHAR(${subcontractorPayments.paymentDate}, 'YYYY-MM')`.as("month"),
        total: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${subcontractorPayments.amount}), 0)`.as("total"),
      }).from(subcontractorPayments)
        .where(inArray(subcontractorPayments.contractId, contractIds.map(c => c.id)))
        .groupBy(sql`TO_CHAR(${subcontractorPayments.paymentDate}, 'YYYY-MM')`)
        .orderBy(sql`TO_CHAR(${subcontractorPayments.paymentDate}, 'YYYY-MM')`);
    }

    const actualByMonth = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const row of laborByMonth) { actualByMonth.set(row.month, (actualByMonth.get(row.month) || 0) + Number(row.total)); }
    for (const row of receiptsByMonth) { actualByMonth.set(row.month, (actualByMonth.get(row.month) || 0) + Number(row.total)); }
    for (const row of subPaymentsByMonth) { actualByMonth.set(row.month, (actualByMonth.get(row.month) || 0) + Number(row.total)); }

    const startMs = earliestStart.getTime();
    const endMs = latestEnd.getTime();
    const totalDuration = endMs - startMs;

    const allMonths: string[] = [];
    const cursor = new Date(earliestStart);
    cursor.setDate(1);
    const endCursor = new Date(Math.max(endMs, Date.now() + 90 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
    while (cursor <= endCursor) {
      allMonths.push(cursor.toISOString().slice(0, 7));
      cursor.setMonth(cursor.getMonth() + 1);
    }

    let cumulativeActual = 0;
    const result = allMonths.map(month => {
      const monthDate = new Date(month + "-15");
      const t = totalDuration > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (monthDate.getTime() - startMs) / totalDuration)) : 0;
      const sCurve = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-10 * (t - 0.5)));
      const minS = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(5));
      const maxS = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-5));
      const norm = (sCurve - minS) / (maxS - minS);
      const plannedCumulative = Math.round(totalBudget * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, norm)));

      const monthActual = actualByMonth.get(month) || 0;
      cumulativeActual += monthActual;

      const isFuture = monthDate > new Date();

      return {
        month: new Date(month + "-01").toLocaleDateString("en-US", { month: "short", year: "2-digit" }),
        planned: plannedCumulative,
        actual: !isFuture ? Math.round(cumulativeActual) : null,
        forecast: isFuture ? Math.round(cumulativeActual) : null,
      };
    });

    res.json({ months: result });
  } catch (error) {
  next(error);
  }
});

----- FILE: client/src/pages/executive-digest.tsx (full) -----
import { useState } from "react";
import { useQuery, useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/use-auth";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import { ScrollArea } from "@/components/ui/scroll-area";
import { 
  FileText, RefreshCw, Mail, Download, Calendar, Clock,
  TrendingUp, TrendingDown, AlertTriangle, CheckCircle, 
  Lightbulb, Target, DollarSign, Users
} from "lucide-react";
import { apiRequest, queryClient } from "@/lib/queryClient";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";

interface DigestSection {
  title: string;
  icon: 'financial' | 'operational' | 'risk' | 'opportunity';
  summary: string;
  bullets: string[];
  sentiment: 'positive' | 'neutral' | 'negative';
}

interface ExecutiveDigest {
  id: string;
  generatedAt: string;
  period: 'daily' | 'weekly' | 'monthly';
  executiveSummary: string;
  keyHighlights: string[];
  sections: DigestSection[];
  actionItems: {
    priority: 'high' | 'medium' | 'low';
    description: string;
    dueBy?: string;
  }[];
  kpiSnapshot: {
    name: string;
    current: number;
    previous: number;
    unit: string;
    trend: 'up' | 'down' | 'stable';
  }[];
}

function SectionIcon({ icon }: { icon: DigestSection['icon'] }) {
  switch (icon) {
    case 'financial':
      return <DollarSign className="h-5 w-5 text-green-500" />;
    case 'operational':
      return <Target className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-500" />;
    case 'risk':
      return <AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5 text-red-500" />;
    case 'opportunity':
      return <Lightbulb className="h-5 w-5 text-yellow-500" />;
    default:
      return <FileText className="h-5 w-5" />;
  }
}

function DigestSectionCard({ section }: { section: DigestSection }) {
  const sentimentColors = {
    positive: 'border-l-green-500',
    neutral: 'border-l-blue-500',
    negative: 'border-l-red-500'
  };

  return (
    <Card className={`border-l-4 ${sentimentColors[section.sentiment]}`}>
      <CardHeader className="pb-2">
        <CardTitle className="text-base flex items-center gap-2">
          <SectionIcon icon={section.icon} />
          {section.title}
        </CardTitle>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-2">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{section.summary}</p>
        <ul className="space-y-1">
          {section.bullets.map((bullet, i) => (
            <li key={i} className="text-sm flex items-start gap-2">
              <span className="text-primary mt-1">•</span>
              {bullet}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

export default function ExecutiveDigestPage() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [selectedPeriod, setSelectedPeriod] = useState<'daily' | 'weekly' | 'monthly'>('daily');

  const { data: digest, isLoading, refetch } = useQuery<ExecutiveDigest>({
    queryKey: ["/api/executive/digest", selectedPeriod],
    enabled: !!user && (user.role === 'ADMIN' || user.role === 'MANAGER' || user.role === 'HUSTLER'),
  });

  const generateMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      return apiRequest('POST', `/api/executive/digest/generate`, { period: selectedPeriod });
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      toast({ title: "Digest generated successfully" });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/executive/digest"], exact: false });
    },
    onError: () => {
      toast({ title: "Failed to generate digest", variant: "destructive" });
    }
  });

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-background p-8 text-center">
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Please log in to view executive digest</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (user.role !== 'ADMIN' && user.role !== 'MANAGER' && user.role !== 'HUSTLER') {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-background p-8 text-center">
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Executive access required</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2" data-testid="text-page-title">
            <FileText className="h-6 w-6" />
            Executive Digest
          </h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">AI-generated business intelligence summary</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
          <Tabs value={selectedPeriod} onValueChange={(v) => setSelectedPeriod(v as typeof selectedPeriod)}>
            <TabsList>
              <TabsTrigger value="daily" data-testid="tab-daily">Daily</TabsTrigger>
              <TabsTrigger value="weekly" data-testid="tab-weekly">Weekly</TabsTrigger>
              <TabsTrigger value="monthly" data-testid="tab-monthly">Monthly</TabsTrigger>
            </TabsList>
          </Tabs>
          <Button 
            variant="outline" 
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => generateMutation.mutate()}
            disabled={generateMutation.isPending}
            data-testid="button-generate-digest"
          >
            <RefreshCw className={`h-4 w-4 mr-2 ${generateMutation.isPending ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
            Generate New
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <Skeleton className="h-32" />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <Skeleton className="h-48" />
            <Skeleton className="h-48" />
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : !digest ? (
        <Card className="border-dashed">
          <CardContent className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-center">
            <FileText className="h-12 w-12 text-muted-foreground/50 mb-4" />
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-2" data-testid="text-no-digest">No digest available for this period</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground max-w-md mb-4">
              Click "Generate New" to create an AI-powered executive digest for the selected {selectedPeriod} period.
            </p>
            <Button 
              variant="default" 
              onClick={() => generateMutation.mutate()}
              disabled={generateMutation.isPending}
              data-testid="button-generate-first-digest"
            >
              <RefreshCw className={`h-4 w-4 mr-2 ${generateMutation.isPending ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
              Generate Digest
            </Button>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <>
          <Card className="bg-gradient-to-r from-primary/10 to-primary/5">
            <CardHeader>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <CardTitle className="text-lg">Executive Summary</CardTitle>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                  <Clock className="h-4 w-4" />
                  {new Date(digest.generatedAt).toLocaleString()}
                </div>
              </div>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed">{digest.executiveSummary}</p>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                {digest.keyHighlights.map((highlight, i) => (
                  <Badge key={i} variant="secondary" className="text-xs">
                    <CheckCircle className="h-3 w-3 mr-1" />
                    {highlight}
                  </Badge>
                ))}
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-4">
            {digest.kpiSnapshot.map((kpi, i) => (
              <Card key={i}>
                <CardContent className="pt-4 text-center">
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1">{kpi.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-xl font-bold">
                    {kpi.unit === '$' ? `$${(kpi.current / 1000).toFixed(0)}K` : `${kpi.current}${kpi.unit}`}
                  </p>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 mt-1">
                    {kpi.trend === 'up' && <TrendingUp className="h-3 w-3 text-green-500" />}
                    {kpi.trend === 'down' && <TrendingDown className="h-3 w-3 text-red-500" />}
                    <span className={`text-xs ${kpi.trend === 'up' ? 'text-green-500' : kpi.trend === 'down' ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-muted-foreground'}`}>
                      vs {kpi.unit === '$' ? `$${(kpi.previous / 1000).toFixed(0)}K` : `${kpi.previous}${kpi.unit}`}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-4">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Detailed Analysis</h2>
              {digest.sections.map((section, i) => (
                <DigestSectionCard key={i} section={section} />
              ))}
            </div>

            <div>
              <Card>
                <CardHeader>
                  <CardTitle className="text-base flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Target className="h-4 w-4" />
                    Action Items
                  </CardTitle>
                  <CardDescription>Items requiring your attention</CardDescription>
                </CardHeader>
                <CardContent>
                  <ScrollArea className="h-[400px]">
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                      {digest.actionItems.map((item, i) => (
                        <div 
                          key={i} 
                          className="p-3 rounded-lg border bg-card"
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                            <div className="flex-1">
                              <p className="text-sm">{item.description}</p>
                              {item.dueBy && (
                                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                                  <Calendar className="h-3 w-3" />
                                  Due: {item.dueBy}
                                </p>
                              )}
                            </div>
                            <Badge 
                              variant={
                                item.priority === 'high' ? 'destructive' : 
                                item.priority === 'medium' ? 'secondary' : 'outline'
                              }
                              className="text-xs"
                            >
                              {item.priority}
                            </Badge>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </ScrollArea>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
