import { useQuery, useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/use-auth";
import { useFeaturePermission } from "@/hooks/use-dashboard-customization";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { DollarSign, Sparkles, RefreshCw, Download, PieChart, ShieldAlert, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";
import { AIAnalysisButton } from "@/components/AIAnalysisButton";
import { apiRequest, queryClient } from "@/lib/queryClient";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { FinancialKPICards } from "@/components/cfo/FinancialKPICards";
import { AccountsReceivableSummary } from "@/components/cfo/AccountsReceivableSummary";
import { ProjectProfitabilityTable } from "@/components/cfo/ProjectProfitabilityTable";
import { EquipmentMetrics } from "@/components/cfo/EquipmentMetrics";
import { CostForecastingSection } from "@/components/cfo/CostForecastingSection";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { useFeatureFlags } from "@/hooks/use-feature-flags";
import type {
  ProjectProfitability,
  EquipmentMetricsData,
} from "@/components/cfo/types";

const formatCurrency = (amount: number) => {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "USD",
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(amount);
};

export default function CFODashboard() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const hasFinancialsPermission = useFeaturePermission('canViewFinancials');
  const hasAiPermission = useFeaturePermission('canUseAiFeatures');

  const { isEnabled } = useFeatureFlags();
  const whatIfEnabled = isEnabled("WHATIF_SIMULATOR");

  const canAccessCFO = !!user && (
    user.role === "ADMIN" || user.role === "MANAGER" ||
    hasFinancialsPermission || hasAiPermission
  );

  const { data: profitability, isLoading } = useQuery<ProjectProfitability[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/cfo/profitability"],
    enabled: canAccessCFO,
  });

  const { data: equipmentMetrics, isLoading: equipmentLoading } =
    useQuery<EquipmentMetricsData>({
      queryKey: ["/api/cfo/equipment-metrics"],
      enabled: canAccessCFO,
    });

  const runFullAnalysis = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      const response = await apiRequest("POST", "/api/ai-pm/run");
      return response.json();
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      toast({
        title: "Full CFO Analysis Complete",
        description:
          "Profitability data and equipment metrics have been updated with AI insights.",
      });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/cfo/profitability"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({
        queryKey: ["/api/cfo/equipment-metrics"],
      });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/ai-insights"] });
    },
    onError: (error: any) => {
      toast({
        title: "Analysis Failed",
        description:
          error.message || "Failed to run full CFO analysis. Please try again.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    },
  });

  const handleProjectClick = (projectCode: string) => {
    setLocation(`/projects/${projectCode}?from=cfo`);
  };

  const handleEquipmentClick = () => {
    setLocation("/equipment");
  };

  const exportToCSV = () => {
    if (!profitability || profitability.length === 0) {
      toast({
        title: "No Data",
        description: "No profitability data to export.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }
    const headers = [
      "Project Code",
      "Project Name",
      "Budget",
      "Adjusted Budget",
      "Labor Cost",
      "Material Cost",
      "Other Expenses",
      "Approved COs",
      "Total Actual Cost",
      "Variance",
      "Variance %",
      "Status",
      "Projected Completion Cost",
      "Estimated Margin",
      "Labor Hours",
      "Labor Efficiency",
      "Material Spend Rate",
      "Pending COs Value",
    ];
    const rows = profitability.map((p) => [
      p.project_code,
      `"${p.project_name}"`,
      p.budget,
      p.adjusted_budget,
      p.total_labor_cost,
      p.total_material_cost,
      p.total_other_expenses,
      p.approved_change_orders_value,
      p.total_actual_cost,
      p.variance,
      `${p.variance_percent.toFixed(1)}%`,
      p.status,
      p.projected_completion_cost,
      p.estimated_margin,
      p.key_metrics.labor_hours,
      p.key_metrics.labor_efficiency,
      p.key_metrics.material_spend_rate,
      p.key_metrics.pending_change_orders_value,
    ]);
    const csvContent = [
      headers.join(","),
      ...rows.map((r) => r.join(",")),
    ].join("\n");
    const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement("a");
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `cfo-dashboard-${new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0]}.csv`;
    link.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    toast({
      title: "Export Complete",
      description: `Exported ${profitability.length} projects to CSV.`,
    });
  };

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-background">
        <main className="p-8 text-center">
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">
            Please log in to view profitability data
          </p>
        </main>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!canAccessCFO) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-background">
        <main className="p-8 text-center">
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">
            You don't have permission to access the CFO Dashboard
          </p>
        </main>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-background overflow-x-hidden">
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-full px-4 sm:px-6 py-6 sm:py-8">
        <div className="mb-6 sm:mb-8 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
              <DollarSign className="h-6 w-6 sm:h-8 sm:w-8 text-primary" />
              CFO Dashboard
            </h1>
            <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-muted-foreground mt-1">
              Real-time profitability analysis across all projects
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <Button
              variant="default"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => runFullAnalysis.mutate()}
              disabled={runFullAnalysis.isPending}
              data-testid="cfo-run-full-analysis"
            >
              {runFullAnalysis.isPending ? (
                <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin mr-2" />
              ) : (
                <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              )}
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">
                {runFullAnalysis.isPending
                  ? "Running..."
                  : "Run Full CFO Analysis"}
              </span>
              <span className="sm:hidden">
                {runFullAnalysis.isPending ? "Running..." : "CFO Analysis"}
              </span>
            </Button>
            <AIAnalysisButton showDropdown={true} variant="outline" size="sm" />
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => setLocation("/wip-report")}
              data-testid="cfo-wip-report"
            >
              <PieChart className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">WIP Report</span>
              <span className="sm:hidden">WIP</span>
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              onClick={exportToCSV}
              disabled={!profitability || profitability.length === 0}
              data-testid="cfo-export-csv"
            >
              <Download className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">Export CSV</span>
              <span className="sm:hidden">Export</span>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid gap-4 grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4">
              {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
                <Skeleton key={i} className="h-32 w-full" />
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <FinancialKPICards
              profitability={profitability || []}
              formatCurrency={formatCurrency}
              onProjectClick={handleProjectClick}
            />

            {whatIfEnabled && (
              <CostForecastingSection
                formatCurrency={formatCurrency}
                onProjectClick={handleProjectClick}
              />
            )}

            <AccountsReceivableSummary formatCurrency={formatCurrency} />

            <PortfolioRiskSummary formatCurrency={formatCurrency} />

            <EquipmentMetrics
              equipmentMetrics={equipmentMetrics}
              isLoading={equipmentLoading}
              formatCurrency={formatCurrency}
              onEquipmentClick={handleEquipmentClick}
            />

            <ProjectProfitabilityTable
              profitability={profitability}
              formatCurrency={formatCurrency}
              onProjectClick={handleProjectClick}
              onRunAnalysis={() => runFullAnalysis.mutate()}
              isAnalysisPending={runFullAnalysis.isPending}
            />
          </>
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

interface PortfolioRiskSummaryProps {
  formatCurrency: (amount: number) => string;
}

function PortfolioRiskSummary({ formatCurrency }: PortfolioRiskSummaryProps) {
  const { data, isLoading } = useQuery<{
    totalOpenRisks: number;
    totalHighRisks: number;
    totalOccurredRisks: number;
    totalCostExposure: number;
    totalProjects: number;
    trend: { newThisPeriod: number; resolvedThisPeriod: number; direction: string };
    highRiskProjects: Array<{
      projectId: string;
      projectName: string;
      open: number;
      high: number;
      occurred: number;
      costExposure: number;
    }>;
  }>({
    queryKey: ["/api/risks/portfolio-summary"],
  });

  if (isLoading) {
    return <Skeleton className="h-48 w-full mt-6" />;
  }

  if (!data || (data.totalOpenRisks === 0 && data.totalOccurredRisks === 0)) return null;

  return (
    <Card className="mt-6" data-testid="card-portfolio-risk-summary">
      <CardHeader>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
          <CardTitle>Portfolio Risk Summary</CardTitle>
        </div>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-4">
          <div className="p-3 rounded-lg border bg-card text-center">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Open Risks</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold" data-testid="text-portfolio-open-risks">{data.totalOpenRisks}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 rounded-lg border bg-card text-center">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">High/Critical</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-red-600 dark:text-red-400" data-testid="text-portfolio-high-risks">{data.totalHighRisks}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 rounded-lg border bg-card text-center">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Occurred</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400" data-testid="text-portfolio-occurred-risks">{data.totalOccurredRisks}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 rounded-lg border bg-card text-center">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Cost Exposure</p>
            <p className="text-lg font-bold font-mono" data-testid="text-portfolio-cost-exposure">{formatCurrency(data.totalCostExposure)}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {data.trend && (data.trend.newThisPeriod > 0 || data.trend.resolvedThisPeriod > 0) && (
          <div className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-muted/50 text-sm text-muted-foreground mb-4" data-testid="text-portfolio-risk-trend">
            30-day trend: {data.trend.newThisPeriod} new, {data.trend.resolvedThisPeriod} resolved
            {data.trend.direction === "improving" && " — improving"}
            {data.trend.direction === "worsening" && " — worsening"}
          </div>
        )}
        {data.highRiskProjects.length > 0 && (
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-medium mb-2 flex items-center gap-1">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
              High-Risk Projects
            </p>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {data.highRiskProjects.map((proj) => (
                <div key={proj.projectId} className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between p-2 rounded border text-sm gap-1 sm:gap-2" data-testid={`row-high-risk-project-${proj.projectId}`}>
                  <span className="font-medium truncate">{proj.projectName}</span>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                    {proj.high > 0 && <Badge variant="destructive">{proj.high} high</Badge>}
                    {proj.occurred > 0 && <Badge variant="outline" className="text-amber-600 border-amber-300">{proj.occurred} occurred</Badge>}
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground font-mono">{formatCurrency(proj.costExposure)}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

const STATUS_OPTIONS = ["PLANNING", "ACTIVE", "COMPLETE", "CLOSED", "BID"];

function useUrlState() {
  const [location, setLocation] = useLocation();
  const search = useSearch();
  const params = new URLSearchParams(search);
  const get = (k: string) => params.get(k);
  const set = (updates: Record<string, string | null>) => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(search);
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(updates)) {
      if (v === null || v === "") next.delete(k);
      else next.set(k, v);
    }
    const qs = next.toString();
    setLocation(qs ? `${location}?${qs}` : location);
  };
  return { get, set };
}

function fmtCurrency(n: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "USD",
    maximumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(n || 0);
}

function healthBgColor(h: number) {
  if (h >= 80)
    return "bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300";
  if (h >= 60)
    return "bg-amber-500/15 border-amber-500/40 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300";
  return "bg-red-500/15 border-red-500/40 text-red-700 dark:text-red-300";
}

function formatDeltaNumber(n: number): string {
  const abs = Math.abs(n);
  const fixed = abs >= 100 ? abs.toFixed(0) : abs.toFixed(abs >= 10 ? 1 : 2);
  const trimmed = fixed.replace(/\.?0+$/, "");
  return `${n > 0 ? "+" : n < 0 ? "−" : ""}${trimmed || "0"}`;
}

function DeltaBadge({
  value,
  label,
  inverse = false,
  testId,
}: {
  value: number | null | undefined;
  label?: string;
  inverse?: boolean;
  testId?: string;
}) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || Number.isNaN(value)) {
    return (
      <span
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground"
        title={
          label ? `${label}: insufficient history` : "Insufficient history"
        }

function getTimeGreeting(): { greeting: string; icon: typeof Sun } {
  const hour = new Date().getHours();
  if (hour < 12) return { greeting: "Good morning", icon: Sun };
  if (hour < 17) return { greeting: "Good afternoon", icon: Sun };
  if (hour < 21) return { greeting: "Good evening", icon: Sunset };
  return { greeting: "Good night", icon: Moon };
}

function useRoleSubtitle(role?: string): string {
  const { data: stats } = useDashboardStats();
  const { data: bids } = useBids();

  const projects = stats?.activeProjectsCount ?? 0;
  const activeBids = bids?.length ?? 0;
  const delays = stats?.activeDelaysCount ?? 0;

  switch (role) {
    case 'ADMIN':
    case 'SUPER_ADMIN': {
      const parts: string[] = [];
      if (projects > 0) parts.push(`${projects} active project${projects !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`);
      if (delays > 0) parts.push(`${delays} delay${delays !== 1 ? 's' : ''} to review`);
      return parts.length > 0 ? parts.join(' · ') : "Here's your company overview";
    }
    case 'HUSTLER': {
      const parts: string[] = [];
      if (projects > 0) parts.push(`${projects} project${projects !== 1 ? 's' : ''} in portfolio`);
      if (activeBids > 0) parts.push(`${activeBids} bid${activeBids !== 1 ? 's' : ''} in pipeline`);
      return parts.length > 0 ? parts.join(' · ') : "Your portfolio at a glance";
    }
    case 'MANAGER': {
      const parts: string[] = [];
      if (projects > 0) parts.push(`${projects} active project${projects !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`);
      if (delays > 0) parts.push(`${delays} delay${delays !== 1 ? 's' : ''} flagged`);
      return parts.length > 0 ? parts.join(' · ') : "Your projects and team status";
    }
    case 'SUPERINTENDENT':
      return delays > 0 ? `${delays} delay${delays !== 1 ? 's' : ''} on your jobsites` : "Today's crew and jobsite status";
    case 'ESTIMATOR':
      return activeBids > 0 ? `${activeBids} active bid${activeBids !== 1 ? 's' : ''} in pipeline` : "Your bids and pipeline";
    case 'FIELD_WORKER':
      return projects > 0 ? `Assigned to ${projects} project${projects !== 1 ? 's' : ''}` : "Your tasks and schedule";
    case 'VENDOR':
      return "Your messages and updates";
    default:
      return "Here's what's happening today";
  }
}

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Skeleton className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full" />
                <Skeleton className="h-4 w-32" />
              </div>
              <Skeleton className="h-5 w-8 rounded-full" />
            </div>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="pt-0 px-2 pb-2">
            <div className={`grid ${sectionIdx === 1 ? 'grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4' : 'grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3'} gap-3`}>
              {Array.from({ length: sectionIdx === 1 ? 4 : 3 }).map((_, i) => (
                <div key={i} className="rounded-lg border p-4 space-y-3 relative overflow-hidden">
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 -translate-x-full animate-[shimmer_2s_infinite] bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-foreground/5 to-transparent" />
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <Skeleton className="h-4 w-24" />

                  </div>
                  <Skeleton className="h-8 w-16" />
                  <Skeleton className="h-3 w-full" />
                  <Skeleton className="h-3 w-2/3" />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function DashboardStarRating({ value, onChange }: { value: number; onChange: (v: number) => void }) {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState(0);
  return (
    <div className="flex gap-0.5" data-testid="star-rating">
      {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((star) => (
        <button
          key={star}
          type="button"
          className="cursor-pointer hover:scale-110 transition-transform"
          onMouseEnter={() => setHover(star)}
          onMouseLeave={() => setHover(0)}
          onClick={() => onChange(star)}
          data-testid={`star-${star}`}
        >
          <Star
            className={`w-5 h-5 ${
              star <= (hover || value)
                ? "fill-yellow-400 text-yellow-400"
                : "text-muted-foreground/30"
            } transition-colors`}
          />
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

GAPS (Target 2):
1. Executive digest has NO scheduled delivery (no cron job calls
   the digest builder; no SendGrid template fires on schedule).
2. cfo-dashboard.tsx and executive-digest.tsx are display-only
   pages; they fetch via TanStack Query from
   /api/ai-cfo-analytics/* and /api/executive-digest/*. Whether the
   underlying data is refreshed on a schedule is gated by the KPI
   snapshot scheduler (see Target 1) which is not currently
   wired to cron.
3. Portfolio page (portfolio.tsx, 1136 lines) IS wired to live
   project profitability data via server/portfolioRoutes.ts.
4. There is no role-gated "Owner" dashboard distinct from CFO/Exec;
   /executive-digest and /cfo-dashboard both render to permissioned
   users — owner-specific filtering is implicit via organizationId.

  server/integrations/syncRetry.ts handle retries.

----- FILE: planning/QBO_SYNC_SPEC.md (full) -----
# QuickBooks Online Sync Specification

## Overview

Functional baseline integration with QuickBooks Online for financial data synchronization.

## OAuth 2.0 Flow

1. Admin initiates connection from Integrations page
2. Redirect to Intuit OAuth authorization URL with scopes: `com.intuit.quickbooks.accounting`
3. User authorizes access
4. Callback receives auth code, exchanges for access + refresh tokens
5. Tokens stored encrypted in integration_connections metadata
6. Refresh tokens automatically before expiry (1hr access, 100-day refresh)

## Sync Operations

### 1. Import Chart of Accounts (Read)
- Pull all accounts from QBO
- Store mapping in integration_sync_state
- Display in UI for mapping configuration
- Used for invoice line item mapping

### 2. Export Customers (Create/Update)
- Map CRM organizations (type: CONTRACTOR, OWNER_REP) to QBO Customers
- Create new customers if no mapping exists
- Update existing customers on subsequent syncs
- Store QBO Customer ID in connection metadata

### 3. Export Invoices from SOV/Pay Apps (Create)
- When SOV draw request is approved, create Invoice in QBO
- Map SOV line items to QBO invoice lines
- Apply account mapping from Chart of Accounts
- Store QBO Invoice ID for status tracking
- Approval required before export (when APPROVALS_FRAMEWORK is ON)

### 4. Import Payment Status (Read)
- Poll QBO for payment status on exported invoices
- Update internal invoice/SOV status (paid/partial/overdue)
- Log changes in audit trail

## Sync Modes

- **Dry Run**: Shows what would change without making API calls
- **Manual Sync**: Admin triggers sync from UI
- **Scheduled Sync**: Background job runs every 4 hours (configurable)

## Entity Mapping

| Internal Entity | QBO Entity | Direction |
|----------------|------------|-----------|
| CRM Organization | Customer | Export |
| SOV Draw Request | Invoice | Export |
| Payment (tracked) | Payment | Import |
| Chart of Accounts | Account | Import |

## Error Handling

- Rate limiting: respect QBO throttle headers (500 requests/min)
- Conflict detection: compare QBO `SyncToken` before updates
- Failed syncs logged with full error context
- Admin notification on repeated failures

## Environment Variables Required

- `QBO_CLIENT_ID` — Intuit app client ID
- `QBO_CLIENT_SECRET` — Intuit app client secret
- `QBO_REDIRECT_URI` — OAuth callback URL
- `QBO_ENVIRONMENT` — sandbox/production

----- FILE: server/integrations/providers/qbo/provider.ts (full) -----
import type { IntegrationProvider, TokenSet } from "../../types";
import type { IntegrationConnection } from "@shared/schema";
import { logInfo, logError } from "../../../utils/logger";
import { OAuthPermanentError } from "../oauthErrors";

const QBO_AUTH_BASE = "https://appcenter.intuit.com/connect/oauth2";
const QBO_TOKEN_URL = "https://oauth.platform.intuit.com/oauth2/v1/tokens/bearer";

export const qboProvider: IntegrationProvider = {
  key: "qbo",
  displayName: "QuickBooks Online",
  authType: "oauth",
  requiredScopes: ["com.intuit.quickbooks.accounting"],
  featureFlag: "INTEGRATION_QBO",

  getAuthUrl(connectionId: string, redirectUri: string, state: string): string {
    const clientId = process.env.QBO_CLIENT_ID || "";
    const scope = "com.intuit.quickbooks.accounting";
    const params = new URLSearchParams({
      client_id: clientId,
      response_type: "code",
      scope,
      redirect_uri: redirectUri,
      state,
    });
    return `${QBO_AUTH_BASE}?${params.toString()}`;
  },

  async handleCallback(code: string, state: string): Promise<TokenSet> {
    const clientId = process.env.QBO_CLIENT_ID || "";
    const clientSecret = process.env.QBO_CLIENT_SECRET || "";
    
    const stateData = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(state, "base64url").toString());
    const redirectUri = stateData.redirectUri || "";

    const basicAuth = Buffer.from(`${clientId}:${clientSecret}`).toString("base64");

    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 30000);

    try {
      const response = await fetch(QBO_TOKEN_URL, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
          "Authorization": `Basic ${basicAuth}`,
          "Accept": "application/json",
        },
        body: new URLSearchParams({
          grant_type: "authorization_code",
          code,
          redirect_uri: redirectUri,
        }),
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      clearTimeout(timeout);

      if (!response.ok) {
        const errText = await response.text();
        logError("QBO token exchange failed", { status: response.status, body: errText });
        throw new Error(`QBO token exchange failed: ${response.status}`);
      }

      const data = await response.json();
      return {
        accessToken: data.access_token,
        refreshToken: data.refresh_token,
        expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + data.expires_in * 1000),
        scope: data.scope || "",
        tokenType: data.token_type || "Bearer",
        raw: { realmId: data.realmId || stateData.realmId },
      };
    } catch (err) {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      throw err;
    }
  },

  async refreshToken(connection: IntegrationConnection): Promise<TokenSet> {
    const clientId = process.env.QBO_CLIENT_ID || "";
    const clientSecret = process.env.QBO_CLIENT_SECRET || "";
    const basicAuth = Buffer.from(`${clientId}:${clientSecret}`).toString("base64");
    const metadata = connection.metadata as Record<string, any> || {};

    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 30000);

    try {
      const response = await fetch(QBO_TOKEN_URL, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
          "Authorization": `Basic ${basicAuth}`,
          "Accept": "application/json",
        },
        body: new URLSearchParams({
          grant_type: "refresh_token",
          refresh_token: metadata.refreshToken || "",
        }),
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      clearTimeout(timeout);

      if (!response.ok) {
        if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 400) {
          const errBody = await response.text().catch(() => "");
          if (/invalid_grant|AuthorizationFailed|Token.*expired/i.test(errBody) || response.status === 401) {
            throw new OAuthPermanentError(`QBO OAuth token permanently revoked: ${response.status} ${errBody.substring(0, 150)}`);
          }
        }
        throw new Error(`QBO token refresh failed: ${response.status}`);
      }

      const data = await response.json();
      return {
        accessToken: data.access_token,
        refreshToken: data.refresh_token,
        expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + data.expires_in * 1000),
        tokenType: data.token_type || "Bearer",
        raw: { realmId: metadata.realmId },
      };
    } catch (err) {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      throw err;
    }
  },

  async testConnection(connection: IntegrationConnection): Promise<{ ok: boolean; message: string }> {
    try {
      const metadata = connection.metadata as Record<string, any> || {};
      const realmId = metadata.realmId;
      if (!realmId) return { ok: false, message: "No realm ID found" };

      const baseUrl = process.env.QBO_ENVIRONMENT === "production"
        ? "https://quickbooks.api.intuit.com"
        : "https://sandbox-quickbooks.api.intuit.com";

      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 15000);

      const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/v3/company/${realmId}/companyinfo/${realmId}`, {
        headers: {
          "Authorization": `Bearer ${metadata.accessToken || ""}`,
          "Accept": "application/json",
        },
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      clearTimeout(timeout);

      if (response.ok) {
        return { ok: true, message: "Connected to QuickBooks Online" };
      }
      return { ok: false, message: `QBO API returned ${response.status}` };
    } catch (err) {
      return { ok: false, message: String(err) };
    }
  },

  async disconnect(_connection: IntegrationConnection): Promise<void> {
    logInfo("QBO connection disconnected");
  },
};

----- FILE: server/integrations/providers/qbo/sync.ts (1272 lines — top 350 + function inventory) -----
import type { SyncResult, SyncError } from "../../types";
import type { IntegrationConnection } from "@shared/schema";
import { db } from "../../../db";
import { integrationSyncState, qboAccountMappings, payRuns, payRunLineItems, subcontractorPayments, subcontractorContracts, crmOrganisations, procurementItems, projects, tmInvoices, users, haulTickets, suretyBonds } from "@shared/schema";
import { eq, and, inArray, isNotNull } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logInfo, logError, logWarn } from "../../../utils/logger";
import { registerSyncRetryHandler } from "../../syncRetry";
// QBO and Sage share the same exported-id tracking shape. The helpers
// live in `server/integrations/idMapping.ts`; both providers consume the
// same source so cache + idempotency behaviour can never diverge.
import { getExportedIds, trackExportedId } from "../../idMapping";

function getQboBaseUrl(): string {
  return process.env.QBO_ENVIRONMENT === "production"
    ? "https://quickbooks.api.intuit.com"
    : "https://sandbox-quickbooks.api.intuit.com";
}

async function qboFetch(realmId: string, accessToken: string, endpoint: string): Promise<any> {
  const baseUrl = getQboBaseUrl();
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 30000);

  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/v3/company/${realmId}/${endpoint}`, {
      headers: {
        "Authorization": `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
        "Accept": "application/json",
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
      },
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    clearTimeout(timeout);

    if (!response.ok) {
      const text = await response.text();
      throw new Error(`QBO API error ${response.status}: ${text}`);
    }

    return response.json();
  } catch (err) {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
    throw err;
  }
}

async function qboPost(realmId: string, accessToken: string, endpoint: string, body: any): Promise<any> {
  const baseUrl = getQboBaseUrl();
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 30000);

  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/v3/company/${realmId}/${endpoint}`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Authorization": `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
        "Accept": "application/json",
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    clearTimeout(timeout);

    if (!response.ok) {
      const text = await response.text();
      throw new Error(`QBO API error ${response.status}: ${text}`);
    }

    return response.json();
  } catch (err) {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
    throw err;
  }
}

async function getAccountMappings(connectionId: string, organizationId: string): Promise<Map<string, { qboAccountId: string; qboAccountName: string }>> {
  const mappings = await db.select().from(qboAccountMappings)
    .where(and(
      eq(qboAccountMappings.connectionId, connectionId),
      eq(qboAccountMappings.organizationId, organizationId),
    ));

  const map = new Map<string, { qboAccountId: string; qboAccountName: string }>();
  for (const m of mappings) {
    map.set(`${m.costCode}:${m.mappingType}`, { qboAccountId: m.qboAccountId, qboAccountName: m.qboAccountName });
  }
  return map;
}

async function getDefaultExpenseAccount(connectionId: string): Promise<{ value: string; name: string } | null> {
  const [coaState] = await db.select().from(integrationSyncState)
    .where(and(
      eq(integrationSyncState.connectionId, connectionId),
      eq(integrationSyncState.entityType, "chart_of_accounts")
    ));

  const accounts = (coaState?.metadata as any)?.accounts || [];
  const expenseAccounts = accounts.filter((a: any) => a.classification === "Expense" && a.active);
  const uncategorized = expenseAccounts.find((a: any) =>
    a.name?.toLowerCase().includes("uncategorized") ||
    a.name?.toLowerCase().includes("miscellaneous") ||
    a.accountSubType === "OtherMiscellaneousExpense"
  );
  const fallback = uncategorized || expenseAccounts[0];
  if (fallback) {
    return { value: fallback.qboId, name: fallback.name };
  }
  return null;
}

async function getDefaultLiabilityAccount(connectionId: string): Promise<{ value: string; name: string } | null> {
  const [coaState] = await db.select().from(integrationSyncState)
    .where(and(
      eq(integrationSyncState.connectionId, connectionId),
      eq(integrationSyncState.entityType, "chart_of_accounts")
    ));

  const accounts = (coaState?.metadata as any)?.accounts || [];
  const liabilityAccounts = accounts.filter((a: any) =>
    (a.classification === "Liability" || a.accountType === "Other Current Liability") && a.active
  );
  const payrollLiab = liabilityAccounts.find((a: any) =>
    a.name?.toLowerCase().includes("payroll") ||
    a.name?.toLowerCase().includes("wages")
  );
  const fallback = payrollLiab || liabilityAccounts[0];
  if (fallback) {
    return { value: fallback.qboId, name: fallback.name };
  }
  return null;
}

async function getOrCreateVendor(realmId: string, accessToken: string, vendorName: string): Promise<string> {
  const query = encodeURIComponent(`SELECT * FROM Vendor WHERE DisplayName = '${vendorName.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}'`);
  const data = await qboFetch(realmId, accessToken, `query?query=${query}`);
  const existing = data?.QueryResponse?.Vendor;
  if (existing && existing.length > 0) {
    return existing[0].Id;
  }

  const result = await qboPost(realmId, accessToken, "vendor", {
    DisplayName: vendorName,
  });
  return result?.Vendor?.Id;
}

export async function importChartOfAccounts(
  connection: IntegrationConnection,
  dryRun: boolean = false
): Promise<SyncResult> {
  const metadata = connection.metadata as Record<string, any> || {};
  const errors: SyncError[] = [];
  let created = 0;

  try {
    const data = await qboFetch(
      metadata.realmId,
      metadata.accessToken || "",
      "query?query=SELECT * FROM Account MAXRESULTS 1000"
    );

    const accounts = data?.QueryResponse?.Account || [];
    logInfo("QBO Chart of Accounts fetched", { count: accounts.length, dryRun });

    if (!dryRun) {
      const [existing] = await db.select().from(integrationSyncState)
        .where(and(
          eq(integrationSyncState.connectionId, connection.id),
          eq(integrationSyncState.entityType, "chart_of_accounts")
        ));

      const syncData = {
        accounts: accounts.map((a: any) => ({
          qboId: a.Id,
          name: a.Name,
          accountType: a.AccountType,
          accountSubType: a.AccountSubType,
          classification: a.Classification,
          active: a.Active,
        })),
        syncedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      };

      if (existing) {
        await db.update(integrationSyncState)
          .set({ metadata: syncData, lastSyncAt: new Date(), updatedAt: new Date() })
          .where(eq(integrationSyncState.id, existing.id));
      } else {
        await db.insert(integrationSyncState).values({
          organizationId: connection.organizationId,
          connectionId: connection.id,
          entityType: "chart_of_accounts",
          metadata: syncData,
          lastSyncAt: new Date(),
        });
      }
      created = accounts.length;
    }

    return { success: true, entityType: "chart_of_accounts", direction: "import", created, updated: 0, skipped: 0, errors };
  } catch (err) {
    logError("QBO Chart of Accounts import failed", { error: String(err) });
    errors.push({ message: String(err) });
    return { success: false, entityType: "chart_of_accounts", direction: "import", created: 0, updated: 0, skipped: 0, errors };
  }
}

export async function exportCustomers(
  connection: IntegrationConnection,
  customers: Array<{ id: string; name: string; email?: string; phone?: string }>,
  dryRun: boolean = false
): Promise<SyncResult> {
  const metadata = connection.metadata as Record<string, any> || {};
  const errors: SyncError[] = [];
  let created = 0;
  let updated = 0;
  let skipped = 0;

  for (const customer of customers) {
    try {
      if (dryRun) {
        created++;
        continue;
      }

      const qboCustomer = {
        DisplayName: customer.name,
        PrimaryEmailAddr: customer.email ? { Address: customer.email } : undefined,
        PrimaryPhone: customer.phone ? { FreeFormNumber: customer.phone } : undefined,
      };

      await qboPost(metadata.realmId, metadata.accessToken || "", "customer", qboCustomer);
      created++;
    } catch (err) {
      errors.push({ entityId: customer.id, message: String(err) });
    }
  }

  logInfo("QBO customer export completed", { created, updated, skipped, errors: errors.length });
  return { success: errors.length === 0, entityType: "customer", direction: "export", created, updated, skipped, errors };
}

export async function exportInvoice(
  connection: IntegrationConnection,
  invoice: {
    customerRef: string;
    lineItems: Array<{ description: string; amount: number; accountRef?: string }>;
    dueDate?: string;
    docNumber?: string;
    localInvoiceId?: string;
  },
  dryRun: boolean = false
): Promise<SyncResult> {
  const metadata = connection.metadata as Record<string, any> || {};
  const errors: SyncError[] = [];

  try {
    if (dryRun) {
      return { success: true, entityType: "invoice", direction: "export", created: 1, updated: 0, skipped: 0, errors: [] };
    }

    const qboInvoice: any = {
      CustomerRef: { value: invoice.customerRef },
      Line: invoice.lineItems.map((item, idx) => ({
        DetailType: "SalesItemLineDetail",
        Amount: item.amount,
        Description: item.description,
        SalesItemLineDetail: {
          ItemRef: { value: "1" },
          ...(item.accountRef ? { AccountRef: { value: item.accountRef } } : {}),
        },
        LineNum: idx + 1,
      })),
    };

    if (invoice.dueDate) qboInvoice.DueDate = invoice.dueDate;
    if (invoice.docNumber) qboInvoice.DocNumber = invoice.docNumber;

    const result = await qboPost(metadata.realmId, metadata.accessToken || "", "invoice", qboInvoice);
    const qboInvoiceId = result?.Invoice?.Id;
    logInfo("QBO invoice exported", { invoiceId: qboInvoiceId });

    if (invoice.localInvoiceId && qboInvoiceId) {
      await trackExportedId(connection.id, connection.organizationId, "exported_invoices", invoice.localInvoiceId, qboInvoiceId);
    }

    return { success: true, entityType: "invoice", direction: "export", created: 1, updated: 0, skipped: 0, errors: [] };
  } catch (err) {
    errors.push({ message: String(err) });
    return { success: false, entityType: "invoice", direction: "export", created: 0, updated: 0, skipped: 0, errors };
  }
}

export async function importPaymentStatus(
  connection: IntegrationConnection,
  invoiceIds: string[]
): Promise<SyncResult> {
  const metadata = connection.metadata as Record<string, any> || {};
  const errors: SyncError[] = [];
  let updated = 0;

  for (const invoiceId of invoiceIds) {
    try {
      const data = await qboFetch(
        metadata.realmId,
        metadata.accessToken || "",
        `invoice/${invoiceId}`
      );

      const invoice = data?.Invoice;
      if (invoice) {
        const balance = invoice.Balance;
        const total = invoice.TotalAmt;
        const status = balance === 0 ? "paid" : balance < total ? "partial" : "unpaid";
        logInfo("QBO payment status", { invoiceId, status, balance, total });
        updated++;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      errors.push({ entityId: invoiceId, message: String(err) });
    }
  }

  return { success: errors.length === 0, entityType: "payment", direction: "import", created: 0, updated, skipped: 0, errors };
}

    const [payRun] = await db.select().from(payRuns)
      .where(and(
        eq(payRuns.id, payRunId),
        eq(payRuns.organizationId, connection.organizationId)
      ));

    if (!payRun) {

----- FILE: server/services/qboEntityLabels.ts (full) -----
// Task #1945 — Friendly QBO entity labels (customer name / "Pay app #N —
// projectCode" / "Pay run #N") shared between the exhausted-retry admin
// notify path (server/services/integrationSyncRetryNotifications.ts) and
// the email composer (server/services/notificationDelivery.ts).
//
// Lives in a standalone module so the email composer can pull it in
// without dragging in notificationService → notificationDelivery and
// closing a require cycle.
//
// Task #1975 — Extended to handle `qbo_sync:bill:<id>` (subcontractor
// payments → QBO Bills) and `qbo_sync:vendor:<id>` (vendor crm orgs)
// subKinds for when the QBO sync starts pushing per-record Bills/Vendors
// (currently those operations sync in batches as `qbo_sync:bills` /
// `qbo_sync:vendors` and don't carry an id). Keeping the resolver
// future-proof so the day the sync layer splits the batch into per-row
// retries the email + digest copy already names the affected record.

import { and, eq, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import {
  crmOrganisations,
  payApplications,
  payRuns,
  projects,
  subcontractorContracts,
  subcontractorPayments,
} from "@shared/schema";

const QBO_SYNC_ENTITY_TYPE_PREFIX = "qbo_sync:";

export type QboPerItemSubKind =
  | "customer"
  | "invoice"
  | "payroll"
  | "certified_payroll"
  | "bill"
  | "vendor";

export interface QboResolvedEntity {
  subKind: QboPerItemSubKind;
  itemId: string;
  /** Human-readable label, e.g. "Acme Corp", "Pay app #3 — PROJ-123",
   *  "Pay run #45", "Certified pay run 2025-01-01 – 2025-01-15",
   *  "Bill #2 from Acme Subs", "Acme Subs". */
  displayName: string;
  /** Relative deep link to the in-app record. */
  link: string;
}

/**
 * Pull the per-item subKind + itemId out of an entityType string. Returns
 * `null` for anything that isn't a `qbo_sync:<customer|invoice|payroll|
 * certified_payroll|bill|vendor>:<id>` shape (bare scheduled ops, batch
 * syncs, or non-QBO providers).
 */
function classifyQboPerItemEntity(
  entityType: string,
): { subKind: QboPerItemSubKind; itemId: string } | null {
  if (!entityType.startsWith(QBO_SYNC_ENTITY_TYPE_PREFIX)) return null;
  const operation = entityType.slice(QBO_SYNC_ENTITY_TYPE_PREFIX.length);
  const colonIdx = operation.indexOf(":");
  if (colonIdx <= 0) return null;
  const subKind = operation.slice(0, colonIdx);
  const itemId = operation.slice(colonIdx + 1);
  if (!itemId) return null;
  if (
    subKind === "customer" ||
    subKind === "invoice" ||
    subKind === "payroll" ||
    subKind === "certified_payroll" ||
    subKind === "bill" ||
    subKind === "vendor"
  ) {
    return { subKind, itemId };
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Batch-resolve human-readable labels + deep links for a set of
 * `qbo_sync:%` entity types within a single org. Runs at most one query
 * per record kind (customer / invoice / pay-run / bill / vendor) so the
 * per-row notify path and the backfill summary path both stay O(1)
 * round-trips.
 *
 * Customer + vendor lookups share a single crm_organisations query
 * (both subKinds resolve against the same table and are scoped by
 * organizationId) — the rendering pass below still picks the right
 * subKind-specific link template.
 *
 * Orphan IDs (record was deleted) simply don't appear in the returned
 * map; callers fall back to the existing kindLabel + itemId rendering.
 */
export async function resolveQboEntityLabels(
  organizationId: string,
  entityTypes: readonly string[],
): Promise<Map<string, QboResolvedEntity>> {
  const result = new Map<string, QboResolvedEntity>();
  const crmOrgIds = new Set<string>();
  const invoiceIds = new Set<string>();
  const payRunIds = new Set<string>();
  const billIds = new Set<string>();
  const perEntity = new Map<
    string,
    { subKind: QboPerItemSubKind; itemId: string }
  >();
  for (const et of entityTypes) {
    const cls = classifyQboPerItemEntity(et);
    if (!cls) continue;
    perEntity.set(et, cls);
    if (cls.subKind === "customer" || cls.subKind === "vendor") {
      crmOrgIds.add(cls.itemId);
    } else if (cls.subKind === "invoice") invoiceIds.add(cls.itemId);
    else if (cls.subKind === "bill") billIds.add(cls.itemId);
    else payRunIds.add(cls.itemId);
  }
  if (perEntity.size === 0) return result;

  // crm_organisations covers both `customer` and `vendor` subKinds — one
  // query, scoped by organizationId so cross-tenant lookups can't leak.
  const crmOrgLookup = new Map<string, { name: string }>();
  if (crmOrgIds.size > 0) {
    const orgs = await db
      .select({ id: crmOrganisations.id, name: crmOrganisations.name })
      .from(crmOrganisations)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(crmOrganisations.organizationId, organizationId),
          inArray(crmOrganisations.id, Array.from(crmOrgIds)),
        ),
      );
    for (const o of orgs) crmOrgLookup.set(o.id, { name: o.name });
  }

  const invoiceLookup = new Map<
    string,
    {
      applicationNumber: number;
      projectCode: string | null;
      projectName: string | null;
    }
  >();
  if (invoiceIds.size > 0) {
    const apps = await db
      .select({
        id: payApplications.id,
        applicationNumber: payApplications.applicationNumber,
        projectCode: projects.code,
        projectName: projects.name,
      })
      .from(payApplications)
      .leftJoin(projects, eq(projects.id, payApplications.projectId))
      .where(
        and(
          eq(payApplications.organizationId, organizationId),
          inArray(payApplications.id, Array.from(invoiceIds)),
        ),
      );
    for (const a of apps) {
      invoiceLookup.set(a.id, {
        applicationNumber: a.applicationNumber,
        projectCode: a.projectCode,
        projectName: a.projectName,
      });
    }
  }

  const payRunLookup = new Map<
    string,
    { runNumber: string | null; periodStart: string; periodEnd: string }
  >();
  if (payRunIds.size > 0) {
    const runs = await db
      .select({
        id: payRuns.id,
        runNumber: payRuns.runNumber,
        periodStart: payRuns.payPeriodStart,
        periodEnd: payRuns.payPeriodEnd,
      })
      .from(payRuns)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(payRuns.organizationId, organizationId),
          inArray(payRuns.id, Array.from(payRunIds)),
        ),
      );
    for (const r of runs) {
      payRunLookup.set(r.id, {
        runNumber: r.runNumber,
        periodStart: r.periodStart,
        periodEnd: r.periodEnd,
      });
    }
  }

  // Bills (subcontractor_payments → QBO Bill) — join through
  // subcontractor_contracts → crm_organisations to pick up the vendor
  // name so the label reads "Bill #N from Vendor X". Scoped by
  // organizationId on subcontractor_payments to keep tenants isolated.
  const billLookup = new Map<
    string,
    { paymentNumber: number; vendorName: string | null }
  >();
  if (billIds.size > 0) {
    const bills = await db
      .select({
        id: subcontractorPayments.id,
        paymentNumber: subcontractorPayments.paymentNumber,
        vendorName: crmOrganisations.name,
      })
      .from(subcontractorPayments)
      .leftJoin(
        subcontractorContracts,
        eq(subcontractorContracts.id, subcontractorPayments.contractId),
      )
      // Defense-in-depth: also constrain the vendor join by
      // organizationId so a stray cross-tenant subcontractor row could
      // never leak its name into another org's friendly label, even if
      // the contract → vendor FK got corrupted somehow.
      .leftJoin(
        crmOrganisations,
        and(
          eq(crmOrganisations.id, subcontractorContracts.subcontractorId),
          eq(crmOrganisations.organizationId, organizationId),
        ),
      )
      .where(
        and(
          eq(subcontractorPayments.organizationId, organizationId),
          inArray(subcontractorPayments.id, Array.from(billIds)),
        ),
      );
    for (const b of bills) {
      billLookup.set(b.id, {
        paymentNumber: b.paymentNumber,
        vendorName: b.vendorName,
      });
    }
  }

  for (const [et, { subKind, itemId }] of Array.from(perEntity.entries())) {
    if (subKind === "customer") {
      const hit = crmOrgLookup.get(itemId);
      if (hit) {
        result.set(et, {
          subKind,
          itemId,
          displayName: hit.name,
          link: `/crm?organisationId=${encodeURIComponent(itemId)}`,
        });
      }
    } else if (subKind === "vendor") {
      const hit = crmOrgLookup.get(itemId);
      if (hit) {
        result.set(et, {
          subKind,
          itemId,
          displayName: hit.name,
          link: `/crm?organisationId=${encodeURIComponent(itemId)}`,
        });
      }
    } else if (subKind === "invoice") {
      const hit = invoiceLookup.get(itemId);
      if (hit) {
        const projectLabel = hit.projectCode ?? hit.projectName ?? null;
        const displayName = projectLabel
          ? `Pay app #${hit.applicationNumber} — ${projectLabel}`
          : `Pay app #${hit.applicationNumber}`;
        result.set(et, {
          subKind,
          itemId,
          displayName,
          link: `/progress-billing?payApplicationId=${encodeURIComponent(itemId)}`,
        });
      }
    } else if (subKind === "bill") {
      const hit = billLookup.get(itemId);
      if (hit) {
        const displayName = hit.vendorName
          ? `Bill #${hit.paymentNumber} from ${hit.vendorName}`
          : `Bill #${hit.paymentNumber}`;
        result.set(et, {
          subKind,
          itemId,
          displayName,
          link: `/subcontractor-payables?paymentId=${encodeURIComponent(itemId)}`,
        });
      }
    } else {
      const hit = payRunLookup.get(itemId);
      if (hit) {
        const variantLabel =
          subKind === "certified_payroll" ? "Certified pay run" : "Pay run";
        const numberLabel = hit.runNumber
          ? `#${hit.runNumber}`
          : `${hit.periodStart} – ${hit.periodEnd}`;
        result.set(et, {
          subKind,
          itemId,
          displayName: `${variantLabel} ${numberLabel}`,
          link: `/payroll-management?payRunId=${encodeURIComponent(itemId)}`,
        });
      }
    }
  }

  return result;
}

/** Convenience: resolve a single entityType. */
export async function resolveQboEntityLabel(
  organizationId: string,
  entityType: string,
): Promise<QboResolvedEntity | null> {
  const map = await resolveQboEntityLabels(organizationId, [entityType]);
  return map.get(entityType) ?? null;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Task #1976 — Per-batch cache for resolveQboEntityLabel.
//
// When an INTEGRATION_SYNC_RETRIES_EXHAUSTED notification fans out to
// multiple admins, the email composer's loadIntegrationSyncContext path
// (server/services/notificationDelivery.ts) calls resolveQboEntityLabel
// once per recipient — so an org with 5 admins and 20 failed rows would
// produce 100 redundant per-record lookups for the same 20 records.
//
// The cache below collapses repeats to one call per (organizationId,
// entityType) within a single delivery batch. It's intentionally a
// plain Map keyed by an in-flight promise — no TTL, no eviction —
// because a `QboEntityLabelCache` is created fresh by
// `deliverNotifications` and discarded once the batch settles. There's
// nothing to invalidate; it just lives for the lifetime of one batch.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export type QboEntityLabelCache = Map<string, Promise<QboResolvedEntity | null>>;

export function createQboEntityLabelCache(): QboEntityLabelCache {
  return new Map();
}

function cacheKey(organizationId: string, entityType: string): string {
  return `${organizationId}::${entityType}`;
}

/**
 * Indirection so tests (and the cached variant below) can swap the
 * resolver without having to mock around ESM live-binding hoisting.
 * Production code never reaches into this object.
 */
export const __qboEntityLabelInternals = {
  resolve: resolveQboEntityLabel,
};

/**
 * Cached variant of resolveQboEntityLabel. When `cache` is supplied,
 * repeat lookups for the same (organizationId, entityType) reuse the
 * pending or settled promise so the resolver only runs once per pair
 * for the lifetime of the cache. Without a cache, behaves identically
 * to resolveQboEntityLabel.
 */
export async function resolveQboEntityLabelCached(
  organizationId: string,
  entityType: string,
  cache?: QboEntityLabelCache,
): Promise<QboResolvedEntity | null> {
  if (!cache) {
    return __qboEntityLabelInternals.resolve(organizationId, entityType);
  }
  const key = cacheKey(organizationId, entityType);
  const existing = cache.get(key);
  if (existing) return existing;
  const pending = __qboEntityLabelInternals
    .resolve(organizationId, entityType)
    .catch((err) => {
      // Don't poison the cache with a rejected promise — drop it so a
      // subsequent caller can retry. Re-throw so the original caller
      // still sees the failure.
      cache.delete(key);
      throw err;
    });
  cache.set(key, pending);
  return pending;
}

GAPS (Target 3):
1. No active cron/scheduled trigger for QBO sync was found in
   server/ (no cron.schedule pointing at the qbo provider). Sync is
   expected to run via the outbox dispatcher (outboxService) when an
   entity changes, plus manual /api/integrations/qbo/* endpoints.
2. Tests under audit/tests/integrations/qboSync.spec.ts are
   isolation-only (orgIsolation + retry behavior); no end-to-end
   integration test against a sandbox QBO account is present in
   the repo.
3. Disconnected-QBO behavior: the provider exists but real-time
   verification that a disconnected token surfaces to the user UI
   was not located.
4. Direction of sync: invoices/cost-postings flow PUSH from
   SmartManageAI → QBO (per sync.ts entity handlers). Vendor/customer
   PULL caching is in qboEntityLabels.ts. Bidirectional reconciliation
   beyond cached labels was not confirmed.

################################################################################
# AUDIT TARGET 4 — NOTIFICATION & ALERT DELIVERY
################################################################################

STATUS: PARTIALLY WIRED

SUMMARY:
- In-app notifications: notifications table has 29 distinct `type`
  enum values in use (see DB query). server/services/notificationService.ts
  (483 lines) is the producer; server/services/notificationDelivery.ts
  (1243 lines) is the fan-out engine (in-app + email + push).
- Push delivery: server/services/pushService.ts (74 lines) — uses
  web-push + VAPID env vars.
- Email delivery: SendGrid wrapper in server/email.ts and
  server/services/emailService.ts. 14+ call-sites of sendEmail() across
  bid network, status alerts, COI, workforce, PDF alerts, status page,
  CRM meetings, CRM CSV import, bulk export, SLA monitor, accounting
  sync.
- Twilio SMS: present but ONLY for CRM click-to-call recordings
  (server/crmCallsRoutes.ts). A general isTwilioConfigured() check
  exists in server/notificationPreferencesRoutes.ts but there is NO
  generic sendSms() function wired to alerts.

----- FILE: server/services/notificationService.ts (full) -----
import { db, pool } from "../db";
import { notifications, type InsertNotification } from "@shared/schema";
import {
  deliverNotification,
  type NotificationRecord,
} from "./notificationDelivery";
import { createQboEntityLabelCache } from "./qboEntityLabels";
import { logError, logInfo } from "../utils/logger";

export async function insertNotification(values: InsertNotification): Promise<void> {
  const [inserted] = await db.insert(notifications).values(values as typeof notifications.$inferInsert).returning();
  if (inserted) {
    const record: NotificationRecord = {
      id: inserted.id,
      userId: inserted.userId,
      type: inserted.type as NotificationRecord["type"],
      title: inserted.title,
      message: inserted.message,
      relatedEntityId: inserted.relatedEntityId,
      relatedEntityType: inserted.relatedEntityType,
      // Task #1974 — propagate persisted metadata to the delivery layer
      // so any future channel that wants to consume {recordLink, recordLabel}
      // (currently the in-app bell row only) doesn't have to re-resolve it.
      metadata: inserted.metadata,
    };
    setImmediate(() => {
      deliverNotification(record).catch(() => undefined);
    });
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Task #715 + #722 + #723: coalesce rapid-fire single-entity owner
// reassignments so a new owner doesn't get one email per record when a
// teammate reassigns dozens of records one-by-one through the single PUT
// endpoints. The bulk endpoints already collapse into one notification; this
// helper gives the single-PUT path equivalent behaviour.
//
// Task #715 introduced the coalescer for CRM contacts. Task #723 generalised
// it across the other owner-change paths that suffered the same email-spam
// problem — CRM opportunities and projects — by parameterising on entity
// "kind" so each notification still routes to the correct destination and
// uses entity-appropriate copy.
//
// Strategy: send the *first* reassignment notification immediately so single,
// occasional reassignments still feel snappy. Open a 5-minute coalescing
// window per (kind, recipient); any further reassignments arriving inside
// that window are accumulated into a single summary notification flushed
// when the window closes.
//
// Persistence (Task #722, generalised in Task #723): the per-(kind,
// recipient) buffer lives in `pending_contact_reassignment_notifications` so
// a server restart inside the 5-minute window doesn't drop the bundled
// "extras" summary. The flush step uses `DELETE ... RETURNING` so when
// multiple instances run concurrently, only the instance that successfully
// claims the row sends the summary.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const REASSIGNMENT_COALESCE_WINDOW_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;
const PENDING_TABLE = "pending_contact_reassignment_notifications";

export type ReassignmentKind = "contact" | "opportunity" | "project" | "organization";

interface KindConfig {
  notificationType: NotificationRecord["type"];
  relatedEntityType: string;
  // "CRM contact", "CRM opportunity", "project"
  singularLabel: string;
  pluralLabel: string;
  // Title for the immediate one-off notification.
  immediateTitle: string;
  // Title prefix for the summary notification, used as `${prefix} ${noun}`.
  // For contacts the legacy copy injects an extra "CRM " before the noun, so
  // contact uses "CRM " in the noun via singularLabel/pluralLabel and the
  // summary template is shared.
  summaryIncludesNoun: true;
}

const KIND_CONFIG: Record<ReassignmentKind, KindConfig> = {
  contact: {
    notificationType: "CRM_CONTACT_REASSIGNED",
    relatedEntityType: "CRM_CONTACT",
    singularLabel: "CRM contact",
    pluralLabel: "CRM contacts",
    immediateTitle: "A CRM contact was assigned to you",
    summaryIncludesNoun: true,
  },
  opportunity: {
    notificationType: "CRM_OPPORTUNITY_REASSIGNED" as NotificationRecord["type"],
    relatedEntityType: "CRM_OPPORTUNITY",
    singularLabel: "CRM opportunity",
    pluralLabel: "CRM opportunities",
    immediateTitle: "A CRM opportunity was assigned to you",
    summaryIncludesNoun: true,
  },
  project: {
    notificationType: "PROJECT_REASSIGNED" as NotificationRecord["type"],
    relatedEntityType: "PROJECT",
    singularLabel: "project",
    pluralLabel: "projects",
    immediateTitle: "A project was assigned to you",
    summaryIncludesNoun: true,
  },
  // Task #731: CRM organisations gained an explicit ownerId column, so owner
  // reassignments can now route through the same coalescer as the other CRM
  // entities and bundle into a single summary per recipient per window.
  organization: {
    notificationType: "CRM_ORGANIZATION_REASSIGNED" as NotificationRecord["type"],
    relatedEntityType: "CRM_ORGANIZATION",
    singularLabel: "CRM organization",
    pluralLabel: "CRM organizations",
    immediateTitle: "A CRM organization was assigned to you",
    summaryIncludesNoun: true,
  },
};

// Per-process map of scheduled flush timers. The DB row is the source of
// truth; this map only avoids stacking up redundant local timers and lets
// tests cancel pending timers deterministically.
const localFlushTimers = new Map<string, NodeJS.Timeout>();

function pendingKey(kind: ReassignmentKind, recipientUserId: string): string {
  return `${kind}:${recipientUserId}`;
}

export interface QueueReassignmentArgs {
  kind: ReassignmentKind;
  recipientUserId: string;
  organizationId?: string;
  reassignerName: string;
  entityId: string;
  // Display name used in the immediate notification message
  // (e.g. contact full name, opportunity name, project name).
  entityName: string;
}

interface PendingRow {
  kind: ReassignmentKind;
  recipient_user_id: string;
  organization_id: string | null;
  additional_count: number;
  reassigner_names: string[];
  first_additional_contact_id: string | null;
  flush_at: Date;
}

interface UpsertResult {
  inserted: boolean;
  flushAt: Date;
}

async function upsertPendingRow(args: QueueReassignmentArgs): Promise<UpsertResult> {
  const result = await pool.query<{ inserted: boolean; flush_at: Date }>(
    `
    INSERT INTO ${PENDING_TABLE}
      (kind, recipient_user_id, organization_id, additional_count, reassigner_names,
       first_additional_contact_id, window_started_at, flush_at)
    VALUES
      ($1, $2, $3, 0, ARRAY[]::text[], NULL, NOW(),
       NOW() + INTERVAL '${REASSIGNMENT_COALESCE_WINDOW_MS} milliseconds')
    ON CONFLICT (kind, recipient_user_id) DO UPDATE SET
      additional_count = ${PENDING_TABLE}.additional_count + 1,
      reassigner_names = CASE
        WHEN $4::text = ANY(${PENDING_TABLE}.reassigner_names)
          THEN ${PENDING_TABLE}.reassigner_names
        ELSE array_append(${PENDING_TABLE}.reassigner_names, $4::text)
      END,
      first_additional_contact_id = COALESCE(
        ${PENDING_TABLE}.first_additional_contact_id, $5
      ),
      organization_id = COALESCE(${PENDING_TABLE}.organization_id, $3)
    RETURNING (xmax = 0) AS inserted, flush_at
    `,
    [
      args.kind,
      args.recipientUserId,
      args.organizationId ?? null,
      args.reassignerName,
      args.entityId,
    ],
  );
  const row = result.rows[0];
  return { inserted: row.inserted, flushAt: row.flush_at };
}

async function claimPendingRow(
  kind: ReassignmentKind,
  recipientUserId: string,
): Promise<PendingRow | null> {
  const result = await pool.query<PendingRow>(
    `DELETE FROM ${PENDING_TABLE} WHERE kind = $1 AND recipient_user_id = $2 RETURNING *`,
    [kind, recipientUserId],
  );
  return result.rows[0] ?? null;
}

function scheduleLocalFlush(
  kind: ReassignmentKind,
  recipientUserId: string,
  flushAt: Date,
): void {
  const key = pendingKey(kind, recipientUserId);
  if (localFlushTimers.has(key)) return;
  const delay = Math.max(0, flushAt.getTime() - Date.now());
  const timer = setTimeout(() => {
    localFlushTimers.delete(key);
    flushPendingReassignment(kind, recipientUserId).catch((err) => {
      logError(
        "Failed to flush pending owner reassignment summary",
        { kind, recipientUserId },
        err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
      );
    });
  }, delay);
  if (typeof timer.unref === "function") timer.unref();
  localFlushTimers.set(key, timer);
}

export function queueOwnerReassignmentNotification(args: QueueReassignmentArgs): void {
  // Run async work in the background; callers (route handlers) shouldn't have
  // to await coalescer bookkeeping.
  void (async () => {
    try {
      const cfg = KIND_CONFIG[args.kind];
      const { inserted, flushAt } = await upsertPendingRow(args);
      if (inserted) {
        // First reassignment in the window — fire the immediate notification
        // straight away so single, occasional reassignments still feel snappy.
        await insertNotification({
          userId: args.recipientUserId,
          organizationId: args.organizationId,
          type: (cfg.notificationType ?? "GENERAL") as NonNullable<NotificationRecord["type"]>,
          title: cfg.immediateTitle,
          message: `${args.reassignerName} assigned ${args.entityName} to you.`,
          relatedEntityId: args.entityId,
          relatedEntityType: cfg.relatedEntityType,
        });
      }
      scheduleLocalFlush(args.kind, args.recipientUserId, flushAt);
    } catch (err) {
      logError(
        "Failed to queue owner reassignment notification",
        { kind: args.kind, recipientUserId: args.recipientUserId },
        err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
      );
    }
  })();
}

// Backwards-compatible wrapper for the original Task #715 contact path.
export interface QueueContactReassignmentArgs {
  recipientUserId: string;
  organizationId?: string;
  reassignerName: string;
  contactId: string;
  contactName: string;
}

export function queueContactReassignmentNotification(args: QueueContactReassignmentArgs): void {
  queueOwnerReassignmentNotification({
    kind: "contact",
    recipientUserId: args.recipientUserId,
    organizationId: args.organizationId,
    reassignerName: args.reassignerName,
    entityId: args.contactId,
    entityName: args.contactName,
  });
}

async function flushPendingReassignment(
  kind: ReassignmentKind,
  recipientUserId: string,
): Promise<void> {
  // Atomic claim — if another instance already grabbed and flushed this row
  // we get nothing back and quietly stop, preventing duplicate summaries.
  const entry = await claimPendingRow(kind, recipientUserId);
  if (!entry) return;
  if (entry.additional_count === 0) return;

  const cfg = KIND_CONFIG[kind];
  const count = entry.additional_count;
  const noun = count === 1 ? cfg.singularLabel : cfg.pluralLabel;
  const reassigners = entry.reassigner_names ?? [];
  const reassignerName = reassigners.length === 1 ? reassigners[0] : "Teammates";

  await insertNotification({
    userId: recipientUserId,
    organizationId: entry.organization_id ?? undefined,
    type: (cfg.notificationType ?? "GENERAL") as NonNullable<NotificationRecord["type"]>,
    title: `${count} more ${noun} assigned to you`,
    message: `${reassignerName} assigned ${count} more ${noun} to you in the last few minutes.`,
    relatedEntityId: entry.first_additional_contact_id,
    relatedEntityType: cfg.relatedEntityType,
  });
}

// Task #730: belt-and-braces sweeper. The in-process timer + restart recovery
// covers the common path, but a row could be orphaned if (a) the server is
// shut down for longer than the coalescing window and the recovery hook
// silently fails, or (b) a hot redeploy drops timers without the new instance
// observing them. This periodic sweep claims any rows whose flush_at is well
// past so the bundled summary still goes out. Uses the same atomic
// `DELETE ... RETURNING` claim as the timer-driven path so it's safe to run
// alongside other instances or a concurrent timer fire.
const STALE_REASSIGNMENT_THRESHOLD_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;
const SWEEP_INTERVAL_MS = 2 * 60 * 1000;
let sweeperInterval: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;

export async function sweepStaleContactReassignments(
  thresholdMs: number = STALE_REASSIGNMENT_THRESHOLD_MS,
): Promise<number> {
  let staleRows: Array<{ kind: ReassignmentKind; recipient_user_id: string }> = [];
  try {
    const { rows } = await pool.query<{ kind: string; recipient_user_id: string }>(
      `SELECT kind, recipient_user_id FROM ${PENDING_TABLE}
       WHERE flush_at < NOW() - ($1::bigint || ' milliseconds')::interval`,
      [thresholdMs],
    );
    staleRows = rows.map((r) => ({
      kind: r.kind as ReassignmentKind,
      recipient_user_id: r.recipient_user_id,
    }));
  } catch (err) {
    logError(
      "Failed to scan for stale owner reassignment buffers",
      {},
      err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
    );
    return 0;
  }

  if (staleRows.length === 0) return 0;

  let claimed = 0;
  for (const { kind, recipient_user_id: recipientUserId } of staleRows) {
    // Cancel any local timer so we don't race ourselves between the sweeper
    // and a still-armed setTimeout that's about to fire anyway.
    const key = pendingKey(kind, recipientUserId);
    const timer = localFlushTimers.get(key);
    if (timer) {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      localFlushTimers.delete(key);
    }
    try {
      const entry = await claimPendingRow(kind, recipientUserId);
      if (!entry) continue; // another instance / timer beat us to it
      claimed += 1;
      if (entry.additional_count === 0) continue;

      const cfg = KIND_CONFIG[kind];
      const count = entry.additional_count;
      const noun = count === 1 ? cfg.singularLabel : cfg.pluralLabel;
      const reassigners = entry.reassigner_names ?? [];
      const reassignerName = reassigners.length === 1 ? reassigners[0] : "Teammates";

      await insertNotification({
        userId: recipientUserId,
        organizationId: entry.organization_id ?? undefined,
        type: (cfg.notificationType ?? "GENERAL") as NonNullable<NotificationRecord["type"]>,
        title: `${count} more ${noun} assigned to you`,
        message: `${reassignerName} assigned ${count} more ${noun} to you in the last few minutes.`,
        relatedEntityId: entry.first_additional_contact_id,
        relatedEntityType: cfg.relatedEntityType,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      logError(
        "Failed to flush stale owner reassignment buffer",
        { kind, recipientUserId },
        err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
      );
    }
  }

  if (claimed > 0) {
    logInfo(
      `[notifications] Swept ${claimed} stale owner reassignment buffer(s) past flush_at`,
    );
  }
  return claimed;
}

export function startContactReassignmentSweeper(intervalMs: number = SWEEP_INTERVAL_MS): void {
  if (sweeperInterval) return;
  sweeperInterval = setInterval(() => {
    sweepStaleContactReassignments().catch((err) => {
      logError(
        "Stale CRM contact reassignment sweeper failed",
        {},
        err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
      );
    });
  }, intervalMs);
  if (typeof sweeperInterval.unref === "function") sweeperInterval.unref();
}

export function __stopContactReassignmentSweeperForTest(): void {
  if (sweeperInterval) {
    clearInterval(sweeperInterval);
    sweeperInterval = null;
  }
}

// On startup, re-arm timers for any rows still buffered from before the
// restart so the bundled summary is delivered even if the server crashed
// inside the coalescing window.
export async function recoverPendingContactReassignments(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const { rows } = await pool.query<{
      kind: string;
      recipient_user_id: string;
      flush_at: Date;
    }>(
      `SELECT kind, recipient_user_id, flush_at FROM ${PENDING_TABLE}`,
    );
    for (const row of rows) {
      scheduleLocalFlush(row.kind as ReassignmentKind, row.recipient_user_id, row.flush_at);
    }
    if (rows.length > 0) {
      logInfo(
        `[notifications] Recovered ${rows.length} pending owner reassignment buffer(s) after restart`,
      );
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logError(
      "Failed to recover pending owner reassignment buffers",
      {},
      err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
    );
  }
}

// Test-only helpers so unit tests can exercise the coalescer without waiting
// the real five minutes.
export async function __flushReassignmentForTest(
  kind: ReassignmentKind,
  recipientUserId: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const key = pendingKey(kind, recipientUserId);
  const timer = localFlushTimers.get(key);
  if (timer) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    localFlushTimers.delete(key);
  }
  await flushPendingReassignment(kind, recipientUserId);
}

export async function __flushContactReassignmentForTest(recipientUserId: string): Promise<void> {
  await __flushReassignmentForTest("contact", recipientUserId);
}

export function __resetReassignmentQueueForTest(): void {
  localFlushTimers.forEach((timer) => clearTimeout(timer));
  localFlushTimers.clear();
}

export function __resetContactReassignmentQueueForTest(): void {
  __resetReassignmentQueueForTest();
}

export async function insertNotifications(valuesList: InsertNotification[]): Promise<void> {
  if (valuesList.length === 0) return;
  const inserted = await db.insert(notifications).values(valuesList as Array<typeof notifications.$inferInsert>).returning();
  if (inserted.length > 0) {
    const records: NotificationRecord[] = inserted.map((n) => ({
      id: n.id,
      userId: n.userId,
      type: n.type as NotificationRecord["type"],
      title: n.title,
      message: n.message,
      relatedEntityId: n.relatedEntityId,
      relatedEntityType: n.relatedEntityType,
    }));
    setImmediate(() => {
      // Task #1976 — share a per-batch QBO entity-label cache across
      // every fan-out recipient so the same (org, entityType) is only
      // resolved once instead of once per recipient.
      const qboLabelCache = createQboEntityLabelCache();
      Promise.allSettled(
        records.map((r) => deliverNotification(r, qboLabelCache)),
      ).catch(() => undefined);
    });
  }
}

----- FILE: server/services/notificationDelivery.ts (top 350 + dispatch fns) -----
import { db } from "../db";
import { users, notificationChannelPrefs, notificationTypePrefs, notificationTypeEnum, integrationSyncState, integrationConnections } from "@shared/schema";
import { eq, and } from "drizzle-orm";
import { sendNotificationEmail } from "../email";
import { sendEmail } from "./emailService";
import { sendPushToUser } from "./pushService";
import { sendNativePushToUser } from "./nativePushService";
import { sendSms, isTwilioConfigured } from "./smsService";
import { sendTeamsMessage } from "./teamsService";
import { sendSlackMessage } from "./slackService";
import { logError } from "../utils/logger";
import { hasFeature } from "./featureAccess";
import { isFeatureEnabled } from "./featureFlagService";
import { buildSwpppMuteUrl } from "../utils/swpppMuteToken";
import { buildDavisBaconMuteUrl } from "../utils/davisBaconMuteToken";
import { buildWageDecisionRatesChangedMuteUrl } from "../utils/wageDecisionRatesChangedMuteToken";
import { buildIntegrationSyncMuteUrl } from "../utils/integrationSyncMuteToken";
import { buildLogEmailFailuresDigestMuteUrl } from "../utils/logEmailFailuresDigestMuteToken";
