  resolveQboEntityLabelCached,
  createQboEntityLabelCache,
  type QboEntityLabelCache,
} from "./qboEntityLabels";

type NotificationType = (typeof notificationTypeEnum.enumValues)[number];

export interface NotificationRecord {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
  type: NotificationType | null;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  relatedEntityId?: string | null;
  relatedEntityType?: string | null;
  // Task #1974 — opaque per-notification metadata (currently the
  // {recordLink, recordLabel} pair the INTEGRATION_SYNC_RETRIES_EXHAUSTED
  // fan-out resolves at creation time so the in-app bell row can render
  // a secondary "Open <label>" link).
  metadata?: { recordLink?: string; recordLabel?: string } | null;
}

interface ChannelPrefs {
  emailEnabled: boolean;
  smsEnabled: boolean;
  pushEnabled: boolean;
  teamsEnabled: boolean;
  slackEnabled: boolean;
  smsPhoneNumber: string | null;
  teamsWebhookUrl: string | null;
  slackWebhookUrl: string | null;
}

// All channels off by default — users must explicitly enable them
const DEFAULT_PREFS: ChannelPrefs = {
  emailEnabled: false,
  smsEnabled: false,
  pushEnabled: false,
  teamsEnabled: false,
  slackEnabled: false,
  smsPhoneNumber: null,
  teamsWebhookUrl: null,
  slackWebhookUrl: null,
};

async function loadEffectivePrefs(userId: string, notifType: NotificationType | null): Promise<ChannelPrefs> {
  const [channelRow] = await db
    .select()
    .from(notificationChannelPrefs)
    .where(eq(notificationChannelPrefs.userId, userId))
    .limit(1);

  const base: ChannelPrefs = channelRow
    ? {
        emailEnabled: channelRow.emailEnabled,
        smsEnabled: channelRow.smsEnabled,
        pushEnabled: channelRow.pushEnabled,
        teamsEnabled: channelRow.teamsEnabled,
        slackEnabled: channelRow.slackEnabled,
        smsPhoneNumber: channelRow.smsPhoneNumber ?? null,
        teamsWebhookUrl: channelRow.teamsWebhookUrl ?? null,
        slackWebhookUrl: channelRow.slackWebhookUrl ?? null,
      }
    : { ...DEFAULT_PREFS };

  if (!notifType) return base;

  const [typeRow] = await db
    .select()
    .from(notificationTypePrefs)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(notificationTypePrefs.userId, userId),
        eq(notificationTypePrefs.notificationType, notifType)
      )
    )
    .limit(1);

  if (!typeRow) return base;

  // Task #1747 — A type-level override row whose snooze has elapsed
  // (`muted_until <= now`) is treated as if it didn't exist. This keeps
  // outbound delivery (email/SMS/push/Teams/Slack) consistent with the
  // dashboard mute card and the in-app feed mute set, both of which
  // already ignore expired rows. Without this filter the user could
  // see "auto-clears in 0 days" in the UI yet still have email
  // delivery suppressed by the stale override.
  if (typeRow.mutedUntil && typeRow.mutedUntil.getTime() <= Date.now()) {
    return base;
  }

  // Merge type-level overrides onto base channel prefs field-by-field.
  // This ensures a per-type row only overrides the channels explicitly stored in it,
  // and future partial-row scenarios are handled gracefully.
  // Webhook URLs and phone numbers are channel-level settings — always carry base values.
  return {
    emailEnabled: typeRow.emailEnabled ?? base.emailEnabled,
    smsEnabled: typeRow.smsEnabled ?? base.smsEnabled,
    pushEnabled: typeRow.pushEnabled ?? base.pushEnabled,
    teamsEnabled: typeRow.teamsEnabled ?? base.teamsEnabled,
    slackEnabled: typeRow.slackEnabled ?? base.slackEnabled,
    smsPhoneNumber: base.smsPhoneNumber,
    teamsWebhookUrl: base.teamsWebhookUrl,
    slackWebhookUrl: base.slackWebhookUrl,
  };
}

function escapeHtml(s: string): string {
  return s
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
}

/**
 * Extra context an async caller can feed the composer. Required for the
 * INTEGRATION_SYNC_RETRIES_EXHAUSTED one-click mute link because the
 * notification row only stores the sync-state PK — we need the
 * connectionId + entityType to sign the token. SMS/push callers skip
 * this (their templates don't render the mute footer).
 */
export interface ComposeEmailExtras {
  integrationSyncContext?: {
    connectionId: string;
    entityType: string;
    integrationLabel: string;
    /**
     * Task #1942 — when the exhausted row belongs to a QuickBooks
     * connection (`provider === "qbo"`), the email's CTA deep-links to
     * the QBO panel anchor (`#qbo-sync-retry-<id>`) instead of the
     * generic SyncRetryPanel anchor, since admins on a QBO failure want
     * to land in the QBO-specific feed (Task #1394). Optional so older
     * callers without provider info still produce a working (generic)
     * link.
     */
    provider?: string;
    /**
     * Task #1945 — when the exhausted row is a per-item QBO sync
     * (customer / invoice / payroll / certified_payroll), surface a
     * direct deep link to the affected record's page (/crm,
     * /progress-billing, /payroll-management) so admins can jump
     * straight to it from the email. Falls through (link unset) for
     * orphan IDs and bare scheduled ops.
     */
    recordLink?: string;
    recordLabel?: string;
  };
}

export function composeEmailContent(
  notification: NotificationRecord,
  extras: ComposeEmailExtras = {},
): {
  subject: string;
  body: string;
  actionUrl?: string;
  actionText?: string;
  html?: string;
} {
  const baseUrl = process.env.APP_BASE_URL || "";
  const type = notification.type;

  // Task #688: rich HTML email for the weekly CRM contact birthday digest.
  if (notification.relatedEntityType === "CRM_CONTACT_BIRTHDAY") {
    const lines = notification.message.split("\n").filter(Boolean);
    // First line is the header ("Upcoming contact birthdays this week:")
    const [header, ...itemLines] = lines.length > 1 ? lines : ["Upcoming contact birthdays this week:", ...lines];
    const actionUrl = `${baseUrl}/crm`;
    const subject = `${notification.title} — SmartManageAI`;
    const items = itemLines.length > 0 ? itemLines : [notification.message];
    const listHtml = items
      .map((line) => `<li style="padding: 6px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #f0f0f0; color: #333;">${escapeHtml(line)}</li>`)
      .join("");
    const html = `
      <div style="font-family: Arial, sans-serif; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto;">
        <h2 style="color: #333; margin-bottom: 8px;">${escapeHtml(notification.title)}</h2>
        <p style="color: #666; line-height: 1.6; margin-top: 0;">${escapeHtml(header)}</p>
        <ul style="list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 16px 0; background: #fafafa; border: 1px solid #eee; border-radius: 6px; padding: 8px 16px;">
          ${listHtml}
        </ul>
        <p style="margin-top: 20px;">
          <a href="${actionUrl}" style="background-color: #0070f3; color: white; padding: 12px 24px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px; display: inline-block;">
            Open CRM
          </a>
        </p>
        <hr style="margin-top: 30px; border: none; border-top: 1px solid #eee;">
        <p style="color: #999; font-size: 12px;">You are receiving this weekly digest because reminder emails are enabled in your SmartManageAI notification preferences.</p>
      </div>
    `;
    return {
      subject,
      body: notification.message,
      actionUrl,
      actionText: "Open CRM",
      html,
    };
  }

  // Task #1116: rich HTML email for the weekly certified payroll digest.
  // The job stores the date range in `relatedEntityId` as
  // `"YYYY-MM-DD|YYYY-MM-DD"` and seeds the message body with a header
  // line followed by `Project — Variant: count` lines.
  if (notification.relatedEntityType === "CERTIFIED_PAYROLL_DIGEST") {
    const range = (notification.relatedEntityId ?? "").split("|");
    const startDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(range[0] ?? "") ? range[0] : "";
    const endDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(range[1] ?? "") ? range[1] : "";
    const qs = new URLSearchParams();
    if (startDate) qs.set("startDate", startDate);
    if (endDate) qs.set("endDate", endDate);
    const actionUrl = `${baseUrl}/payroll/certified-history${qs.toString() ? `?${qs.toString()}` : ""}`;
    const subject = `${notification.title} — SmartManageAI`;

    const lines = notification.message.split("\n");
    const header = lines[0] ?? notification.message;
    const breakdown = lines.slice(1).filter((l) => l.trim().length > 0);

    const rowsHtml = breakdown.map((line) => {
      const sepIdx = line.lastIndexOf(": ");
      const left = sepIdx >= 0 ? line.slice(0, sepIdx) : line;
      const count = sepIdx >= 0 ? line.slice(sepIdx + 2) : "";
      const dashIdx = left.lastIndexOf(" — ");
      const project = dashIdx >= 0 ? left.slice(0, dashIdx) : left;
      const variant = dashIdx >= 0 ? left.slice(dashIdx + 3) : "";
      return `<tr>
        <td style="padding:8px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0;color:#333;">${escapeHtml(project)}</td>
        <td style="padding:8px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0;color:#555;">${escapeHtml(variant)}</td>
        <td style="padding:8px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0;color:#111;text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;">${escapeHtml(count)}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join("");

    const html = `
      <div style="font-family: Arial, sans-serif; max-width: 640px; margin: 0 auto;">
        <h2 style="color: #333; margin-bottom: 8px;">${escapeHtml(notification.title)}</h2>
        <p style="color: #666; line-height: 1.6; margin-top: 0;">${escapeHtml(header)}</p>
        <table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin:16px 0;border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:6px;overflow:hidden;">
          <thead>
            <tr style="background:#fafafa;">

----- FILE: server/services/pushService.ts (full) -----
import webpush from "web-push";
import { db } from "../db";
import { pushSubscriptions } from "@shared/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logError, logInfo } from "../utils/logger";

const VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY = process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY || "";
const VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY = process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY || "";
const VAPID_SUBJECT = process.env.VAPID_SUBJECT || "mailto:support@smartmanageai.com";

if (VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY && VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY) {
  webpush.setVapidDetails(VAPID_SUBJECT, VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY, VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY);
}

export function getVapidPublicKey(): string {
  return VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY;
}

export async function sendPushToUser(
  userId: string,
  payload: { title: string; message: string; url?: string }
): Promise<void> {
  if (!VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY || !VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY) {
    return;
  }

  const subs = await db
    .select()
    .from(pushSubscriptions)
    .where(eq(pushSubscriptions.userId, userId));

  if (subs.length === 0) return;

  const message = JSON.stringify(payload);
  const staleEndpoints: string[] = [];

  await Promise.allSettled(
    subs.map(async (sub) => {
      try {
        await webpush.sendNotification(
          {
            endpoint: sub.endpoint,
            keys: { p256dh: sub.p256dh, auth: sub.auth },
          },
          message
        );
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        const statusCode = (err as { statusCode?: number }).statusCode;
        if (statusCode === 404 || statusCode === 410) {
          staleEndpoints.push(sub.endpoint);
        } else {
          logError("pushService: sendNotification failed", {
            userId,
            endpoint: sub.endpoint,
            error: String(err),
          });
        }
      }
    })
  );

  if (staleEndpoints.length > 0) {
    logInfo("pushService: removing stale push subscriptions", {
      userId,
      count: staleEndpoints.length,
    });
    for (const endpoint of staleEndpoints) {
      await db
        .delete(pushSubscriptions)
        .where(eq(pushSubscriptions.endpoint, endpoint))
        .catch(() => undefined);
    }
  }
}

function formatDate(date: Date): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    weekday: "long",
    year: "numeric",
    month: "long",
    day: "numeric",
    hour: "numeric",
    minute: "2-digit",
  }).format(date);
}

function escapeHtml(str: string): string {
  return str
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
}

function formatCurrency(amount: number): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "USD",
  }).format(amount);
}

async function getOrgNotificationSettings(organizationId: string): Promise<{ notifyByEmail: boolean; notifyInApp: boolean }> {
  try {
    const [settings] = await db
      .select()
      .from(organizationNetworkSettings)
      .where(eq(organizationNetworkSettings.organizationId, organizationId));
    
    return {
      notifyByEmail: settings?.notifyByEmail ?? true,
      notifyInApp: settings?.notifyInApp ?? true,
    };
  } catch {
    logWarn("Non-critical operation failed");
    return { notifyByEmail: true, notifyInApp: true };
  }
}

async function getOrgAdminEmails(organizationId: string): Promise<NotificationRecipient[]> {
  try {
    const admins = await db
      .select({
        email: users.email,
        firstName: users.firstName,
      })
      .from(users)
      .where(and(
        eq(users.organizationId, organizationId),
        eq(users.role, "ADMIN")
      ));
    
    const [org] = await db
      .select({ name: organizations.name })
      .from(organizations)
      .where(eq(organizations.id, organizationId));
    
    return admins.map(a => ({
      email: a.email,
      firstName: a.firstName || undefined,
      organizationName: org?.name,
    }));
  } catch {
    logWarn("Non-critical operation failed");
    return [];
  }
}

----- FILE: server/emailPushNotifications.ts (full) -----
/**
 * Real-time email push notifications (Task #524)
 *
 * Manages provider-side subscriptions that let Gmail and Outlook deliver
 * mailbox change events to our webhook endpoints. These eliminate the
 * up-to-2-minute polling latency from Task #522 — incoming mail triggers an
 * immediate incremental sync. Background polling continues at a slower
 * cadence as a safety net for missed pushes and unsubscribed accounts.
 *
 * Configuration (env vars):
 *   GMAIL_PUBSUB_TOPIC           e.g. projects/your-project/topics/gmail-watch
 *   EMAIL_WEBHOOK_SECRET         shared secret added to Pub/Sub push URL and
 *                                Outlook subscription notificationUrl
 *   APP_BASE_URL                 public https origin used to build webhook URLs
 *   GMAIL_PUBSUB_AUDIENCE        OIDC audience configured on the Pub/Sub push
 *                                subscription. When set, the Gmail webhook
 *                                additionally verifies Google's signed JWT in
 *                                the Authorization header (see
 *                                ./gmailPubsubAuth.ts).
 *   GMAIL_PUBSUB_SERVICE_ACCOUNT optional service-account email the JWT must
 *                                be issued by (defence in depth).
 *
 * If Gmail Pub/Sub or the public webhook URL is not configured, the ensure
 * helpers no-op and the system gracefully falls back to polling alone.
 */

import crypto from "crypto";
import { db } from "./db";
import { emailAccounts } from "@shared/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { gmailApiFetch, outlookApiFetch } from "./emailRoutes";
import { logInfo, logWarn, logError } from "./utils/logger";
import { getCanonicalBaseUrl } from "./utils/routeHelpers";

type EmailAccountRow = typeof emailAccounts.$inferSelect;

// Renew the Gmail watch when fewer than 24 hours remain. Watches expire
// after at most 7 days; our cron runs every 15 minutes so this leaves
// plenty of headroom.
const GMAIL_RENEWAL_BUFFER_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

// Outlook subscriptions for mail messages can live up to 4230 minutes
// (~70 hours). We renew when fewer than 12 hours remain.
const OUTLOOK_MAX_EXPIRATION_MINUTES = 4230;
const OUTLOOK_RENEWAL_BUFFER_MS = 12 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

export function getEmailWebhookSecret(): string {
  return process.env.EMAIL_WEBHOOK_SECRET || "";
}

export function isGmailPushConfigured(): boolean {
  return !!process.env.GMAIL_PUBSUB_TOPIC && !!getEmailWebhookSecret();
}

export function isOutlookPushConfigured(): boolean {
  if (!getEmailWebhookSecret()) return false;
  const baseUrl = getCanonicalBaseUrl();
  return /^https:\/\//i.test(baseUrl);
}

/**
 * Build the notificationUrl Microsoft Graph will POST to. The `token` query
 * parameter authenticates the delivery: ideally a per-subscription rotating
 * token (Task #548) so that leaking one URL doesn't expose every mailbox.
 * Falls back to the global EMAIL_WEBHOOK_SECRET when no per-subscription
 * token has been generated yet (e.g. legacy rows pre-migration).
 */
function buildOutlookNotificationUrl(perSubscriptionToken?: string | null): string {
  const baseUrl = getCanonicalBaseUrl().replace(/\/+$/, "");
  const token = perSubscriptionToken && perSubscriptionToken.length > 0
    ? perSubscriptionToken
    : getEmailWebhookSecret();
  return `${baseUrl}/api/webhooks/email/outlook?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}`;
}

/**
 * Register or renew a Gmail watch for the account. The watch tells Gmail
 * to publish history change events to our Pub/Sub topic, which Pub/Sub
 * forwards to /api/webhooks/email/gmail.
 */
export async function ensureGmailWatch(account: EmailAccountRow): Promise<void> {
  if (!isGmailPushConfigured()) return;
  if (account.provider !== "GMAIL" || !account.isActive) return;
  if (account.syncStatus === "NEEDS_RECONNECT") return;

  if (
    account.gmailWatchExpiration &&
    account.gmailWatchExpiration.getTime() - Date.now() > GMAIL_RENEWAL_BUFFER_MS
  ) {
    return;
  }

  try {
    const response = await gmailApiFetch(
      "https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/watch",
      account,
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          topicName: process.env.GMAIL_PUBSUB_TOPIC,
          labelIds: ["INBOX", "SENT"],
          labelFilterBehavior: "INCLUDE",
        }),
      },
    );
    if (!response.ok) {
      const errText = await response.text();
      throw new Error(`Gmail watch failed: ${response.status} ${errText.slice(0, 300)}`);
    }
    const data = (await response.json()) as { historyId: string; expiration: string };
    await db
      .update(emailAccounts)
      .set({
        gmailWatchExpiration: new Date(Number(data.expiration)),
        gmailWatchHistoryId: data.historyId,
        // Seed the incremental cursor so the first push notification has a
        // valid baseline to diff from.
        gmailHistoryId: account.gmailHistoryId ?? data.historyId,
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      })
      .where(eq(emailAccounts.id, account.id));
    logInfo("Gmail watch registered", {
      account: account.emailAddress,
      expiration: new Date(Number(data.expiration)).toISOString(),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    logError(
      "Failed to register Gmail watch",
      { account: account.emailAddress },
      err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
    );
  }
}

export async function stopGmailWatch(account: EmailAccountRow): Promise<void> {
  if (account.provider !== "GMAIL") return;
  if (!account.gmailWatchExpiration && !account.gmailWatchHistoryId) return;
  try {
    const response = await gmailApiFetch(
      "https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/stop",
      account,
      { method: "POST" },
    );
    if (!response.ok && response.status !== 404) {
      logWarn("Gmail watch stop returned non-OK", {
        account: account.emailAddress,
        status: response.status,
      });
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logError(
      "Failed to stop Gmail watch",
      { account: account.emailAddress },
      err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
    );
  }
  await db
    .update(emailAccounts)
    .set({
      gmailWatchExpiration: null,
      gmailWatchHistoryId: null,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
    .where(eq(emailAccounts.id, account.id));
}

/**
 * Create or renew the Microsoft Graph subscription for the inbox folder.
 * If a renewal PATCH fails (e.g. the subscription was garbage collected by
 * Graph), we recreate the subscription from scratch.
 */
export async function ensureOutlookSubscription(account: EmailAccountRow): Promise<void> {
  if (!isOutlookPushConfigured()) return;
  if (account.provider !== "OUTLOOK" || !account.isActive) return;
  if (account.syncStatus === "NEEDS_RECONNECT") return;

  const hasFresh =
    account.outlookSubscriptionId &&
    account.outlookSubscriptionExpiration &&
    account.outlookSubscriptionExpiration.getTime() - Date.now() > OUTLOOK_RENEWAL_BUFFER_MS;
  if (hasFresh) return;

  const newExpiration = new Date(Date.now() + OUTLOOK_MAX_EXPIRATION_MINUTES * 60 * 1000);

  // Try to renew first if we have an existing subscription that is still
  // valid (just inside the renewal buffer).
  if (
    account.outlookSubscriptionId &&
    account.outlookSubscriptionExpiration &&
    account.outlookSubscriptionExpiration.getTime() > Date.now()
  ) {
    try {
      const resp = await outlookApiFetch(
        `https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/subscriptions/${account.outlookSubscriptionId}`,
        account,
        {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ expirationDateTime: newExpiration.toISOString() }),
        },
      );
      if (resp.ok) {
        await db
          .update(emailAccounts)
          .set({
            outlookSubscriptionExpiration: newExpiration,
            updatedAt: new Date(),
          })
          .where(eq(emailAccounts.id, account.id));
        logInfo("Outlook subscription renewed", {
          account: account.emailAddress,
          expiration: newExpiration.toISOString(),
        });
        return;
      }
      logWarn("Outlook subscription renew failed, will recreate", {
        account: account.emailAddress,
        status: resp.status,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      logWarn("Outlook subscription renew threw, will recreate", {
        account: account.emailAddress,
        error: String(err),
      });
    }
  }

  // Create a fresh subscription.
  const clientState = crypto.randomBytes(24).toString("hex");
  // Per-subscription rotating webhook token (Task #548). Persist it BEFORE
  // calling Graph so that the synchronous validation handshake — which
  // Graph sends to the new notificationUrl with this token in the query
  // string — can be authenticated against the database.
  const webhookToken = crypto.randomBytes(24).toString("hex");
  await db
    .update(emailAccounts)
    .set({
      outlookSubscriptionWebhookToken: webhookToken,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
    .where(eq(emailAccounts.id, account.id));
  try {
    const resp = await outlookApiFetch(
      "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/subscriptions",
      account,
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          changeType: "created,updated,deleted",
          notificationUrl: buildOutlookNotificationUrl(webhookToken),
          resource: "/me/mailFolders('inbox')/messages",
          expirationDateTime: newExpiration.toISOString(),
          clientState,
        }),
      },
    );
    if (!resp.ok) {
      const errText = await resp.text();
      throw new Error(
        `Outlook subscription create failed: ${resp.status} ${errText.slice(0, 300)}`,
      );
    }
    const data = (await resp.json()) as { id: string; expirationDateTime: string };
    await db
      .update(emailAccounts)
      .set({
        outlookSubscriptionId: data.id,
        outlookSubscriptionExpiration: new Date(data.expirationDateTime),
        outlookSubscriptionClientState: clientState,
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      })
      .where(eq(emailAccounts.id, account.id));
    logInfo("Outlook subscription created", {
      account: account.emailAddress,
      expiration: data.expirationDateTime,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    logError(
      "Failed to create Outlook subscription",
      { account: account.emailAddress },
      err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
    );
  }
}

export async function deleteOutlookSubscription(account: EmailAccountRow): Promise<void> {
  if (account.provider !== "OUTLOOK" || !account.outlookSubscriptionId) return;
  try {
    const resp = await outlookApiFetch(
      `https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/subscriptions/${account.outlookSubscriptionId}`,
      account,
      { method: "DELETE" },
    );
    if (!resp.ok && resp.status !== 404) {
      logWarn("Outlook subscription delete returned non-OK", {
        account: account.emailAddress,
        status: resp.status,
      });
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logError(
      "Failed to delete Outlook subscription",
      { account: account.emailAddress },
      err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
    );
  }
  await db
    .update(emailAccounts)
    .set({
      outlookSubscriptionId: null,
      outlookSubscriptionExpiration: null,
      outlookSubscriptionClientState: null,
      outlookSubscriptionWebhookToken: null,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
    .where(eq(emailAccounts.id, account.id));
}

/**
 * Sweep all active Gmail/Outlook accounts and ensure each has a current
 * push subscription. Called from the background job runner on a schedule.
 */
export async function ensureAllEmailPushSubscriptions(): Promise<void> {
  if (!isGmailPushConfigured() && !isOutlookPushConfigured()) return;

  const accounts = await db
    .select()
    .from(emailAccounts)
    .where(eq(emailAccounts.isActive, true));

  for (const account of accounts) {
    if (account.syncStatus === "NEEDS_RECONNECT") continue;
    try {
      if (account.provider === "GMAIL") {
        await ensureGmailWatch(account);
      } else if (account.provider === "OUTLOOK") {
        await ensureOutlookSubscription(account);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      logError(
        "ensureAllEmailPushSubscriptions failed for account",
        { account: account.emailAddress },
        err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
      );
    }
  }
}

/**
 * @openapi
 * /api/notifications:
 *   get:
 *     tags: [Notifications]
 *     summary: List user notifications
 *     description: Returns up to 50 most recent notifications for the authenticated user.
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: List of notifications
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: array
 *               items:
 *                 $ref: '#/components/schemas/Notification'
 * /api/notifications/unread-count:
 *   get:
 *     tags: [Notifications]
 *     summary: Get unread notification count
 *     responses:
 *       200:
 *         description: Count of unread notifications
 *         content:
 *           application/json:
 *             schema:
 *               type: object
 *               properties:
 *                 count: { type: integer }
 * /api/notifications/{id}/read:
 *   patch:
 *     tags: [Notifications]
 *     summary: Mark a notification as read
 *     parameters:
 *       - name: id
 *         in: path
 *         required: true
 *         schema: { type: string, format: uuid }
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: Notification marked as read }
 *       404: { description: Notification not found }
 * /api/notifications/read-all:
 *   post:
 *     tags: [Notifications]
 *     summary: Mark all notifications as read
 *     responses:
 *       200: { description: All notifications marked as read }
 */
import { logError } from "./utils/logger";
import { Router } from "express";
import { db } from "./db";
import { notifications, notificationTypePrefs } from "@shared/schema";
import { requireAuth, type AuthRequest } from "./auth";
import { eq, and, desc, sql, notInArray } from "drizzle-orm";

type NotificationType = NonNullable<typeof notifications.$inferSelect.type>;

const router = Router();

/**
 * Task #1260 — Returns the set of notification types whose in-app feed
 * delivery the user has explicitly muted (`inAppEnabled = false`). The
 * notification is still inserted (so email/push/sms/Teams/Slack can be
 * routed independently by `deliverNotification`), but it is hidden
 * from the in-app feed and the unread badge.
 *
 * NULL `inAppEnabled` is treated as "not configured", which preserves
 * the historical always-show-in-feed behavior.
 *
 * Task #1747 — Rows whose snooze (`mutedUntil`) has elapsed are
 * skipped, so an "auto-clear in 7 days" mute starts surfacing
 * notifications again on its own without the user having to revisit
 * the dashboard to flip the switch back. NULL `mutedUntil` keeps the
 * historical "indefinite" semantics.
 */
async function loadInAppMutedTypes(userId: string): Promise<NotificationType[]> {
  const rows = await db
    .select({
      notificationType: notificationTypePrefs.notificationType,
      inAppEnabled: notificationTypePrefs.inAppEnabled,
      mutedUntil: notificationTypePrefs.mutedUntil,
    })
    .from(notificationTypePrefs)
    .where(eq(notificationTypePrefs.userId, userId));
  const nowMs = Date.now();
  return rows
    .filter((r) => r.inAppEnabled === false)
    .filter((r) => !r.mutedUntil || new Date(r.mutedUntil).getTime() > nowMs)
    .map((r) => r.notificationType as NotificationType);
}

router.get("/api/notifications", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    const orgId = req.user!.organizationId;
    const baseConditions = orgId
      ? and(eq(notifications.userId, req.user!.id), eq(notifications.organizationId, orgId))
      : and(eq(notifications.userId, req.user!.id), sql`${notifications.organizationId} IS NULL`);

    const mutedTypes = await loadInAppMutedTypes(req.user!.id);
    // Task #1260 — strip in-app-muted types from the feed without
    // affecting any other delivery channel for those notifications.
    const conditions = mutedTypes.length > 0
      ? and(
          baseConditions,
          sql`(${notifications.type} IS NULL OR ${notInArray(notifications.type, mutedTypes)})`,
        )
      : baseConditions;

    const userNotifications = await db
      .select()
      .from(notifications)
      .where(conditions)
      .orderBy(desc(notifications.createdAt))
      .limit(50);

    res.json(userNotifications);
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

router.get("/api/notifications/unread-count", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    const orgId = req.user!.organizationId;
    const orgFilter = orgId ? sql` AND ${notifications.organizationId} = ${orgId}` : sql` AND ${notifications.organizationId} IS NULL`;

    // Task #1260 — exclude in-app-muted types from the unread badge so
    // the count matches what the user actually sees in their feed.
    const mutedTypes = await loadInAppMutedTypes(req.user!.id);
    const muteFilter = mutedTypes.length > 0
      ? sql` AND (${notifications.type} IS NULL OR ${notInArray(notifications.type, mutedTypes)})`
      : sql``;

    const [result] = await db
      .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(notifications)
      .where(sql`${notifications.userId} = ${req.user!.id} AND ${notifications.isRead} = false${orgFilter}${muteFilter}`);

    res.json({ count: result.count });
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
});

router.patch("/api/notifications/:id/read", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    const orgId = req.user!.organizationId;
    const orgFilter = orgId ? sql` AND ${notifications.organizationId} = ${orgId}` : sql` AND ${notifications.organizationId} IS NULL`;

    const [notification] = await db
      .update(notifications)
      .set({ isRead: true })
      .where(sql`${notifications.id} = ${req.params.id} AND ${notifications.userId} = ${req.user!.id}${orgFilter}`)
      .returning();

    if (!notification) {
      res.status(404).json({ error: "Notification not found" });
      return;
    }

    res.json(notification);
  } catch (error: any) {
    next(error);
  }
});

router.post("/api/notifications/read-all", requireAuth, async (req: AuthRequest, res, next) => {
  try {
    const orgId = req.user!.organizationId;
    const conditions = orgId
      ? and(eq(notifications.userId, req.user!.id), eq(notifications.organizationId, orgId))
      : and(eq(notifications.userId, req.user!.id), sql`${notifications.organizationId} IS NULL`);

    await db
      .update(notifications)
      .set({ isRead: true })
      .where(conditions);

    res.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error: any) {
    next(error);
  }
});

export default router;

----- FILE: client/src/components/notifications/JobMetricAlertItem.tsx (full) -----
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { apiRequest, queryClient } from "@/lib/queryClient";
import { useMutation, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { format, formatDistanceToNow } from "date-fns";
import { AlertTriangle, BellOff, Check, ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "wouter";
import type { Notification } from "@shared/schema";

/**
 * Task #2010 — Snooze the noisy `pool_health` (and any other
 * job-metric alert) page from inside the in-app notification feed
 * without making the operator hop to the Job Metrics dashboard first.
 *
 * The alert engine writes one notification per fired alert with
 * `relatedEntityType: "job_metric_alert"` and
 * `relatedEntityId: "<job>:<counter|_>:<kind>"` (see
 * `server/utils/jobMetricAlerts.ts`). The dedupe key the engine reads
 * via `fetchActiveSnoozeLookup` is the same `(job, counter, kind)`
 * tuple, so snoozing any one matching event suppresses both in-app and
 * email re-notifications for the chosen window.
 *
 * Task #2022 — The same snooze controls are now exposed inside the
 * global notification bell (top header) via the compact
 * `JobMetricAlertSnoozeMenu` so admins glancing at the bell can
 * dismiss recurring pages without first deep-linking into the Job
 * Metrics dashboard.
 *
 * Task #2023 — Once snoozed, the bell row stays in the feed only as a
 * persistent banner ("Snoozed until 6:00 PM by Jane") with an
 * "Unsnooze" button, so the operator gets a clear visual confirmation
 * instead of just a transient toast. Active snoozes for the
 * `(job, counter, kind)` keys are looked up via a single shared
 * `/active-snoozes` query so we don't N+1 the bell.
 */
export function parseJobMetricAlertKey(
  relatedEntityId: string | null | undefined,
): { job: string; counter: string | null; kind: "spike" | "stall" } | null {
  if (!relatedEntityId) return null;
  const parts = relatedEntityId.split(":");
  if (parts.length !== 3) return null;
  const [job, counterRaw, kindRaw] = parts;
  if (!job) return null;
  const kind = kindRaw === "stall" ? "stall" : kindRaw === "spike" ? "spike" : null;
  if (!kind) return null;
  // The engine uses "_" as the counter sentinel for stall alerts (which
  // have a null counter); preserve that distinction so the snooze
  // lookup matches the engine's dedupe key exactly.
  const counter = counterRaw === "_" || counterRaw === "" ? null : counterRaw;
  return { job, counter, kind };
}

export const SNOOZE_OPTIONS: Array<{ hours: number; label: string }> = [
  { hours: 1, label: "1 hour" },
  { hours: 4, label: "4 hours" },
  { hours: 24, label: "24 hours" },
];

interface ActiveSnooze {
  job: string;
  counter: string | null;
  kind: "spike" | "stall";
  eventId: string;
  snoozedUntil: number;
  snoozedById: string | null;
  snoozedByName: string | null;
}

function snoozeKey(
  job: string,
  counter: string | null,
  kind: "spike" | "stall",
): string {
  return `${job}::${counter ?? ""}::${kind}`;
}

function formatSnoozeUntil(ts: number): string {
  const d = new Date(ts);
  const sameDay = new Date().toDateString() === d.toDateString();
  return sameDay ? format(d, "h:mm a") : format(d, "MMM d, h:mm a");
}

/**
 * Shared hook returning the active snooze (if any) for the
 * notification's `(job, counter, kind)` key. Backed by a single
 * shared `/active-snoozes` query so N rendered rows still issue one
 * network request (react-query dedupes by queryKey).
 */
export function useJobMetricAlertActiveSnooze(
  notification: Notification,
): ActiveSnooze | null {
  const key = parseJobMetricAlertKey(notification.relatedEntityId);
  const { data: activeSnoozesData } = useQuery<{ snoozes: ActiveSnooze[] }>({
    queryKey: ["/api/admin/metrics/jobs/alerts/active-snoozes"],
    // Cheap to refresh and lets the banner expire naturally without
    // requiring a manual refetch when the snooze window ends.
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
    staleTime: 30_000,
  });

  if (!key || !activeSnoozesData?.snoozes?.length) return null;
  const target = snoozeKey(key.job, key.counter, key.kind);
  const now = Date.now();
  return (
    activeSnoozesData.snoozes.find(
      (s) =>
        snoozeKey(s.job, s.counter, s.kind) === target &&
        s.snoozedUntil > now,
    ) ?? null
  );
}

/**
 * Shared hook that POSTs to the snooze-by-key endpoint, surfaces a
 * toast, and refreshes the bell + dashboard caches. Used by both the
 * dashboard widget item and the compact bell variant so the two share
 * one source of truth for endpoint, payload, and cache invalidation.
 */
export function useJobMetricAlertSnooze(
  notification: Notification,
  options?: { onSnoozed?: () => void },
) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const key = parseJobMetricAlertKey(notification.relatedEntityId);

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ hours }: { hours: number }) => {
      if (!key) throw new Error("Alert key missing");
      const res = await apiRequest(
        "POST",
        "/api/admin/metrics/jobs/alerts/snooze-by-key",
        { job: key.job, counter: key.counter, kind: key.kind, hours },
      );
      return res.json();
    },
    onSuccess: (_data, vars) => {
      toast({
        title: `Snoozed for ${vars.hours}h`,
        description: key
          ? `${key.job}${key.counter ? ` · ${key.counter}` : ""} · ${key.kind}`
          : undefined,
      });
      // Mark the bell row as read so it disappears from the unread feed
      // — the underlying alert is recorded on the dashboard regardless.
      options?.onSnoozed?.();
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/notifications"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({
        queryKey: ["/api/notifications/unread-count"],
      });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({
        queryKey: ["/api/admin/metrics/jobs/alerts"],
      });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({
        queryKey: ["/api/admin/metrics/jobs/alerts/active-snoozes"],
      });
    },
    onError: (err: Error) => {
      toast({
        title: "Could not snooze alert",
        description: err.message,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    },
  });

  return { key, mutation };
}

/**
 * Shared hook that POSTs to the per-event unsnooze endpoint and
 * refreshes the same caches as the snooze hook. Used by both the
 * dashboard widget item and the compact bell variant so the
 * "Snoozed until …" banner + Unsnooze affordance behave identically
 * on every surface.
 */
export function useJobMetricAlertUnsnooze(
  notification: Notification,
  options?: { onUnsnoozed?: () => void },
) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const key = parseJobMetricAlertKey(notification.relatedEntityId);

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ eventId }: { eventId: string }) => {
      const res = await apiRequest(
        "POST",
        `/api/admin/metrics/jobs/alerts/events/${eventId}/unsnooze`,
      );
      return res.json();
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      toast({
        title: "Snooze cleared",
        description: key
          ? `${key.job}${key.counter ? ` · ${key.counter}` : ""} · ${key.kind}`
          : undefined,
      });
      options?.onUnsnoozed?.();
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/notifications"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({
        queryKey: ["/api/notifications/unread-count"],
      });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({
        queryKey: ["/api/admin/metrics/jobs/alerts"],
      });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({
        queryKey: ["/api/admin/metrics/jobs/alerts/active-snoozes"],
      });
    },
    onError: (err: Error) => {
      toast({
        title: "Could not unsnooze alert",
        description: err.message,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    },
  });

  return { key, mutation };
}

/**
 * Compact "Snoozed until …" banner — shared between the dashboard
 * widget item and the bell row variant so both surfaces show the
 * persistent confirmation introduced in Task #2023.
 */
function SnoozedBanner({
  notification,
  activeSnooze,
}: {
  notification: Notification;
  activeSnooze: ActiveSnooze;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-amber-300/50 bg-amber-100/50 px-2 py-1 text-xs text-amber-900 dark:border-amber-700/50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-200"
      data-testid={`banner-snoozed-${notification.id}`}
    >
      <BellOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
      <span className="break-words">
        Snoozed until {formatSnoozeUntil(activeSnooze.snoozedUntil)}
        {activeSnooze.snoozedByName ? ` by ${activeSnooze.snoozedByName}` : ""}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Compact snooze dropdown — the same Snooze 1h/4h/24h options as the
 * dashboard widget, sized for the global notification bell rows. The
 * trigger stops click propagation so opening the menu does not
 * navigate the parent row's deep-link or close the bell popover; menu
 * items use `onSelect` (Radix) which does close the inner menu after
 * the choice is made, leaving the bell popover open.
 *
 * When the alert is currently snoozed (Task #2023), the dropdown
 * collapses to a single "Unsnooze" button so the operator can clear
 * the active snooze with one click instead of picking a window.
 */
export function JobMetricAlertSnoozeMenu({
  notification,
  onSnoozed,
  size = "default",
}: {
  notification: Notification;
  onSnoozed?: () => void;
  size?: "default" | "sm";
}) {
  const { key, mutation } = useJobMetricAlertSnooze(notification, { onSnoozed });
  const activeSnooze = useJobMetricAlertActiveSnooze(notification);
  const { mutation: unsnoozeMutation } = useJobMetricAlertUnsnooze(notification);
  if (!key) return null;

  const triggerClass =
    size === "sm" ? "h-6 px-2 text-[11px]" : "h-7 text-xs";

  if (activeSnooze) {
    return (
      <Button
        variant="outline"
        size="sm"
        className={triggerClass}
        disabled={unsnoozeMutation.isPending}
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          unsnoozeMutation.mutate({ eventId: activeSnooze.eventId });
        }}
        data-testid={`button-unsnooze-notification-${notification.id}`}
      >
        <BellOff className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
        Unsnooze
      </Button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <DropdownMenu>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
        <Button
          variant="outline"
          size="sm"
          className={triggerClass}
          disabled={mutation.isPending}
          onClick={(e) => {
            // Don't let the click bubble to the parent bell row
            // (which would mark-read / navigate / close the popover).
            e.stopPropagation();
          }}
          data-testid={`button-snooze-notification-${notification.id}`}
        >
          <BellOff className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
          Snooze
        </Button>
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>
      <DropdownMenuContent
        align="end"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {SNOOZE_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
          <DropdownMenuItem
            key={opt.hours}
            onSelect={(e) => {
              // Keep the parent bell popover open after choosing a
              // snooze window — Radix would otherwise propagate the
              // close request up to the outer DropdownMenu.
              e.preventDefault();
              mutation.mutate({ hours: opt.hours });
            }}
            data-testid={`option-snooze-notification-${notification.id}-${opt.hours}h`}
          >
            {opt.label}
          </DropdownMenuItem>
        ))}
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  );
}

/**
 * Compact bell-row variant for `relatedEntityType === "job_metric_alert"`
 * notifications shown in the global header bell. Shares the same
 * snooze + dismiss wiring as the dashboard widget item but keeps the
 * tighter padding / hover styling used by sibling bell rows so admins
 * get the same one-click experience without the dashboard's outlined
 * card chrome.
 *
 * `onNavigate` is invoked when the user clicks the title to open
 * the Job Metrics dashboard — bells use it to close the popover.
 */
export function JobMetricAlertBellItem({
  notification,
  onDismiss,
  onNavigate,
  isDismissing,
}: {
  notification: Notification;
  onDismiss?: () => void;
  onNavigate?: () => void;
  isDismissing?: boolean;
}) {
  const isUnread = !notification.isRead;
  const activeSnooze = useJobMetricAlertActiveSnooze(notification);

  return (
    <div
      className={`flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-sm hover-elevate ${
        isUnread ? "bg-accent/50" : ""
      }`}
      data-testid={`notification-${notification.id}`}
    >
      <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-destructive" />
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-1.5">
        <Link
          href="/admin/job-metrics"
          onClick={() => {
            if (isUnread) onDismiss?.();
            onNavigate?.();
          }}
          className="block"
          data-testid={`link-alert-notification-${notification.id}`}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <p
              className="text-sm font-medium"
              data-testid={`text-alert-title-${notification.id}`}
            >
              {notification.title}
            </p>
            <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3 text-muted-foreground" />
          </div>
          <p
            className="text-xs text-muted-foreground break-words"
            data-testid={`text-alert-message-${notification.id}`}
          >
            {notification.message}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
            {formatDistanceToNow(new Date(notification.createdAt), {
              addSuffix: true,
            })}
          </p>
        </Link>
        {activeSnooze && (
          <SnoozedBanner
            notification={notification}
            activeSnooze={activeSnooze}
          />
        )}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <JobMetricAlertSnoozeMenu
            notification={notification}
            onSnoozed={onDismiss}
            size="sm"
          />
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1 shrink-0">
        {isUnread && (
          <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-primary mt-1.5" />
        )}
        {onDismiss && (
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="icon"
            className="h-6 w-6"
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              onDismiss();
            }}
            disabled={isDismissing}
            data-testid={`button-dismiss-notification-${notification.id}`}
          >
            <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function JobMetricAlertItem({
  notification,
  onDismiss,
  isDismissing,
}: {
  notification: Notification;
  onDismiss?: () => void;
  isDismissing?: boolean;
}) {
  const key = parseJobMetricAlertKey(notification.relatedEntityId);
  const activeSnooze = useJobMetricAlertActiveSnooze(notification);

  return (
    <div
      className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3 rounded-md bg-muted/50 border border-border"
      data-testid={`notification-item-${notification.id}`}
    >
      <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-destructive" />
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-2">
        <div>
          <p
            className="text-sm font-medium"
            data-testid={`text-alert-title-${notification.id}`}
          >
            {notification.title}
          </p>
          <p
            className="text-xs text-muted-foreground break-words"
            data-testid={`text-alert-message-${notification.id}`}
          >
            {notification.message}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
            {formatDistanceToNow(new Date(notification.createdAt), {
              addSuffix: true,
            })}
          </p>
        </div>
        {activeSnooze && (
          <SnoozedBanner
            notification={notification}
            activeSnooze={activeSnooze}
          />
        )}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-wrap">
          <Link href="/admin/job-metrics" onClick={() => onDismiss?.()}>
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              className="h-7 text-xs"
              data-testid={`link-alert-dashboard-${notification.id}`}
            >
              <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
              View dashboard
            </Button>
          </Link>
          {key && (
            <JobMetricAlertSnoozeMenu
              notification={notification}
              onSnoozed={onDismiss}
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      {onDismiss && (
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="icon"
          className="shrink-0"
          onClick={onDismiss}
          disabled={isDismissing}
          data-testid={`button-dismiss-notification-${notification.id}`}
        >
          <Check className="w-4 h-4" />
        </Button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

----- FILE: server/cpmEngine.ts (full) -----
import { logError, logWarn } from "./utils/logger";
import { db } from "./db";
import {
  projectScheduleTasks,
  projectSchedules,
  scheduleForecastSnapshots,
  scheduleTaskDependencies,
} from "@shared/schema";
import { eq, and } from "drizzle-orm";

export type DependencyType = "FS" | "SS" | "FF" | "SF";

export interface TypedDependency {
  predecessorId: string;
  type: DependencyType;
  lagDays: number;
}

interface TaskNode {
  id: string;
  durationDays: number;
  plannedStartDate: string | null;
  plannedEndDate: string | null;
  actualStartDate: string | null;
  actualEndDate: string | null;
  dependencies: TypedDependency[];
  status: string;
  percentComplete: number;
  earlyStart: number;
  earlyFinish: number;
  lateStart: number;
  lateFinish: number;
  totalFloat: number;
  freeFloat: number;
}

function addWorkingDays(startDate: Date, days: number, workingDays: number[] = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]): Date {
  if (days <= 0) return new Date(startDate);
  const result = new Date(startDate);
  let added = 0;
  while (added < days) {
    result.setDate(result.getDate() + 1);
    if (workingDays.includes(result.getDay())) {
      added++;
    }
  }
  return result;
}

function workingDaysBetween(start: Date, end: Date, workingDays: number[] = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]): number {
  if (end <= start) return 0;
  let count = 0;
  const current = new Date(start);
  while (current < end) {
    current.setDate(current.getDate() + 1);
    if (workingDays.includes(current.getDay())) {
      count++;
    }
  }
  return count;
}

function dateToDay(date: string, projectStart: string, workingDaysList?: number[]): number {
  if (workingDaysList && workingDaysList.length > 0 && workingDaysList.length < 7) {
    return workingDaysBetween(new Date(projectStart), new Date(date), workingDaysList);
  }
  const d = new Date(date);
  const s = new Date(projectStart);
  return Math.round((d.getTime() - s.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
}

function dayToDate(day: number, projectStart: string, workingDaysList?: number[]): string {
  if (workingDaysList && workingDaysList.length > 0 && workingDaysList.length < 7) {
    const result = addWorkingDays(new Date(projectStart), day, workingDaysList);
    return result.toISOString().split("T")[0];
  }
  const s = new Date(projectStart);
  s.setDate(s.getDate() + day);
  return s.toISOString().split("T")[0];
}

export interface CPMResult {
  tasks: Array<{
    id: string;
    earlyStart: string;
    earlyFinish: string;
    lateStart: string;
    lateFinish: string;
    totalFloat: number;
    freeFloat: number;
    isCriticalPath: boolean;
    plannedStartDate: string;
    plannedEndDate: string;
  }>;
  projectedCompletionDate: string;
  criticalPathDaysRemaining: number;
}

export function runCPM(
  tasks: Array<{
    id: string;
    durationDays: number | null;
    plannedStartDate: string | null;
    plannedEndDate: string | null;
    actualStartDate: string | null;
    actualEndDate: string | null;
    dependencies: string[] | null;
    status: string;
    percentComplete: number;
  }>,
  projectStartDate: string,
  workingDaysList?: number[],
  typedDependenciesByTask?: Map<string, TypedDependency[]>,
): CPMResult {
  if (tasks.length === 0) {
    return { tasks: [], projectedCompletionDate: projectStartDate, criticalPathDaysRemaining: 0 };
  }

  const taskIdSet = new Set(tasks.map(t => t.id));
  const taskMap = new Map<string, TaskNode>();
  const successorMap = new Map<string, string[]>();

  for (const t of tasks) {
    const typedDeps = typedDependenciesByTask?.get(t.id);
    let deps: TypedDependency[];
    if (typedDeps && typedDeps.length > 0) {
      deps = typedDeps.filter(d => taskIdSet.has(d.predecessorId));
    } else {
      deps = (t.dependencies || [])
        .filter(d => taskIdSet.has(d))
        .map(predecessorId => ({ predecessorId, type: "FS" as DependencyType, lagDays: 0 }));
    }
    taskMap.set(t.id, {
      id: t.id,
      durationDays: t.durationDays || 1,
      plannedStartDate: t.plannedStartDate,
      plannedEndDate: t.plannedEndDate,
      actualStartDate: t.actualStartDate,
      actualEndDate: t.actualEndDate,
      dependencies: deps,
      status: t.status,
      percentComplete: t.percentComplete,
      earlyStart: 0,
      earlyFinish: 0,
      lateStart: 0,
      lateFinish: 0,
      totalFloat: 0,
      freeFloat: 0,
    });
  }

  for (const [id, node] of taskMap) {
    for (const dep of node.dependencies) {
      if (!successorMap.has(dep.predecessorId)) successorMap.set(dep.predecessorId, []);
      successorMap.get(dep.predecessorId)!.push(id);
    }
  }

  const sorted = topologicalSort(taskMap);

  for (const id of sorted) {
    const node = taskMap.get(id)!;
    let earliest = 0;
    let constrained = false;
    for (const dep of node.dependencies) {
      const pred = taskMap.get(dep.predecessorId);
      if (!pred) continue;
      let candidate: number;
      switch (dep.type) {
        case "SS":
          candidate = pred.earlyStart + dep.lagDays;
          break;
        case "FF":
          candidate = pred.earlyFinish + dep.lagDays - node.durationDays;
          break;
        case "SF":
          candidate = pred.earlyStart + dep.lagDays - node.durationDays;
          break;
        case "FS":
        default:
          candidate = pred.earlyFinish + dep.lagDays;
          break;
      }
      if (!constrained || candidate > earliest) {
        earliest = candidate;
        constrained = true;
      }
    }
    node.earlyStart = constrained ? Math.max(0, earliest) : 0;

    if (node.status === "COMPLETE" && node.actualStartDate && node.actualEndDate) {
      const actualDuration = dateToDay(node.actualEndDate, projectStartDate, workingDaysList) - dateToDay(node.actualStartDate, projectStartDate, workingDaysList) + 1;
      node.earlyStart = dateToDay(node.actualStartDate, projectStartDate, workingDaysList);
      node.earlyFinish = node.earlyStart + Math.max(actualDuration, 1);
    } else if (node.status === "IN_PROGRESS" && node.actualStartDate) {
      node.earlyStart = dateToDay(node.actualStartDate, projectStartDate, workingDaysList);
      const remainingDuration = Math.ceil(node.durationDays * (1 - node.percentComplete / 100));
      node.earlyFinish = node.earlyStart + Math.max(remainingDuration, 1);
    } else {
      node.earlyFinish = node.earlyStart + node.durationDays;
    }
  }

  let projectDuration = 0;
  for (const [, node] of taskMap) {
    projectDuration = Math.max(projectDuration, node.earlyFinish);
  }

  for (let i = sorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const id = sorted[i];
    const node = taskMap.get(id)!;
    const succIds = successorMap.get(id) || [];

    if (succIds.length === 0) {
      node.lateFinish = projectDuration;
      node.lateStart = node.lateFinish - (node.earlyFinish - node.earlyStart);
      continue;
    }

    let latestStart = Infinity;
    let latestFinish = Infinity;
    for (const succId of succIds) {
      const succ = taskMap.get(succId);
      if (!succ) continue;
      const dep = succ.dependencies.find(d => d.predecessorId === id);
      if (!dep) continue;
      switch (dep.type) {
        case "SS": {
          const ls = succ.lateStart - dep.lagDays;
          latestStart = Math.min(latestStart, ls);
          break;
        }
        case "FF": {
          const lf = succ.lateFinish - dep.lagDays;
          latestFinish = Math.min(latestFinish, lf);
          break;
        }
        case "SF": {
          const lf = succ.lateFinish - dep.lagDays;
          // SF: predecessor start to successor finish
          const lsFromSf = lf - (node.earlyFinish - node.earlyStart);
          latestStart = Math.min(latestStart, lsFromSf + (node.earlyFinish - node.earlyStart));
          break;
        }
        case "FS":
        default: {
          const lf = succ.lateStart - dep.lagDays;
          latestFinish = Math.min(latestFinish, lf);
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    const duration = node.earlyFinish - node.earlyStart;
    if (latestStart === Infinity && latestFinish === Infinity) {
      node.lateFinish = projectDuration;
    } else if (latestStart !== Infinity && latestFinish !== Infinity) {
      node.lateFinish = Math.min(latestFinish, latestStart + duration);
    } else if (latestFinish !== Infinity) {
      node.lateFinish = latestFinish;
    } else {
      node.lateFinish = latestStart + duration;
    }
    node.lateStart = node.lateFinish - duration;
  }

  for (const [id, node] of taskMap) {
    node.totalFloat = node.lateStart - node.earlyStart;

    const succIds = successorMap.get(id) || [];
    if (succIds.length === 0) {
      node.freeFloat = projectDuration - node.earlyFinish;
    } else {
      // Free float = minimum slack across each successor edge, respecting type+lag.
      // For each successor, compute the earliest moment by which this predecessor
      // must finish (or start, for SS/SF) without delaying the successor's ES.
      let minSlack = Infinity;
      for (const succId of succIds) {
        const succ = taskMap.get(succId);
        if (!succ) continue;
        const dep = succ.dependencies.find(d => d.predecessorId === id);
        if (!dep) continue;
        let slack: number;
        switch (dep.type) {
          case "SS":
            slack = (succ.earlyStart - dep.lagDays) - node.earlyStart;
            break;
          case "FF":
            slack = (succ.earlyFinish - dep.lagDays) - node.earlyFinish;
            break;
          case "SF":
            slack = (succ.earlyFinish - dep.lagDays) - node.earlyStart;
            break;
          case "FS":
          default:
            slack = (succ.earlyStart - dep.lagDays) - node.earlyFinish;
            break;
        }
        if (slack < minSlack) minSlack = slack;
      }
      node.freeFloat = minSlack === Infinity ? 0 : Math.max(0, minSlack);
    }
  }

  const resultTasks = sorted.map(id => {
    const node = taskMap.get(id)!;
    const isCritical = Math.abs(node.totalFloat) < 0.5;
    const inclusiveEF = node.earlyFinish > 0 ? node.earlyFinish - 1 : 0;
    const inclusiveLF = node.lateFinish > 0 ? node.lateFinish - 1 : 0;
    return {
      id: node.id,
      earlyStart: dayToDate(node.earlyStart, projectStartDate, workingDaysList),
      earlyFinish: dayToDate(inclusiveEF, projectStartDate, workingDaysList),
      lateStart: dayToDate(node.lateStart, projectStartDate, workingDaysList),
      lateFinish: dayToDate(inclusiveLF, projectStartDate, workingDaysList),
      totalFloat: node.totalFloat,
      freeFloat: node.freeFloat,
      isCriticalPath: isCritical,
      plannedStartDate: dayToDate(node.earlyStart, projectStartDate, workingDaysList),
      plannedEndDate: dayToDate(inclusiveEF, projectStartDate, workingDaysList),
    };
  });

  let criticalPathDaysRemaining = 0;
  for (const t of resultTasks) {
    if (t.isCriticalPath) {
      const originalTask = tasks.find(ot => ot.id === t.id);
      if (originalTask && originalTask.status !== "COMPLETE") {
        const dur = originalTask.durationDays || 1;
        const pct = originalTask.percentComplete || 0;
        criticalPathDaysRemaining += Math.ceil(dur * (1 - pct / 100));
      }
    }
  }

  const projectedCompletionDate = dayToDate(projectDuration > 0 ? projectDuration - 1 : 0, projectStartDate, workingDaysList);

  return {
    tasks: resultTasks,
    projectedCompletionDate,
    criticalPathDaysRemaining,
  };
}

function topologicalSort(taskMap: Map<string, TaskNode>): string[] {
  const visited = new Set<string>();
  const result: string[] = [];
  const visiting = new Set<string>();

  function visit(id: string) {
    if (visited.has(id)) return;
    if (visiting.has(id)) {
      logWarn(`CPM: Cycle detected involving task ${id}, breaking cycle`);
      return;
    }
    visiting.add(id);

    const node = taskMap.get(id);
    if (node) {
      for (const dep of node.dependencies) {
        if (taskMap.has(dep.predecessorId)) {
          visit(dep.predecessorId);
        }
      }
    }
    visiting.delete(id);
    visited.add(id);
    result.push(id);
  }

  for (const id of taskMap.keys()) {
    visit(id);
  }

  return result;
}

export function detectCycle(
  taskId: string,
  dependencies: string[],
  allTasks: Array<{ id: string; dependencies: string[] | null }>,
  extraEdges?: Array<{ successorId: string; predecessorId: string }>,
): boolean {
  const graph = new Map<string, string[]>();
  for (const t of allTasks) {
    graph.set(t.id, t.id === taskId ? dependencies : (t.dependencies || []));
  }
  if (extraEdges) {
    for (const edge of extraEdges) {
      if (!graph.has(edge.successorId)) graph.set(edge.successorId, []);
      const arr = graph.get(edge.successorId)!;
      if (!arr.includes(edge.predecessorId)) arr.push(edge.predecessorId);
    }
  }

  const visited = new Set<string>();
  const visiting = new Set<string>();

  function hasCycle(id: string): boolean {
    if (visiting.has(id)) return true;
    if (visited.has(id)) return false;
    visiting.add(id);
    for (const dep of (graph.get(id) || [])) {
      if (hasCycle(dep)) return true;
    }
    visiting.delete(id);
    visited.add(id);
    return false;
  }

  return hasCycle(taskId);
}

export async function loadTypedDependencyMap(scheduleId: string): Promise<Map<string, TypedDependency[]>> {
  const rows = await db.select().from(scheduleTaskDependencies).where(eq(scheduleTaskDependencies.scheduleId, scheduleId));
  const map = new Map<string, TypedDependency[]>();
  for (const r of rows) {
    if (!map.has(r.successorTaskId)) map.set(r.successorTaskId, []);
    map.get(r.successorTaskId)!.push({
      predecessorId: r.predecessorTaskId,
      type: r.dependencyType as DependencyType,
      lagDays: r.lagDays || 0,
    });
  }
  return map;
}

export async function recalculateSchedule(scheduleId: string): Promise<CPMResult | null> {
  const [schedule] = await db.select().from(projectSchedules).where(eq(projectSchedules.id, scheduleId));
  if (!schedule) return null;

  const tasks = await db.select().from(projectScheduleTasks).where(eq(projectScheduleTasks.scheduleId, scheduleId));
  if (tasks.length === 0) return null;

  const projectStart = schedule.baselineStartDate || tasks[0].plannedStartDate || new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
  const workingDaysList = schedule.workingDays ? (schedule.workingDays as number[]) : undefined;
  const typedDepsMap = await loadTypedDependencyMap(scheduleId);
  const previousTaskDates = new Map(tasks.map(t => [t.id, { start: t.plannedStartDate, end: t.plannedEndDate }]));
  const result = runCPM(tasks, projectStart, workingDaysList, typedDepsMap);

  for (const cpmTask of result.tasks) {
    await db.update(projectScheduleTasks)
      .set({
        earlyStart: cpmTask.earlyStart,
        earlyFinish: cpmTask.earlyFinish,
        lateStart: cpmTask.lateStart,
        lateFinish: cpmTask.lateFinish,
        totalFloat: cpmTask.totalFloat,
        freeFloat: cpmTask.freeFloat,
        isCriticalPath: cpmTask.isCriticalPath,
        plannedStartDate: cpmTask.plannedStartDate,
        plannedEndDate: cpmTask.plannedEndDate,
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      })
      .where(eq(projectScheduleTasks.id, cpmTask.id));
  }

  const overallProgress = tasks.length > 0
    ? Math.round(tasks.reduce((sum, t) => sum + (t.percentComplete || 0), 0) / tasks.length)
    : 0;

  let spiValue: string | null = null;
  if (schedule.baselineLocked && schedule.baselineEndDate && schedule.baselineStartDate) {
    const baselineDuration = dateToDay(schedule.baselineEndDate, schedule.baselineStartDate, workingDaysList);
    if (baselineDuration > 0) {
      const earnedDuration = (overallProgress / 100) * baselineDuration;
      const today = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
      const elapsedDuration = Math.max(1, dateToDay(today, schedule.baselineStartDate, workingDaysList));
      const plannedProgress = Math.min(elapsedDuration / baselineDuration, 1);
      const plannedDuration = plannedProgress * baselineDuration;
      spiValue = plannedDuration > 0 ? (earnedDuration / plannedDuration).toFixed(3) : "1.000";
    }
  }

  await db.update(projectSchedules)
    .set({
      projectedCompletionDate: result.projectedCompletionDate,
      spiValue,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
    .where(eq(projectSchedules.id, scheduleId));

  try {
    await db.insert(scheduleForecastSnapshots).values({
      scheduleId,
      snapshotDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      projectedCompletionDate: result.projectedCompletionDate,
      spiValue,
      baselineEndDate: schedule.baselineEndDate,
      currentEndDate: result.projectedCompletionDate,
      criticalPathDaysRemaining: result.criticalPathDaysRemaining,
      overallProgress,
    });
  } catch (e) {
    logError("Failed to save forecast snapshot:", {}, e instanceof Error ? e : new Error(String(e)));
  }

  try {
    const reschedules: Array<{ taskId: string; previousEnd: string | null; newEnd: string | null; deltaDays: number }> = [];
    for (const cpmTask of result.tasks) {
      const prev = previousTaskDates.get(cpmTask.id);
      if (prev?.end && cpmTask.plannedEndDate && prev.end !== cpmTask.plannedEndDate) {
        const deltaDays = Math.round(
          (new Date(cpmTask.plannedEndDate).getTime() - new Date(prev.end).getTime()) / 86400000,
        );
        if (deltaDays !== 0) {
          reschedules.push({ taskId: cpmTask.id, previousEnd: prev.end, newEnd: cpmTask.plannedEndDate, deltaDays });
        }
      }
    }
    const baselineVariance = schedule.baselineEndDate && result.projectedCompletionDate
      ? Math.round((new Date(result.projectedCompletionDate).getTime() - new Date(schedule.baselineEndDate).getTime()) / 86400000)
      : null;
    if (reschedules.length > 0 || (baselineVariance !== null && baselineVariance >= 1)) {
      const { notifyScheduleChange } = await import("./services/scheduleNotificationService");
      await notifyScheduleChange({ scheduleId, reschedules, baselineVarianceDays: baselineVariance });
    }
  } catch (e) {
    logError("Failed to emit schedule change notifications", {}, e instanceof Error ? e : new Error(String(e)));
  }

  return result;
}

export function computeScheduleImpact(
  delayStartDate: string,
  delayEndDate: string | null,
  estimatedDuration: number | null,
  criticalPathTasks: Array<{ plannedStartDate: string | null; plannedEndDate: string | null; status: string }>
): { impactDays: number; affectsCriticalPath: boolean } {
  const delayStart = new Date(delayStartDate);
  const delayEnd = delayEndDate ? new Date(delayEndDate) : (estimatedDuration ? new Date(delayStart.getTime() + estimatedDuration * 86400000) : new Date());
  
  let impactDays = 0;
  let affectsCriticalPath = false;

  for (const task of criticalPathTasks) {
    if (task.status === "COMPLETE" || !task.plannedStartDate || !task.plannedEndDate) continue;
    const taskStart = new Date(task.plannedStartDate);
    const taskEnd = new Date(task.plannedEndDate);
    
    const overlapStart = new Date(Math.max(delayStart.getTime(), taskStart.getTime()));
    const overlapEnd = new Date(Math.min(delayEnd.getTime(), taskEnd.getTime()));
    
    if (overlapStart <= overlapEnd) {
      affectsCriticalPath = true;
      const overlapDays = Math.ceil((overlapEnd.getTime() - overlapStart.getTime()) / 86400000) + 1;
      impactDays = Math.max(impactDays, overlapDays);
    }
  }

  if (!affectsCriticalPath && estimatedDuration) {
    impactDays = estimatedDuration;
  }

  return { impactDays, affectsCriticalPath };
}

export async function computeProjectDelayImpact(
  projectId: string,
  estimatedDuration: number,
  delayStartDate?: string
): Promise<{ impactDays: number; criticalPathAffected: boolean; projectedCompletionDate: string | null } | null> {
  const { projectSchedules: ps, projectScheduleTasks: pst } = await import("@shared/schema");
  const [schedule] = await db.select().from(ps).where(and(eq(ps.projectId, projectId), eq(ps.isActive, true)));
  if (!schedule) return null;

  const tasks = await db.select().from(pst).where(eq(pst.scheduleId, schedule.id));
  const criticalPathTasks = tasks.filter(t => t.isCriticalPath);

  const startDate = delayStartDate || new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
  const result = computeScheduleImpact(startDate, null, estimatedDuration, criticalPathTasks);

  return {
    impactDays: result.impactDays,
    criticalPathAffected: result.affectsCriticalPath,
    projectedCompletionDate: schedule.projectedCompletionDate || null,
  };
}
